        var panel = Nodes<BasePanel>().First();
        Button Buy(string id)
        {
            ShowTab(panel, Economy.ById(id).Tab);                  // an upgrade lives on its own tab
            return Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).First(r => r.Name == "Up_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
        }
        Check(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(panel).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Up_")) == 5, "MINERS tab: five upgrades (count, hold, engines, beam, hull)");
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("miner_hold").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Level("miner_hold") == 2 && Y.Credits == 775 && Math.Abs(miner.Hold - 120) < 1e-9, "+10%: 100 then 125 (x1.25); hold 120");
        Buy("mine_rate").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(miner.Rate - 2.2) < 1e-9, "mining beam +10%: 2.2/s");
        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("salvager_speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Math.Abs(salv.Speed - 121) < 1e-9, "salvager engines +10%: 121 u/s");
        Y.Credits = 10; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Buy("salvage_rate").Disabled && !Y.TryBuy("salvage_rate"), "short of credits: BUY greyed out and refused");
        Y.Credits = 30000; panel.ShowTab("MINERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        double c0 = Y.Credits;
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { Buy("miner_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }   // one press too many
        Check(Y.Level("miner_count") == 4 && Math.Abs(c0 - Y.Credits - 6000) < 1e-6, "+1 miner x4: 400, 800, 1600, 3200 (the fifth press refused)");
        Check(Buy("miner_count").Disabled && Buy("miner_count").Text == "MAX" && !Y.TryBuy("miner_count"), "capped at 5 miners: MAX");
        // THE BASE IS PART OF THE CHARACTER NOW. Read back off DISK, not out of memory: the
        // claim is that a purchase survives the session, and memory would say yes either way.
        // A purchase saves at once -- the autosave is for income ticking over, not for this.
        var onDisk = new ConfigFile();
        Check(onDisk.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") == Error.Ok, "the character file is on disk after a purchase");
        Check((int)onDisk.GetValue("base_levels", "miner_count", 0) == 4
              && Math.Abs((double)onDisk.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits) < 1e-6
              && Math.Abs((double)onDisk.GetValue("base_invested", "MINERS", -1.0) - Y.Invested("MINERS")) < 1e-6,
              $"a base purchase reaches the character file at once (4 miners, {Y.Credits:0} cr, {Y.Invested("MINERS"):0} invested)");

        panel.ShowTab("SALVAGERS"); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { Buy("salvager_count").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.02); }
        await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner) == 5 && Y.Gatherers.Count(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager) == 5, "five miners and five salvagers");
        panel.ShowTab("HAULER"); await Wait(0.05);
        Buy("hauler_pods").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Y.Pods == 2 && Y.Capacity == 600, "HAULER tab: a second pod (500 cr): capacity 600");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);

        // ── the arms and the queue ──
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) { g.State = Gatherer.St.Working; g.Cargo = g.Hold; }   // everyone home at once
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var occupants = Y.Arms.Select(a => a.Occupant).Where(o => o != null).ToList();
        Check(occupants.Count == 5 && occupants.Distinct().Count() == 5 && Y.QueueLength == 5, $"five arms taken, one ship each; five wait in line ({Y.QueueLength})");
        var head = Y.Gatherers.First(g => Y.QueueIndex(g) == 0);
        bool firstIsHead = false, northOk = true, oneEach = true; int unloadedSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9000 && (Y.QueueLength > 0 || Y.Gatherers.Any(g => g.State is Gatherer.St.Docking or Gatherer.St.Unloading or Gatherer.St.Queued)); i++)
        {
            if (!firstIsHead && Y.ArmOf(head) >= 0) firstIsHead = Y.QueueLength == 4;
            foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.State == Gatherer.St.Unloading))
            {
                var arm = Y.Arms[Y.ArmOf(g)]; var pad = Hub.BasePos + arm.Pad;
                northOk &= (g.Position - pad).Dot(arm.Open) > arm.Reach;       // on the bay's open side
                unloadedSeen++;
            }
            oneEach &= Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null) <= 5 && Y.Arms.Where(a => a.Occupant != null).Select(a => a.Occupant).Distinct().Count() == Y.Arms.Count(a => a.Occupant != null);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(firstIsHead, "the first in line takes the first arm that frees");
        Check(northOk && unloadedSeen > 0, "ships unload through each bay's open north face");
        Check(oneEach && Y.QueueLength == 0 && Y.Gatherers.All(g => g.Cargo == 0 || g.State is Gatherer.St.Working or Gatherer.St.Outbound), "the line clears; never two ships on one arm");

        // ── the hauler: dispatch rules, landing, the run ──
        // Ten ships are working by now. Send them all back out empty, so no delivery
        // lands in the stock while the hauler's rules are checked (a hold takes 50+ s).
        foreach (var g in Y.Gatherers) g.ResetToWork();
        hauler.ResetToPad();                // it may be mid-run from the earlier sections
        hauler.Cargo = 0; Y.Ore = 150; Y.Salvage = 0;
        await Wait(8.0);
        GD.Print($"  hauler: {hauler.State} cargo {hauler.Cargo:0} / pod {Y.PodSize:0} x {Y.Pods}; stock {Y.Ore:0}+{Y.Salvage:0}");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && !hauler.CanDispatch, "150 of 300 in the first pod: cannot dispatch");
        Unmeet("hauler"); await Wait(0.1);
        bool haulerEarly = Met("hauler");
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await Wait(0.05);
        bool plainRefused = hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: true); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(plainRefused && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading, "dispatch refused until a pod is full, alone or escorted");
        Hauler.PretendRoll = 0f;                    // the dice with this run: the lost run has checks of its own
        Y.Ore += 200; await Wait(10.0);
        var hud = Nodes<HaulerHud>().First(); var go = Nodes<Button>(hud).First(b => b.Name == "Dispatch");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && hauler.CanDispatch && hauler.FullPods == 1 && !go.Disabled,
              $"one pod full (350 of 600): the floating DISPATCH button lights ({go.Text})");
        Check(!haulerEarly && Met("hauler"), "THE HAULER: met when its DISPATCH goes live, not before");
        go.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && !hud.Visible, "DISPATCH: it lifts off (and the button goes)");
        float s0 = hauler.VisualScale; await Wait(Economy.HaulerLift + 0.1);
        Check(s0 < 0.7f && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - 1f) < 1e-3 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing, "lifting grows it from landed to flight size");
        bool flat = true, facing = true; int frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && frames++ < 6000)
        {
            flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; facing &= Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.001f;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(flat && facing, $"it moves perfectly flat along the lane, facing the portal ({frames} frames checked)");
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging && hauler.AuraOn && Math.Abs(hauler.Position.X - Hub.PortalPos.X) < 0.6f, "at the portal: blue aura, shaking");
        double credits0 = Y.Credits;
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerCharge + 0.5);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away && hauler.WarpedOut, "warped out through the portal");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerAway + 0.6);
        Check(Math.Abs(Y.Credits - credits0 - 350) < 1e-6 && !hauler.WarpedOut && hauler.LastSale == 350, "back after 30 s: its 350 sold for 350 credits");
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State != Hauler.St.Returning; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        flat = true; frames = 0;
        while (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Returning && frames++ < 6000) { flat &= hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(flat && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && hauler.Position == Hub.HaulerPad, "back over its pad along the same flat lane");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2);
        float midScale = hauler.VisualScale, midRot = hauler.Rotation;
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Landing && midScale > Economy.HaulerLandedScale + 0.05f && midScale < 0.98f
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.West)) > 0.3f && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(midRot, Hauler.East)) > 0.3f,
              $"it turns WHILE it descends (half way: scale {midScale:0.00}, heading between west and east)");
        // half way round it points north: its nose, at that size, must clear the station (lowest point y ~ 110)
        Check(Hub.HaulerPad.Y - Hauler.Length * midScale / 2 > 110, "turning on the way down, it clears the station");
        await Wait(Economy.HaulerLand / 2 + 0.2);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(hauler.VisualScale - Economy.HaulerLandedScale) < 1e-4
              && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, Hauler.East)) < 0.01f, "lands facing the portal, and loads");
        // AUTO-SELL: completely full, it waits for DISPATCH -- until the level-3 boss is beaten and
        // the 4462 cr upgrade bought; then it goes by itself
        Y.Ore = 700; Y.Salvage = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.Cargo < Y.Capacity - 1e-6; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        await Wait(2.0);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(hauler.Cargo - 600) < 1e-6, $"completely full (600), it waits while AUTO-SELL is locked ({hauler.State}, {hauler.Cargo:0})");
        Check(Math.Abs(Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("hauler_autosell"), 0) - 4462) < 1e-9,
              $"AUTO-SELL costs 4462 cr: 3x the average level-5 salvager upgrade ({Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("hauler_autosell"), 0):0})");
        var ev = Economy.ById("hauler_evasion");
        Check(ev != null && Economy.Value(ev, 0) == 60 && Economy.Value(ev, 1) == 67 && Economy.Value(ev, 5) == 95 && Economy.Maxed(ev, 5) && !Economy.Maxed(ev, 4)
              && new[] { 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 }.Select(l => Economy.Cost(ev, l)).SequenceEqual(new double[] { 400, 800, 1600, 3200, 6400 }),
              "EVASION: a lone run is 60% safe, +7% a level to 95% at its 5-level cap (400, 800, 1600, 3200, 6400 cr)");
        Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2 };
        Character.BossCleared["ironhold"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 9 };     // a boss this build does not know counts for nothing
        Check(Missions.HighestBeaten == 2, $"the highest boss beaten is of the bosses this build knows ({Missions.HighestBeaten})");
        Y.Credits = 100000;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<BasePanel>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Tab_HAULER").ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.1);
        var asRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(Nodes<BasePanel>().First()).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Up_hauler_autosell");
        var asBuy = asRow == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(asRow).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
        bool lockedUi = asBuy != null && asBuy.Disabled && asBuy.Text.Contains("LOCKED");
        bool asRefused = !Y.TryBuy("hauler_autosell");
        Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].Add(3);
        await Wait(0.1);
        bool open = asBuy != null && !asBuy.Disabled;
        bool bought = Y.TryBuy("hauler_autosell");
        Check(lockedUi && asRefused && open && bought && Y.Level("hauler_autosell") == 1 && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - (100000 - 4462)) < 1e-6,
              $"AUTO-SELL reads LOCKED and is refused until the level-3 boss is beaten; then it is bought for 4462 cr (ui {lockedUi}, refused {asRefused}, open {open}, {Y.Credits:0} cr)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3000 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting && Math.Abs(hauler.Cargo - 600) < 1e-6 && !hauler.Escorted, "AUTO-SELL bought: completely full, it leaves by itself, alone");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_autosell");
        Character.BossCleared.Clear();

        // ── a LONE RUN against the dice: EVASION's chance, or the cargo is lost past the portal ──
        async Task FastRun()
        {   // lift, cruise, charge and sell in a moment: the states are what is being checked
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && hauler.State is not (Hauler.St.Arriving or Hauler.St.Destroyed); i++)
            {
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && hauler.Position.X < Hub.PortalPos.X - 5) hauler.Position = new Vector2(Hub.PortalPos.X - 1, Hub.LaneY);
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerAway;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
        }
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 350; Hauler.PretendRoll = 0.65f;          // Evasion 0: 60% safe, and the dice read 0.65
        double lc0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await FastRun();
        Check(hauler.RunLost && hauler.LastSale == 0 && Y.Credits == lc0 && hauler.Cargo == 0,
              $"a lone run the dice go against (0.65 against 60% safe): the cargo is lost past the portal, nothing paid ({hauler.LastSale:0} cr)");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y))["hauler_evasion"] = 1;
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 350;
        double lc1 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.Dispatch(escorted: false); await FastRun();
        Check(!hauler.RunLost && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - lc1 - 350) < 1e-6,
              $"one level of EVASION (67%) gets the same dice through: 350 sold for 350 ({Y.Credits - lc1:0})");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_evasion");
        Hauler.PretendRoll = null;

        // ── the hauler's OWN point defence: one mount, always on, bought up 5% a level ──
        {
            hauler.ResetToPad();
            var mounts = Nodes<Turret>(hauler).ToList();
            Check(mounts.Count == 1 && mounts[0].PointDefense && hauler.PdOnline && hauler.PdRing == 0f,
                  $"the hauler carries one point-defence mount, always online, with no window to run down ({mounts.Count} mounts)");
            Check(Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Damage - 1.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Interval - 0.5) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Range - 400f) < 1e-3,
                  $"its gun: 1 damage a shot every 0.5 s inside 400 u ({hauler.Spec(true).Damage:0.00} / {hauler.Spec(true).Interval:0.00} s / {hauler.Spec(true).Range:0} u)");
            ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y))["hauler_pd_damage"] = 3;
            Check(Math.Abs(hauler.Spec(true).Damage - 1.15) < 1e-9,
                  $"three levels of hauler point defence: +5% each, 1.15 a shot ({hauler.Spec(true).Damage:0.000})");
            ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_pd_damage");
            // a hostile missile crossing the pad is shot down without anyone opening a window
            int down0 = Torpedo.Intercepted;
            var mp = hauler.Position + new Vector2(0, -250);
            Combat.LaunchTorpedo(mp, Vector2.Down, 40f, 600f, 5, 0, 0f, heavy: false, hostile: true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Torpedo.Intercepted == down0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Torpedo.Intercepted > down0, "and it shoots down a missile that comes at it, with nobody flying it");
        }

        // bought for real: five levels to 95%, and no sixth
        Y.Credits = 100000;
        bool fiveBought = Enumerable.Range(0, 5).All(_ => Y.TryBuy("hauler_evasion"));
        Check(fiveBought && Math.Abs(Y.RunSafe - 0.95) < 1e-9 && !Y.TryBuy("hauler_evasion") && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - (100000 - 12400)) < 1e-6,
              $"EVASION bought five times (12400 cr): 95% safe, and a sixth is refused ({Y.RunSafe:0.00})");
        ((System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, int>)typeof(Yard).GetField("_levels", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Y)).Remove("hauler_evasion");

        // ── THE ESCORT: the long way round, waves of hunters after the hauler, x5 at the portal ──
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Y.Ore = 0; Y.Salvage = 0;
        await Wait(0.2);
        var hh = Nodes<HaulerHud>().FirstOrDefault(); var escBtn = hh == null ? null : Nodes<Button>(hh).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Escort");
        Check(escBtn != null && escBtn.Visible, "the base owner's hauler offers ESCORT beside DISPATCH");
        Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true);
        // THE ROUTE, in the spec's terms: the four outposts counter-clockwise from the south-east --
        // held beside, on the base's side -- then the portal
        var corners = new[] { new Vector2(2121.3f, 2121.3f), new Vector2(2121.3f, -2121.3f), new Vector2(-2121.3f, -2121.3f), new Vector2(-2121.3f, 2121.3f) };
        bool routeOk = Hub.EscortRoute.Length == 5 && Hub.EscortRoute[4] == new Vector2(1500, 219)
                       && Enumerable.Range(0, 4).All(i => Hub.EscortRoute[i].DistanceTo(corners[i]) < 250f && Hub.EscortRoute[i].Length() < corners[i].Length()
                                                          && Hub.EscortRoute[i].DistanceTo(corners[i]) > 100f + 85f);
        Check(routeOk, $"the escort's route: SE, NE, NW, SW -- beside each outpost -- then the portal ({string.Join(" ", Hub.EscortRoute.Select(p => $"({p.X:0},{p.Y:0})"))})");
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.HaulerPad) < 150; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var toFirst = Hub.EscortRoute[0] - hauler.Position;
        float heading = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hauler.Rotation, toFirst.Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
        var went = (hauler.Position - Hub.HaulerPad).Normalized();
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Escorted && hauler.Leg == 0 && went.X > 0.6f && went.Y > 0.5f && heading < 10,
              $"ESCORT leaves the lane south-east, for the first outpost, nose on its heading ({hauler.State}, going ({went.X:0.00}, {went.Y:0.00}), {heading:0.0} deg off)");
        // the first wave: 1 patrol for a pilot alone -- and it goes for the hauler, not the nearer ship
        int huntR0 = H.Raiders.Count;
        var hunterEdge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hauler.Position);
        Me.Position = hauler.Position + (hunterEdge - hauler.Position) * 0.35f; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        for (int i = 0; i < 600 && H.Raiders.Count == huntR0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double firstWaveAt = hauler.T;
        await Wait(0.5);
        var hunters = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Quarry == hauler).ToList();
        bool meNearer = hunters.Count > 0 && hunters.All(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Me.Position) < r.Position.DistanceTo(hauler.Position));
        Check(H.Raiders.Count - huntR0 == 3 && hunters.Count == 3 && hunters.All(r => r.Target == hauler) && meNearer && Math.Abs(firstWaveAt - 5.0) < 0.05,
              $"5 s in, a wave of 3 hunts the hauler -- not the ship that is nearer to them ({H.Raiders.Count - huntR0} spawned at {firstWaveAt:0.00} s, {hunters.Count(r => r.Target == hauler)} on it)");
        Check(hunters.All(r => !r.Heavy && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - 0.5 * 25 * r.Strength) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.Hp - r.MaxHull) < 1e-9),
              $"the first wave is light fighters only, at half hull ({string.Join(", ", hunters.Select(r => $"{(r.Heavy ? "heavy" : "light")} {r.Hp:0.0}"))})");
        // THE ESCORT'S THREAT sets each wave: a quarter each from the load (a level per 1000 cr it will
        // fetch escorted), the party (half its mean level, half its ships' toughness -- a level per 10%
        // of hull over its class's own), the highest boss, and the route (half a level a wave)
        Check(Math.Abs(Hub.EscortThreat(0, 1, 1, 0, 0) - 1.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Hub.EscortThreat(4000, 5, 3, 5, 8) - 4.75) < 1e-9,
              "an escort's threat: 1 for a fresh base's first wave; 4.75 for 4000 cr aboard, a level-5 party of toughness 3, the level-5 boss beaten, the ninth wave");
        Check(Math.Abs(Hub.ThreatStrength(4.75) - Math.Pow(1.1, 3.75)) < 1e-9 && Hub.ThreatLights(1.0) == 3 && Hub.ThreatLights(4.75) == 4 && Hub.ThreatLights(40) == 6
              && Math.Abs(Hub.ThreatAgility(4.75) - 1.0375) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Hub.ThreatAgility(40) - 1.1) < 1e-9,
              "...which sets the hunters' hull and damage (+10% a level), their numbers (a light more every 3 levels, 3 more at most) and, very slightly, their speed and turning (1% a level, 10% at most)");
        {
            double ownHull = new ShipStats(Me.Class)["hull"];
            double tough = 1 + Math.Log(Math.Max(1, Me.MaxHp / ownHull)) / Math.Log(1.1);
            double threat = 0.25 * ((1 + 300 * 5 / 1000.0) + (0.5 * Character.Level + 0.5 * tough) + Math.Max(1, Missions.HighestBeaten) + 1);
            Check(hunters.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - Math.Pow(1.1, threat - 1)) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.Agility - (1 + Math.Min(0.1, 0.01 * (threat - 1)))) < 1e-9),
                  $"the first wave carries THIS escort's threat: 300 units at x5 aboard, this pilot (level {Character.Level}), the highest boss, the first wave ({threat:0.000}; x{hunters.FirstOrDefault()?.Strength:0.000})");
        }
        Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(-2600, 1800); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        hauler.T = 24.9;
        await Wait(0.05); int before25 = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        await Wait(0.2);
        var wave2 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Quarry == hauler).ToList();
        var heavy2 = wave2.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Heavy);
        Check(before25 == 3 && wave2.Count == 7 && wave2.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1 && heavy2 != null && Math.Abs(heavy2.MaxHull - 0.5 * Enemies.Of(heavy2.Kind).Hull * heavy2.Strength) < 1e-9,
              $"the next wave 20 s later, at 25 s, brings a heavy (at half hull): 3 hunters just before, 7 after ({before25}, {wave2.Count}, {wave2.Count(r => r.Heavy)} heavy)");
        hauler.T = 44.9; await Wait(0.2);
        Check(H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 10 && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler && r.Heavy) == 1,
              $"...and the third, at 45 s, is light fighters only again ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler)} hunters)");

        // AT THE FIRST OUTPOST: it eases in beside it and holds 4 s, offloading a quarter of the load --
        // the pods drain, the cargo (and so the pay) untouched, the waves still coming -- then flies on
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);                     // a latched hunter pins it: clear them
        await Wait(0.4);
        hauler.Position = Hub.EscortRoute[0] + new Vector2(-30, -30);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hauler.StopLeft <= 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var docked = hauler.Position; double stop0 = hauler.StopLeft, shown0 = hauler.ShownCargo, tStop0 = hauler.T, cargo0 = hauler.Cargo;
        await Wait(2.0);
        double shownMid = hauler.ShownCargo, tMid = hauler.T;
        bool held = hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked) < 0.01f && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Leg == 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && hauler.Leg == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double shownAfter = hauler.ShownCargo;
        await Wait(1.0);
        Check(stop0 > 3.9 && stop0 <= 4.0 && docked.DistanceTo(Hub.EscortRoute[0]) < 0.6f && held && tMid - tStop0 > 1.9,
              $"at the south-east outpost it holds beside it for 4 s -- its clock, and the waves, running on ({stop0:0.00} s, {docked.DistanceTo(Hub.EscortRoute[0]):0.0} u off)");
        Check(Math.Abs(shown0 - 300) < 8 && Math.Abs(shownMid - 262.5) < 8 && Math.Abs(shownAfter - 225) < 1 && hauler.Cargo == cargo0,
              $"offloading: the pods drain a quarter of the load, 300 -> 262 -> 225 shown, the cargo aboard untouched ({shown0:0}, {shownMid:0}, {shownAfter:0}; {hauler.Cargo:0})");
        Check(hauler.Leg == 1 && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked) > 5f && (hauler.Position - docked).Dot(Hub.EscortRoute[1] - docked) > 0,
              $"then on to the north-east outpost (leg {hauler.Leg}, {hauler.Position.DistanceTo(docked):0} u on)");
        // FOR THE WHOLE ROUTE: the twelfth wave, far past the four there used to be, still comes on time
        typeof(Hauler).GetField("_waves", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, 11);
        int preLate = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        hauler.T = 224.9; await Wait(0.2);
        Check(H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) - preLate == 4, $"waves keep coming for the whole route: the twelfth, at 225 s ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) - preLate} more)");
        // near the end of the route: it charges at the portal, and the hunters withdraw at the jump
        typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1);
        hauler.Position = Hub.PortalPos + new Vector2(0, 20);
        for (int i = 0; i < 120 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging && hauler.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.PortalPos) < 0.6f, $"the route ends in the portal: it charges there ({hauler.State})");
        int booms0 = Nodes<Explosion>().Count();
        double ec0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge; await Wait(0.1);
        Check(hauler.State == Hauler.St.Away && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 0 && Nodes<Explosion>().Count() <= booms0,
              $"through the portal: the hunters withdraw, none shot down ({H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler)} left)");
        double atJump = hauler.Cargo;                  // 300, and whatever a delivery added before it lifted
        hauler.T = Economy.HaulerAway; await Wait(0.1);
        Check(atJump >= 300 && Math.Abs(Y.Credits - ec0 - 5 * atJump) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(hauler.LastSale - 5 * atJump) < 1e-6,
              $"an escort pays 5x: {atJump:0} sold for {Y.Credits - ec0:0}");
        // lost on an escort: the cargo goes, the hunters leave, and the usual rebuild starts
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300;
        Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true); hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift; await Wait(0.1);
        hauler.T = Economy.EscortFirstWave; await Wait(0.3);
        int huntersOut = H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler);
        double dc0 = Y.Credits;
        hauler.TakeDamage(1000); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(huntersOut == 3 && hauler.State == Hauler.St.Destroyed && hauler.Cargo == 0 && Y.Credits == dc0 && !hauler.Escorted
              && Math.Abs(hauler.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2 && H.Raiders.Count(r => r.Quarry == hauler) == 0,
              $"destroyed on the escort: the cargo is gone, the hunters leave, a rebuild in 30 s ({huntersOut} hunters, {hauler.RebuildIn:0.0} s)");
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        hauler.ResetToPad();
        // ...and one through the portal counts what it will be paid: x5 on an escort, nothing for a lost run
        async Task ToAway()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && hauler.State != Hauler.St.Away; i++)
            {
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Lifting) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerLift;
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Departing && hauler.Position.X < Hub.PortalPos.X - 5) hauler.Position = new Vector2(Hub.PortalPos.X - 1, Hub.LaneY);
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Escorting && hauler.Leg < Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1)
                { typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hauler, Hub.EscortRoute.Length - 1); hauler.Position = Hub.PortalPos + new Vector2(0, 20); }
                if (hauler.State == Hauler.St.Charging) hauler.T = Economy.HaulerCharge;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
        }
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: true); await ToAway();
        Y.SaveBase(); double escortOnFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits;
        foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
        hauler.ResetToPad(); hauler.Cargo = 300; Hauler.PretendRoll = 0.99f; Y.RequestDispatch(escorted: false); await ToAway();
        Y.SaveBase(); double lostOnFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "credits", -1.0) - Y.Credits;
        double lostOre = (double)OnDisk("base", "ore", -1.0) - Y.Ore - Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner).Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        double lostSalvage = (double)OnDisk("base", "salvage", -1.0) - Y.Salvage - Y.Gatherers.Where(g => g.Kind != GatherKind.Miner).Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        Hauler.PretendRoll = null;
        Check(Math.Abs(escortOnFile - 1500) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostOnFile) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostOre) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lostSalvage) < 1e-6,
              $"a save while the hauler is through the portal counts what it will be paid: an escort's 300 as 1500, a lost run's as nothing -- no credits, no stock back ({escortOnFile:0}, {lostOnFile:0}, ore {lostOre:0}, salvage {lostSalvage:0})");
        hauler.ResetToPad();
        // A SAVE COUNTS THE LOADS: with AUTO-SELL locked a full hauler waiting on its pad is the
        // usual state, and a quit used to throw its load away
        hauler.Cargo = 300;
        double loaded = Y.Ore + Y.Salvage + hauler.Cargo + Y.Gatherers.Sum(g => g.Cargo);
        Y.SaveBase();
        double onFile = (double)OnDisk("base", "ore", -1.0) + (double)OnDisk("base", "salvage", -1.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(onFile - loaded) < 1e-6 && hauler.Cargo == 300, $"a save counts what the hauler and the fleet are carrying, as if home ({onFile:0} of {loaded:0})");
        hauler.ResetToPad();

        var t = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First();
        ship.Rotation = 1.3f; await Wait(0.05);
        Check(t.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.ToGlobal(t.Offset)) < 0.5f, "turret on its mount after turning");
        Check(Count<HullHud>() == 1 && Count<AbilityBar>() == 1, "hull bar and ability bar on the HUD");

        // ── naval helm ──
        await Park(new Vector2(-1200, 1400));
        var p0 = ship.Position; float r0 = ship.Rotation;
        // dead in the water: A/D PIVOT the hull slowly (10 deg/s) -- and never strafe
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(1.5); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(0.2);
        float portTurn = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(r0, ship.Rotation));
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(1.5); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(0.2);
        Check(portTurn < -12f && portTurn > -18f && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(r0, ship.Rotation)) < Mathf.DegToRad(2f) && ship.Position.DistanceTo(p0) < 0.5f,
              $"dead in the water: A/D pivot the hull slowly ({portTurn:0.0} deg, then back) and never strafe");
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(4.0);
        var fwd = Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation);
        Check(ship.SpeedAhead > 0.95 * ship.Stats["max_speed"] && (ship.Position - p0).Normalized().Dot(fwd) > 0.999f, $"W drives ahead along the keel ({ship.SpeedAhead:0} u/s)");
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["max_speed"] - 104) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["turn_radius"] - 107) < 0.01, "battleship: 104 u/s top speed, 107 u turning radius");
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(10.0);                    // settle into the turning circle
        float ra = ship.Rotation; double v = ship.SpeedAhead; await Wait(1.0);
        float omega = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(ra, ship.Rotation));
        double radius = v / omega;
        var side = new Vector2(-Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation).Y, Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation).X);
        float drift = Mathf.Abs(ship.Velocity.Dot(side));
        GD.Print($"  turning: {v:0.0} u/s at {omega:0.000} rad/s = radius {radius:0.0} (sheet {ship.Stats["turn_radius"]:0}); sideways drift {drift:0.00} u/s");
        Check(Math.Abs(radius - ship.Stats["turn_radius"]) / ship.Stats["turn_radius"] < 0.05, "turning radius matches the sheet (±5%)");
        Check(drift < 3f, "the keel holds: almost no sideways drift in the turn");
        KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.S); await Wait(8.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.S);
        Check(ship.SpeedAhead < -10 && ship.SpeedAhead >= -ship.Stats["reverse_speed"] - 0.5, $"S drives astern, capped ({ship.SpeedAhead:0} u/s)");
        // turning in place: at rest (<= 5% of top speed) the rudder pivots the hull, 10 deg/s, no strafing
        {
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
            var pp0 = ship.Position; float pr0 = ship.Rotation;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(2.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D);
            float pivoted = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(pr0, ship.Rotation));
            Check(Math.Abs(pivoted - 20f) < 3f && ship.Position.DistanceTo(pp0) < 1f && ship.Velocity.Length() < 0.5f,
                  $"at rest, D pivots the hull on the spot at 10 deg/s ({pivoted:0.0} deg in 2 s), without sliding");
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Up.Rotated(ship.Rotation) * (float)ship.Stats["max_speed"] * 0.6f;   // well above 5%
            float pr1 = ship.Rotation;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.D); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.D);
            float underWay = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(pr1, ship.Rotation));
            Check(underWay > 15f, $"under way it turns by the rudder as before, far faster ({underWay:0} deg in 1 s)");
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        }

        // ── the K window: tabs, stats, rebinding ──
        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 1, "K opens the ability & stats window");
        var win = Nodes<StatsWindow>().First();
        var labels = Nodes<Label>(win).Select(l => l.Text).ToList();
        Check(labels.Any(x => x.Contains("Main guns (4 barrels):  44.75 DPS")) && labels.Any(x => x.Contains("Turning radius")), "stats tab: gun DPS and helm stats");
        Check(labels.Any(x => x.Contains("Broadside: 3 volleys × 4 shells") && x.Contains("= 268.5 damage, every 15.0 s")) && !labels.Any(x => x.StartsWith("Missiles")),
              "stats tab: the broadside, 3 volleys of 4 shells = 268.5 damage every 15 s; no missiles");
        Check(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(win).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Key_")) == 10, "keys tab lists the battleship's 4 abilities + 6 open slots");
        Check(Enumerable.Range(1, 6).All(i => Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, $"open{i}") == global::Godot.Key.Key0 + i)
              && Abilities.For(ShipClass.Carrier).Count(a => a.Open) == 6 && Abilities.For((ShipClass)99).Length == 6,
              "six open hotkeys (1-6) on every class, including undefined ones");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.H); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.H && Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.broadside"), "broadside rebound to H and saved");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.H,
              "binding a taken key (G, fire mode's) swaps the two");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G, "a reserved key (W) is refused");
        win.StartCapture("broadside"); Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G, "V is warp, a fixed key: refused too");
        win.StartCapture("pd"); Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 1 && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "pd") == global::Godot.Key.Q, "Esc cancels a capture without closing the window");
        Nodes<Button>(win).First(b => b.Name == "ResetKeys").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.F && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.G, "reset restores defaults (fire mode on G)");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0, "K closes it");
        // KEYS FROM BEFORE THE BROADSIDE, in this machine's settings file: fire mode moved onto F (so
        // the missile went to G), and the reload kept on T. The broadside takes the missile's key --
        // never F beside fire mode -- and the battleship's reload, which is gone, keeps none.
        Settings.Keys.Clear();
        Settings.Keys["Battleship.firemode"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.F; Settings.Keys["Battleship.missile"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.G;
        Settings.Keys["Battleship.reload"] = (int)global::Godot.Key.T;
        Settings.Save(); Settings.Load();
        Check(Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.G && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "firemode") == global::Godot.Key.F
              && !Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.missile") && !Settings.Keys.ContainsKey("Battleship.reload"),
              $"an old settings file: the missile's key (G) is the broadside's, fire mode keeps F alone, the reload's key is dropped ({Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside")})");
        Settings.Keys.Clear(); Settings.Save();

        // ── dummies ──
        var d1 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
        var d2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter1");            // where dummy 2 stood: two practice fighters
        var pf2 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("PracticeFighter2");
        var d3 = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
        Check(Count<TargetDummy>() == 4 && new[] { d1.NetId, d2.NetId, pf2.NetId, d3.NetId }.Distinct().Count() == 4
              && d2.Fighter && pf2.Fighter && d2.HitRadius < 20f && !d1.Fighter && !d3.Fighter,
              "two dummies and two practice light fighters (small craft), all with distinct ids");

        // ── battleship main guns: cursor aim, hold Space, salvo == staggered ──
        var dummy = d1;
        await Park(dummy.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
        AimWorld(dummy.Position); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.GetGlobalMousePosition().DistanceTo(dummy.Position) < 3f, "test cursor lands on the dummy");
        await Wait(2.5);
        var mainT = Nodes<Turret>(ship).First(x => !x.PointDefense);
        float aimErr = Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mainT.GlobalRotation, (dummy.Position - mainT.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
        Check(aimErr < 0.02f, $"main guns point at the cursor (off {aimErr:0.000} rad)");
        Check(Mathf.Abs(ship.Rotation) < 0.001f, "the hull does not turn toward the cursor");
        dummy.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(dummy.Total == 0, "no fire without Space");

        ship.Staggered = false; dummy.ResetMeter();
        // shells take ~1 s to arrive: wait for the last ones before reading the total
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double salvo = dummy.Total; await Wait(1.2);
        ship.Staggered = true; dummy.ResetMeter();
        KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(20.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(1.5);
        double stag = dummy.Total;
        GD.Print($"  salvo {salvo:0.0} dmg / 20s;  staggered {stag:0.0} / 20s;  sheet {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.000} DPS");
        double want20 = ship.Stats.MainDps * 20, oneShot = ship.Stats["main_damage"];
        Check(Math.Abs(salvo - want20) <= 4 * oneShot + 0.01, $"salvo delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 salvo)");
        Check(Math.Abs(stag - want20) <= oneShot + 0.01, $"staggered delivers the sheet's {ship.Stats.MainDps:0.0} DPS (±1 shot)");
        bool before = ship.Staggered; Key(global::Godot.Key.G); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.Staggered != before, "G toggles salvo / staggered");

        // ── THE BROADSIDE (F): the main turrets swing onto the cursor through a 0.5 s wind-up, then
        //    every main gun fires three volleys 0.25 s apart; a 14 s cooldown; the ship steers throughout ──
        Check(Abilities.For(ShipClass.Battleship).Where(a => !a.Open).Select(a => a.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "firemode", "broadside", "pd" })
              && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Battleship, "broadside") == global::Godot.Key.F,
              "the battleship's bar: guns, fire mode, the BROADSIDE on F, point defence -- no missile, no reload");
        Check(ship.Stats["broadside_volleys"] == 3 && ship.Stats["broadside_mult"] == 1.0 && ship.Stats["broadside_windup"] == 0.5
              && ship.Stats["broadside_gap"] == 0.25 && ship.Stats["broadside_cooldown"] == 14 && ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 0,
              "the broadside: 3 volleys at a shell's own damage, a 0.5 s wind-up, 0.25 s between volleys, a 14 s cooldown");
        {
            // Aimed well AWAY first (south-east, ~135 degrees off the dummy): at their own 90 deg/s the
            // turrets would still be 90 degrees off when a 0.5 s wind-up ends, so only a wind-up that
            // really swings them onto the cursor can put every shell on the dummy.
            var mainsB = Nodes<Turret>(ship).Where(x => !x.PointDefense).ToList();
            float Off(Turret t) => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(t.GlobalRotation, (d1.Position - t.GlobalPosition).Angle()));
            AimWorld(ship.Position + new Vector2(450, 450)); await Wait(2.5);
            float off0 = mainsB.Min(Off);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            var seen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shell>(Nodes<Shell>());
            var volleys = new System.Collections.Generic.List<(double t, int n)>();
            double bc0 = ship.Clock, cdAtLast = -1, speedAtFire = -1; float offAtFire = -1; string barAiming = null, barFiring = null;
            AimWorld(d1.Position);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W);                                  // under way through the wind-up
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2; i++)
            {
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                AimWorld(d1.Position);                                     // the camera follows the ship: keep the cursor on the dummy
                if (barAiming == null && ship.BroadsideWindupLeft > 0) barAiming = AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Line;
                int fresh = Nodes<Shell>().Count(s => seen.Add(s));
                if (fresh == 0) continue;
                if (volleys.Count == 0) { offAtFire = mainsB.Max(Off); speedAtFire = ship.SpeedAhead; }
                volleys.Add((ship.Clock - bc0, fresh));
                if (volleys.Count == 1) barFiring = AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Line;
                if (volleys.Count == 3) cdAtLast = ship.BroadsideCooldownLeft;
            }
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            GD.Print($"  broadside: turrets {off0:0.00} rad off at the press, {offAtFire:0.000} at the first volley; volleys at {string.Join(", ", volleys.Select(v => $"{v.t:0.000}s x{v.n}"))}");
            Check(barAiming == "AIMING" && barFiring == "FIRING", $"the slot reads AIMING through the wind-up, FIRING through the volleys ({barAiming}, {barFiring})");
            Check(off0 > 2.0 && offAtFire >= 0 && offAtFire < 0.03,
                  $"the wind-up swings every main turret onto the cursor from ~135 degrees away -- far faster than their own 90 deg/s ({off0:0.00} -> {offAtFire:0.000} rad)");
            Check(volleys.Count == 3 && volleys.All(v => v.n == 4) && volleys[0].t > 0.45 && volleys[0].t < 0.6
                  && Math.Abs(volleys[1].t - volleys[0].t - 0.25) < 0.035 && Math.Abs(volleys[2].t - volleys[1].t - 0.25) < 0.035,
                  $"three volleys of all four guns: the first at the end of the 0.5 s wind-up, then 0.25 s apart ({volleys.Count} volleys)");
            Check(speedAtFire > 10, $"the ship steers throughout: under way through the wind-up ({speedAtFire:0} u/s ahead at the first volley)");
            await Wait(0.6);
            Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 268.5) < 0.01, $"every shell lands: 3 volleys x 4 shells x 22.375 (17.9 x1.25 by the chips) = 268.5 ({d1.Total:0.000})");
            Check(cdAtLast > 13.9 && cdAtLast <= 14 && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "broadside", H.Selected).Busy > 0.8f,
                  $"the 14 s cooldown starts with the last volley, and the slot shows it ({cdAtLast:0.00} s)");
            int shellsBefore = Nodes<Shell>().Count(s => seen.Add(s));
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.8);
            Check(ship.BroadsideWindupLeft == 0 && ship.BroadsideVolleysLeft == 0 && Nodes<Shell>().Count(s => seen.Add(s)) == 0,
                  "F during the cooldown does nothing");
            ship.Sl("broadside").Cool = 0.02;                       // the slot by name, not a private field
            await Wait(0.1); Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(ship.BroadsideWindupLeft > 0.4, $"once the cooldown is over, F winds up again ({ship.BroadsideWindupLeft:0.00} s)");
            await Wait(1.5);
            Check(Nodes<Torpedo>().All(t => t.HostileFire), "a battleship launches no missile of its own");
        }

        // ── the main guns fire SHELLS: straight, at 650 u/s (the battleship's), damage on arrival ──
        {
            var gs = Me; var gd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            var gKeep = gs.Position; gs.Position = gd.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; gs.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            AimWorld(gd.Position); await Wait(1.2);                                   // turrets swing onto it
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); gd.ResetMeter();
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = true });
            Shell sh1 = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && sh1 == null; i++) { AimWorld(gd.Position); sh1 = Nodes<Shell>().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.Space, Pressed = false });
            double atFire = gd.Total;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var sp0 = sh1.Position; var sd0 = sh1.Dir;
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float shSpd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) ? (sh1.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0f;
            Check(Math.Abs(shSpd - 650) < 6f && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(sh1) && sh1.Dir == sd0 && atFire == 0,
                  $"the guns fire shells at 650 u/s, straight, and nothing lands at once ({shSpd:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && gd.Total == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(gd.Total > 0 && Math.Abs(gd.Total % gs.Stats["main_damage"]) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gs.Stats["main_damage"] - 22.375) < 1e-9, $"a shell lands for 17.9 x1.25 by the chips = 22.375 ({gd.Total:0.000})");
            // the balance: guns 4 x 17.9 every 2 s = 35.8 DPS, and a broadside of 3 x 4 x 17.9 = 214.8 every
            // 15 s (the wind-up, two gaps, the cooldown): 50.12 DPS in all. The carrier reaches 1.5x as far.
            var bsS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship); var caS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier);
            double bsTotal = bsS.MainDps + bsS.BroadsideDps + bsS.MissileDps;
            Check(Math.Abs(bsS.MainDps - 35.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(bsS.BroadsideDamage - 214.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(bsS.BroadsideCycle - 15) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(bsTotal - 50.12) < 0.001,
                  $"the battleship: guns 35.8 DPS and a 214.8-damage broadside every 15 s -- 50.12 DPS in all, no missile ({bsTotal:0.000})");
            Check(Math.Abs(caS["control_range"] - 1500) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bsS["main_range"] - 1000) < 1e-6,
                  "the carrier's fighters reach 1500 u, 1.5x the battleship's 1000 u guns");
            gs.Position = gKeep; gs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        }

        // ── warp (V): every capital ship; a hull cooldown, not a slot ──
        {
            var me = Me; var warpHome = me.Position; var d2w = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            void Ready() { typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_warpCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; }
            void Deselect() => typeof(Hub).GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            Check(Abilities.For(me.Class).All(a => a.Id != "warp"), "warp takes no slot on the ability bar");
            Ready(); Deselect(); var start = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(me.Warping && me.Position.DistanceTo(start) < 20f, "V starts a 3 s warm-up; the ship holds until it jumps");
            await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup);
            var moved = me.Position - start;
            Check(!me.Warping && Math.Abs(moved.Length() - 2000f) < 40f && Mathf.Abs(Vector2.Up.AngleTo(moved)) < 0.05f,
                  $"nothing selected ahead: 2000 u straight ahead ({moved.Length():0} u)");
            Check(Math.Abs(me.WarpCooldownLeft - PlayerShip.WarpCooldown) < 0.6 && PlayerShip.WarpCooldown == 30, "then a 30 s cooldown");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!me.Warping, "V does nothing while it cools down");
            // a target within 45 degrees of the bow: arrive just short of it
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); await Wait(0.3);
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float want = d2w.HitRadius + Classes.Art(me.Class).Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f, $"a target ahead: it warps to just short of it ({me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position):0} u, wanted {want:0})");
            // a target behind: outside the cone, so straight ahead instead
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); var s3 = me.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Position - s3).Y + 2000f) < 40f && H.Selected == d2w, "a target behind (outside 45 degrees): straight ahead instead");
            // aimed when it JUMPS, by the heading then: swing onto a target during the charge...
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.2);          // facing north, d2 behind
            Click(Scr(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);                // now facing it
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(d2w.Position) - want) < 20f, "V pressed facing away, then turned onto the target: it warps to the target");
            // ...or turn away from it, and it goes straight on along the new heading
            Ready(); me.Position = d2w.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); await Wait(0.2);           // facing north, d2 ahead
            var s4 = me.Position;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(1.0);
            me.Rotation = Mathf.Pi / 2f; await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup - 1.0 + 0.2);            // now facing east
            var mv = me.Position - s4;
            Check(Math.Abs(mv.X - 2000f) < 40f && Math.Abs(mv.Y) < 40f, $"V pressed facing the target, then turned east: 2000 u east ({mv.X:0}, {mv.Y:0})");
            // the radar: click a marker to select it -- an enemy as the target, a landmark as a waypoint
            // beside the base: every landmark it clicks below is within the radar's 3600 u reach from here
            Ready(); me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 300); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.6);
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First(); var rr = radar.GetGlobalRect(); float rad = rr.Size.X / 2f;
            Vector2 OnRadar(Vector2 w) => rr.Position + new Vector2(rad, rad) + (w - me.ViewPosition) * ((rad - 6f) / Radar.Range);
            H.ClearSelection();
            Click(OnRadar(d2w.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == d2w, "clicking an enemy on the radar makes it the target");
            Click(OnRadar(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null && H.Waypoint == Hub.BasePos && H.WaypointName == "BASE", "clicking the base on the radar makes it a waypoint (and drops the target)");
            var ne = new Vector2(2121.3f, -2121.3f);
            Click(OnRadar(ne)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.WaypointName == "OUTPOST NE" && H.Waypoint is { } wp && wp.DistanceTo(ne) < 1f, $"an outpost is on the radar: clicking it makes OUTPOST NE the waypoint ({H.WaypointName})");
            Click(OnRadar(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.05);
            me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 1500); me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.2);    // the base dead ahead
            Key(global::Godot.Key.V); await Wait(PlayerShip.WarpWarmup + 0.2);
            float wantB = 260f + Classes.Art(me.Class).Length * 0.5f + PlayerShip.WarpStandoff;
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - wantB) < 20f, $"a warp to the waypoint lands just short of the base ({me.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u)");
            H.ClearSelection();
            Ready(); me.Position = warpHome;
        }

        // ── battleship PD: idle until Q; slow independent turrets ──
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);                      // among all three dummies
        foreach (var d in Nodes<TargetDummy>()) d.ResetMeter();
        await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == 0 && !ship.PdActive, "PD does nothing until activated");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(4.0);
        var pds = ship.PdTurrets;
        Check(ship.PdActive && pds.All(x => x.Target != null) && pds.Select(x => x.Target).Distinct().Count() == pds.Count,
              $"Q: each battleship PD turret picks its own target ({string.Join(",", pds.Select(x => (x.Target as TargetDummy)?.Number))})");
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) > 0, "PD fires while active");
        Check(ship.Stats["pd_turn"] < Mathf.Tau / 2.9f, "battleship PD turns slowly (τ/3)");
        await Wait(11.5);
        Check(!ship.PdActive && ship.PdRechargeLeft > 14, "the window closes after 15 s and recharge starts");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(!ship.PdActive, "Q does nothing while recharging");
        double mid = Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(Nodes<TargetDummy>().Sum(d => d.Total) == mid, "no PD fire while recharging");
        // A readout goes to the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position. The dummies are 1, 3,
        // 4 and 5 (2's spot holds the two practice fighters), so indexing the list by number-1
        // sent 3's figures to 4, 4's to 5, and dropped 5's entirely because 5 > Count: guests
        // read the wrong numbers off the wrong hulls.
        var dummy5 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 5);
        var dummy4 = Nodes<TargetDummy>().First(x => x.Number == 4);
        double l5 = dummy5.LastSecond, a5 = dummy5.Average10, t5 = dummy5.Total;
        double l4 = dummy4.LastSecond, a4 = dummy4.Average10, t4 = dummy4.Total;
        Priv(H, "NetDummy", 5, 7.0, 8.0, 9.0);
        double got5 = dummy5.Total, got4 = dummy4.Total;                   // read BEFORE restoring
        bool routed = Math.Abs(got5 - 9.0) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(got4 - t4) < 1e-9;
        dummy5.SetReadout(l5, a5, t5); dummy4.SetReadout(l4, a4, t4);      // put them back
        Check(routed, $"a dummy's readout reaches the dummy with that NUMBER, not that position (5 got {got5:0.#} of 9, 4 sat at {got4:0.#})");
        // Combat.Clear drops the hooks the world installed on its way out. OnShell was left
        // behind: a lambda holding the Hub, so the next shot called into a freed node, and a Hub
        // that could never be collected once you were back at the menu. Snapshot and restore --
        // this all happens inside one frame, so nothing ever sees the cleared state.
        var keepHostiles = Combat.Hostiles.ToList(); var keepPlayers = Combat.Players.ToList();
        var keepFlash = Combat.OnFlash; var keepWorld = Combat.World; var keepShell = Combat.ShellFired; var keepTorp = Combat.TorpedoFired;
        Combat.Clear();
        bool hooksDropped = Combat.OnFlash == null && Combat.World == null && Combat.ShellFired == null && Combat.TorpedoFired == null && Combat.SlugFired == null;
        Combat.Hostiles.AddRange(keepHostiles); Combat.Players.AddRange(keepPlayers);
        Combat.OnFlash = keepFlash; Combat.World = keepWorld; Combat.ShellFired = keepShell; Combat.TorpedoFired = keepTorp;
        Check(hooksDropped, "Combat.Clear lets go of the world and every hook it installed");

        // ── A REFIT, on the battleship: the hull keeps its fraction (keeping the damage taken let a
        //    pilot heal by swapping parts) ──
        {
            var bl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Battleship);
            double max0 = ship.MaxHp;
            ship.Hp = 75;
            bl[2] = "sh_bulwark_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            double bulMax = ship.MaxHp, bulHp = ship.Hp;
            bl[2] = "sh_glass_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            double glMax = ship.MaxHp, glHp = ship.Hp;
            bl[2] = "basic_deflector"; H.PilotChanged();
            Check(Math.Abs(max0 - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bulMax - 615) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bulHp - 123) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(glMax - 300) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(glHp - 60) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.MaxHp - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.Hp - 75) < 1e-6,
                  $"a refit keeps the hull's fraction: 75/375, Bulwark III {bulHp:0}/{bulMax:0}, Glass III {glHp:0}/{glMax:0}, back {ship.Hp:0}/{ship.MaxHp:0} -- no heal by swapping");
            ship.Hp = ship.MaxHp;
        }

        // ── THE DESTROYER: the owner's hull, the smallest of the three; two main guns, missile bursts, the fastest ──
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Destroyer); ship = Me;
        {
            var dspr = ship.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First();
            var dts = Nodes<Turret>(ship).ToList();
            Check(ship.Class == ShipClass.Destroyer && dspr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("destroyer_hull.png") && Math.Abs(dspr.Texture.GetHeight() * dspr.Scale.Y - 212.625f) < 0.5f
                  && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).HalfWidth - 27.8f) < 1e-4 && dts.Count(t => !t.PointDefense) == 2 && dts.Count(t => t.PointDefense) == 2
                  && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Carrier).Length - 283.5f) < 1e-4 && Math.Abs(Classes.Art(ShipClass.Battleship).Length - 378f) < 1e-4,
                  "the destroyer 25% smaller than the carrier (212.625 u), the carrier 25% smaller than the battleship (283.5 of 378); a 27.8 u half-beam; two main turrets, two PD");
            Check(help.Text.StartsWith("DESTROYER") && help.Text.Contains("mouse aims the main guns"), "controls line is destroyer-specific");
            Check(Abilities.For(ShipClass.Destroyer).Where(a => !a.Open).Select(a => a.Id).SequenceEqual(new[] { "guns", "firemode", "missile", "reload", "pd" })
                  && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Destroyer, "missile") == global::Godot.Key.F && Abilities.KeyFor(ShipClass.Destroyer, "reload") == global::Godot.Key.R,
                  "the destroyer's bar: guns, fire mode, the missile burst on F, reload on R, point defence");
            var ddS = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Destroyer); var bsS0 = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship); var cvS0 = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier);
            bool N9(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-9;
            Check(N9(ddS["hull"], 250) && N9(ship.MaxHp, 312.5) && N9(ddS["main_count"], 2) && N9(ddS["pd_count"], 2) && ddS["fighter_count"] == 0 && ddS["broadside_volleys"] == 0,
                  $"250 hull (312.5 with the five chips), two main guns, two PD; no wing, no broadside ({ship.MaxHp:0.0})");
            Check(N9(bsS0["max_speed"], 104) && N9(ddS["max_speed"], 130) && N9(cvS0["max_speed"], 116.48)
                  && N9(ddS["thrust"], 70) && N9(ddS["reverse_thrust"], 30) && N9(ddS["reverse_speed"], 45)
                  && N9(cvS0["thrust"], 48 * 116.48 / 96) && N9(cvS0["reverse_thrust"], 20 * 116.48 / 96) && N9(cvS0["reverse_speed"], 32 * 116.48 / 96)
                  && N9(ddS["turn_radius"], 107) && N9(ddS["turn_rate"], 1.08) && N9(cvS0["turn_radius"], 127) && N9(cvS0["turn_rate"], 0.9),
                  "speeds: the destroyer 130 u/s (the battleship's 104 +25%), the carrier 116.48 (+12%); each class's accelerations and astern speed scale with it; turning unchanged");
            Check(N9(ddS.MainDps, 15.0) && Math.Abs(ddS.MissileDps - 441.0 / 9.8) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ddS.MainDps + ddS.MissileDps - 60.0) < 0.001,
                  $"the destroyer's damage: guns 11.8 DPS, bursts 2 x 3 x 5 every 16.6 s (1.81) -- 13.61 DPS ({ddS.MainDps + ddS.MissileDps:0.000})");
            // the fastest: flat out for 4 s it is past anything a battleship can reach
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), Mathf.Pi);
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(4.0);
            double ddAhead = ship.SpeedAhead;
            KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            Check(ddAhead > 0.95 * 130, $"W drives the destroyer to its 130 u/s -- past a battleship's 104 ({ddAhead:0} u/s)");
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
            ship.Staggered = false;
            // its two guns: Space fires both (salvo)
            AimWorld(d1.Position); await Wait(1.5);
            var seenD = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Shell>(Nodes<Shell>());
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.15); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            int ddShells = Nodes<Shell>().Count(s => seenD.Add(s));
            Check(ddShells == 2, $"Space fires the destroyer's two main guns ({ddShells} shells)");
            await Wait(2.0);

            // ── the missile BURST: a magazine of three bursts; a selected target in range, or it refuses ──
            System.Collections.Generic.List<Torpedo> Burst() => Nodes<Torpedo>().Where(x => x.Heavy && !x.HostileFire && Smoke(x).Count < 40).ToList();
            string SlotMsg() => AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "missile", H.Selected) is var st && st.Fail ? st.Line : null;
            Check(ship.Stats["missile_mag"] == 3 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, "the destroyer starts with a magazine of three bursts");
            // Esc only if something is selected: with nothing selected, Esc opens the Esc menu
            if (H.Selected != null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05); }
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Burst().Count == 0 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3 && SlotMsg() == "NO TARGET", "no target: nothing fires, and the slot says NO TARGET");
            await Wait(PlayerShip.FailShow + 0.1);
            Check(SlotMsg() == null, "the message clears after a moment");
            Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var keep = ship.Position; ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, (float)ship.Stats["missile_range"] + 150f); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Burst().Count == 0 && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3 && SlotMsg() == "OUT OF RANGE", "target out of range: nothing fires, and the slot says OUT OF RANGE");
            ship.Position = keep; ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.1);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var burst = Burst();
            var toTarget = (d1.Position - ship.ToGlobal(new Vector2(0, -Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).Length * 0.5f))).Angle();
            var offs = burst.Select(m => Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(toTarget, m.Dir.Angle()))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            GD.Print($"  burst: {burst.Count} missiles at {string.Join(", ", offs.Select(a => a.ToString("0.0")))} degrees off the line to the target");
            Check(burst.Count == 3 && burst.All(m => m.TargetId == d1.NetId && m.TurnRate == 1.5f) && ship.MissilesLoaded == 2 && d1.Total == 0
                  && Math.Abs(offs[1]) < 3 && offs[0] < -64 && offs[0] > -71 && offs[2] > 64 && offs[2] < 71,
                  "F launches a burst of three guided missiles: one at the target, two launched 70 degrees either side (magazine 2/3); nothing lands at once");
            float mspd = 0;
            if (burst.Count > 0)
            {
                var mp1 = burst[0].GlobalPosition; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                mspd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(burst[0]) ? (burst[0].GlobalPosition - mp1).Length() * 60f : 0;
            }
            Check(Math.Abs(mspd - 200) < 0.5f, $"they fly at 200 u/s ({mspd:0.0})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 900 && d1.Total < 183.74; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 183.75) < 0.01, $"all three land -- the outer two curve in: 3 x 49 x1.25 by the chips = 183.75 ({d1.Total:0.00})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.5);        // the 0.4 s between bursts
            int before3 = Burst().Count;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(ship.MissilesLoaded == 0 && Burst().Count - before3 == 3, $"the third burst empties the magazine ({Burst().Count - before3} new missiles, {ship.MissilesLoaded} left)");
            await Wait(0.7);
            var flying = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Torpedo>(Nodes<Torpedo>());
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Nodes<Torpedo>().All(flying.Contains), "empty magazine: F does nothing");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(ship.Reloading && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "reload", H.Selected).Busy > 0.9f, "R starts the reload; the bar shows it");
            await Wait(9.2);
            Check(!ship.Reloading && ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, "reload refills the magazine (3 bursts) after 9 s");
            // guided: move the target sideways and watch the middle missile follow, no faster than 1.5 rad/s
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            var midM = Burst().OrderBy(m => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(toTarget, m.Dir.Angle()))).FirstOrDefault(); var dhome = d1.Position;
            d1.Position = dhome + new Vector2(260, 0);
            float a0 = midM?.Dir.Angle() ?? 0; await Wait(1.0);
            float turned = midM != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(midM) ? Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(a0, midM.Dir.Angle())) : 0;
            Check(turned > 0.05 && turned <= 1.51, $"it steers toward the target, no faster than 1.5 rad/s ({turned:0.000} rad in 1 s)");
            await Wait(6.0); d1.Position = dhome;

            // A REFIT: the magazine never holds more than it has room for
            var dl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Destroyer); var dlKeep = (string[])dl.Clone();
            for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) dl[k] = "";
            dl[4] = "dd_salvo_3"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_mag"] - 6) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_reload"] - 12.0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["missile_damage"] - 39.2) < 1e-9,
                  $"Salvo Rack III: 6 bursts a magazine, a 12.0 s reload, 39.2 a missile ({ship.Stats["missile_mag"]}, {ship.Stats["missile_reload"]:0.000}, {ship.Stats["missile_damage"]})");
            ship.Sl("missile").N = 6;
            dl[4] = "dd_missile_rack"; H.PilotChanged();
            Check(ship.MissilesLoaded == 3, $"back to the kit rack: a 3-burst magazine holds 3, not the 6 loaded before ({ship.MissilesLoaded})");
            for (int k = 0; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) dl[k] = dlKeep[k];
            H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;

            // CLOSE IN, the fan narrows so the outer two can still come round: at 70 degrees on a
            // ~107 u turning circle they would circle a target 150 u off the nose until their run ended
            ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 150f + Classes.Art(ShipClass.Destroyer).Length * 0.5f);
            ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; ship.Rotation = 0f;
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); d1.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var close = Burst();
            float closeSplay = close.Count == 3 ? close.Max(m => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(Vector2.Up.Angle(), m.Dir.Angle())))) : -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && d1.Total < 183.74; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(close.Count == 3 && closeSplay > 20 && closeSplay < 50 && Math.Abs(d1.Total - 183.75) < 0.01,
                  $"a target 150 u off the nose: the outer two launch narrower ({closeSplay:0} degrees) and all three land (18.75; {d1.Total:0.00})");
            await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 600));
        }

        // ── carrier ──
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier); ship = Me;
        Check(ship.PdTurrets.Count == 3, "carrier has three PD turrets");

        // bombers park in bays on the deck, half each side of the runway, and stay put as the carrier moves
        Wing[] Bombers() => Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.IsBomber).ToArray();
        async Task CheckBays(int n)
        {
            await Wait(0.3);
            var bs = Bombers();
            int port = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X < 0), stbd = bs.Count(w => ship.ToLocal(w.GlobalPosition).X > 0);
            Check(bs.Length == n && bs.All(w => w.Docked && w.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.ToGlobal(ship.Bay(w))) < 0.5f) && port == n / 2 + n % 2 && stbd == n / 2,
                  $"{n} bombers parked in their bays: {port} port, {stbd} starboard");
        }
        await CheckBays(2);
        Character.Bonuses["bomber_count"] = 2.0; ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;   // 2 x (1 + 2) = 6
        await CheckBays(6);
        Character.Bonuses.Remove("bomber_count"); ship.SetClass(ship.Class); ship = Me;
        // ── GEAR THROUGH THE SHIP'S OWN REFIT: the owner's hangars, the wing rebuilt to match, and
        //    nothing left behind when craft are taken away ──
        {
            var cl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier); var clKeep = (string[])cl.Clone();
            for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) cl[k] = "";
            async Task Hangar(string part) { cl[0] = part; H.PilotChanged(); await Wait(0.1); ship = Me; }
            bool Near(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-6;
            int Craft() => Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship);
            await Hangar("cv_elite_3");
            bool elite = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 2) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 1056) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 7.5) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_turn"], 5.25);
            await Hangar("cv_swarm_3");
            bool swarm = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 9) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 274.56) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 1.95);
            int swarmCraft = Craft();
            await Hangar("cv_line_3");
            bool line = Near(ship.Stats["fighter_count"], 4) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_damage"], 3.5) && Near(ship.Stats["fighter_speed"], 422.4);
            Check(elite && swarm && line, $"the owner's hangars on the carrier itself: Elite III, Swarm III, Line III (elite {elite}, swarm {swarm}, line {line})");
            // a swarm launched, then Elite fitted mid-flight: the extra fighters are freed where they fly
            await Hangar("cv_swarm_3");
            await Park(d3.Position + new Vector2(0, 520), 0);
            H.SelectTarget(d3); Key(global::Godot.Key.Space);
            for (int i = 0; i < 180 && Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && !w.Inside) < 2; i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int out0 = Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && !w.Inside);
            await Hangar("cv_elite_3");
            int eliteCraft = Craft();
            await Hangar("cv_fighter_hangars");
            var wingOrder = (List<Wing>)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_wings", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(ship);
            int firstBomber = wingOrder.FindIndex(w => w.IsBomber);
            bool fightersFirst = firstBomber < 0 || wingOrder.Skip(firstBomber).All(w => w.IsBomber);
            Check(swarmCraft == 11 && out0 >= 2 && eliteCraft == 4 && Craft() == 5 && ship.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 3 && fightersFirst,
                  $"the wing follows the hangar: 9 + 2 craft, then Elite with fighters out -- 2 + 2, the rest freed -- then the kit's 3 + 2; fighters listed first ({swarmCraft}, {out0} out, {eliteCraft}, {Craft()})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.2);
            // bombers through gear: the bays re-split, and a strike counted on the old bombers is
            // called off (set up as one in flight, so the old code -- which never touched it -- differs)
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetProperty("StrikeTarget").SetValue(ship, d3);
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_strikesOut", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(ship, 2);
            int armed0 = ship.BombersReady;
            cl[4] = "cv_widebay_3"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            int armedAfter = ship.BombersReady;
            bool strikeOff = ship.StrikeTarget == null;
            await CheckBays(5);
            cl[4] = "cv_heavybay_1"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            await CheckBays(1);
            cl[4] = "cv_bomber_bay"; H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
            await CheckBays(2);
            Check(strikeOff, "a change in the bombers calls off a strike counted on the old ones");
            Check(armed0 == 2 && armedAfter == 2, $"a bay fitted in service adds bombers that arrive rearming, not armed: still 2 ready of 5 ({armed0} -> {armedAfter})");
            for (int k = 0; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) cl[k] = clKeep[k];
            H.PilotChanged(); ship = Me;
        }
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
        Check(Math.Abs(ship.Stats["control_range"] - 1500) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["strike_range"] - 3000) < 0.01,
              "fighter control range 1500 (1.5x the battleship's 1000 u guns); bomber strike range 3000 (2x)");
        var spr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(ship).First();
        Check(spr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("carrier_player.png") && Math.Abs(spr.Texture.GetHeight() * spr.Scale.Y - 283.5f) < 0.5f,
              "carrier flies the owner's line-art sprite at 283.5 u, 25% smaller than the battleship");
        await Park(new Vector2(780, 700), 0);
        var pdLight = H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(700, 860));            // a third light target (the practice fighters are two)
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(2.0);
        var cpds = ship.PdTurrets;
        var pdts = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts.Distinct().Count() == 3 && pdts.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td && td.Fighter)),
              "carrier PD: three turrets on three different LIGHT targets -- never a plain dummy");
        // two light targets for three turrets: the spare must double up on a light one, never a dummy
        if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(pdLight)) pdLight.TakeDamage(9999);
        await Wait(1.5);
        var pdts2 = cpds.Select(x => x.Target).Where(x => x != null).ToList();
        Check(pdts2.Count > 0 && pdts2.All(t => t is Raider || (t is TargetDummy td2 && td2.Fighter)),
              "with more turrets than light targets, the spare turret still never takes a plain dummy");

        // clicks select only; Space attacks; R recalls
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Click(Scr(d3.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d3, "carrier: left-click selects dummy 3");
        Click(Scr(d3.Position), MouseButton.Right); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "carrier: clicks never order an attack");
        Check(Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).All(w => w.Inside && !w.Visible),
              "idle fighters wait docked inside the carrier, out of sight");
