        // parked SMALL, as far below as the deck is: 65% of 28.125 u (the hauler's landed size), facing
        // the bow, the whole of it (18 u across) on the white between the runway's edge (15.2 u out) and
        // the hull's (37.9 u)
        foreach (var bw in Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.IsBomber && w.Docked))
        {
            var lp = ship.ToLocal(bw.Position); var bsp = Nodes<Sprite2D>(bw).First();
            float half = bsp.Texture.GetWidth() * bsp.Scale.X * 0.5f;
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(bw.Rotation, ship.Rotation)) < 0.01f && Math.Abs(bsp.Texture.GetHeight() * bsp.Scale.Y - 28.125f * 0.65f) < 0.05f
                  && Mathf.Abs(lp.X) - half > 14.7f && Mathf.Abs(lp.X) + half < 36f,
                  $"a parked bomber sits at 65% on the white deck beside the runway, facing the bow ({Mathf.Abs(lp.X) - half:0.0} to {Mathf.Abs(lp.X) + half:0.0} u out)");
        }
        int sig0 = ship.SignalsLit;
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == d3 && ship.StrikeTarget == null, "Space sends the fighters only (bombers are their own ability)");
        // fighters leave one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart (a hard limit, not a stat)
        var fts = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter).ToList();
        for (int fr = 0; fr < 200 && fts.Any(w => w.LaunchedAt < 0); fr++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var order = fts.Select(w => w.LaunchedAt).Where(t => t >= 0).OrderBy(t => t).ToList();
        double minGap = 99; for (int g = 1; g < order.Count; g++) minGap = Math.Min(minGap, order[g] - order[g - 1]);
        GD.Print($"  fighter launches at {string.Join(", ", order.Select(t => t.ToString("0.000")))} s; smallest gap {minGap:0.000} s");
        Check(fts.Count == 3 && ship.Stats["fighter_count"] == 3, "the carrier starts with 3 fighters");
        Check(order.Count == 3 && minGap >= Wing.LaunchInterval - 1e-9, "fighters launch one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart");
        Check(ship.SignalsLit > sig0 || order.Count == 3, "launching fighters light the hangar signal");
        Check(ship.Stats["torpedo_speed"] == 300 && ship.Stats["launch_range"] == 1900 && ship.Stats["torpedo_interval"] == 0.5,
              "torpedoes: 300 u/s, launched from 1900 u, one every 0.5 s");
        var fx0 = H.GetChildren().OfType<FlashLayer>().FirstOrDefault();
        Check(fx0 != null && !fx0.ZAsRelative && fx0.ZIndex > ship.ZIndex && fx0.ZIndex < ship.ZIndex + Nodes<Turret>(ship).First().ZIndex,
              "hit flashes draw above the hulls and below the turrets");
        // strafing runs: 3 shots, straight through by 1.2x the target's diameter, then round again
        var fx = Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter);
        int passes = 0; bool three = true, through = true; string lastPhase = fx.FighterPhase; float worst = 999f;
        for (int i = 0; i < 780; i++)                     // 13 s, inside the first 15 s of firing
        {
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            string ph = fx.FighterPhase;
            if (lastPhase == "Burst" && ph == "Overshoot") { passes++; three &= fx.ShotsThisPass == Wing.BurstShots; }
            if (lastPhase == "Overshoot" && ph == "Turn") { through &= fx.LastOvershoot >= Math.Max(1.2 * 2 * d3.HitRadius, 2 * 352 / 3.5) - 0.5; worst = Mathf.Min(worst, fx.LastOvershoot); }
            lastPhase = ph;
        }
        GD.Print($"  fighter: {passes} passes in 13 s; shortest overshoot {worst:0} u (needs {Math.Max(1.2 * 2 * d3.HitRadius, 2 * 352 / 3.5):0}: 1.2 diameters, and the fighter's own 201 u turning circle)");
        Check(passes >= 2 && three, $"fighters strafe: every pass fires exactly 3 shots ({passes} passes)");
        Check(through, "each pass carries on through the target by at least 1.2x its diameter -- and a turning circle -- then turns");
        var fighters = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.IsBomber).ToList();
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.Resting), "after 15 s of firing, every fighter heads home to rest");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && !fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => f.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(ship.GlobalPosition) < 1f), "they rest at the centre of the carrier");
        d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(2.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "no fire while resting");
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && fighters.Any(f => f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Check(fighters.All(f => !f.Resting), "after 3 s they rejoin the fight");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.WingTarget == null, "R recalls the fighters");
        await Wait(3.0); d3.ResetMeter(); await Wait(1.0);
        Check(d3.Total == 0, "recalled fighters stop firing");

        // A SMALL, SLOW TARGET -- the owner's report: fighters flew through an escort's light raiders
        // without firing. A practice light fighter drifting sideways at 12 u/s, a light riding a pinned
        // hauler's pace: a pass that ended 1.2 diameters past it left it inside the fighters' ~100 u
        // turning radius, and they flew laps round it, never firing again until they went home. Every
        // fighter must keep coming back for passes.
        {
            var pf = d2; var pfHome = pf.Position;
            await Park(pf.Position + new Vector2(0, 500), 0);
            var wing = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.IsBomber).ToList();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && !wing.All(f => f.Inside && !f.Resting); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Click(Scr(pf.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05);
            bool onIt = ship.WingTarget == pf;
            var shots = wing.ToDictionary(f => f, f => new System.Collections.Generic.List<double>());
            var prev = wing.ToDictionary(f => f, f => f.ShotsThisPass);
            double clock = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 780; i++)
            {
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double step = GetProcessDeltaTime(); clock += step;
                pf.Position += new Vector2(12f, 0) * (float)step;
                foreach (var f in wing)
                {
                    if (f.ShotsThisPass > prev[f]) shots[f].Add(clock);
                    prev[f] = f.ShotsThisPass;
                }
            }
            double Gap(System.Collections.Generic.List<double> t) =>
                t.Count == 0 ? 99 : Math.Max(t.Zip(t.Skip(1), (a, b) => b - a).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Max(), clock - t[^1]);
            var fired = wing.Where(f => shots[f].Count > 0).ToList();
            GD.Print($"  a light drifting at 12 u/s: {string.Join("; ", wing.Select(f => $"{shots[f].Count} shots, longest gap {Gap(shots[f]):0.0} s"))}");
            Check(onIt && fired.Count == wing.Count && fired.All(f => shots[f].Count >= 9 && Gap(shots[f]) < 4.0),
                  $"fighters on a small target drifting at 12 u/s keep making passes: every one fires again and again, no gap of 4 s ({string.Join(", ", wing.Select(f => $"{shots[f].Count}/{Gap(shots[f]):0.0}s"))})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(0.05);
            pf.Position = pfHome;
            // AND A TARGET THAT LEAVES: a hunter withdrawn when its escort ends is freed with hull left,
            // so it reads "alive" -- the wing must let go of it, not go on reading a freed node
            var gone = H.SpawnRaider(ship.Position + new Vector2(400, 0));
            gone.Quarry = H.Yard.Hauler;
            await Wait(0.1);
            Click(Scr(gone.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.3);
            bool attacked = ship.WingTarget == gone;
            // point defence on it too: a turret holding it must let go as well
            ship.Sl("pd").Left = 15.0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 90 && !ship.PdTurrets.Any(tu => tu.Target == gone); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool pdOn = ship.PdTurrets.Any(tu => tu.Target == gone);
            H.CallOff(H.Yard.Hauler); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(attacked && pdOn && ship.WingTarget == null && ship.PdTurrets.All(tu => tu.Target != gone),
                  $"a hunter called off while the fighters and point defence are on it: both let go of it ({attacked}, {pdOn}, {ship.WingTarget != null})");
            ship.Sl("pd").Left = 0.0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5);
        }

        // ── bombers: an active ability; straight, steady torpedoes with smoke ──
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5);
        Click(Scr(d1.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        // beyond the strike range the order is refused
        ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 3200); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == null && AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "OUT OF RANGE",
              "a target beyond 2x control range: strike refused, slot says OUT OF RANGE");
        // inside the 3000 u strike range, outside the 1900 u launch range: the run in is real
        ship.Position = d1.Position + new Vector2(0, 2100); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.5);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(ship.StrikeTarget == d1, "F sends the bombers at the target");
        // THEY TAKE OFF IN TURN, at the fighters' cadence: each rolls out onto the runway and up it to
        // the bow, growing from deck size to flight size -- lifting off, as the hauler does
        {
            var bws = Bombers();
            float liftMin = 9f, liftMax = 0f, bowY = 0f;
            for (int fr = 0; fr < 600 && bws.Any(w => w.LaunchedAt < 0 || w.BomberPhase == "Lifting"); fr++)
            {
                foreach (var w in bws.Where(w => w.BomberPhase == "Lifting"))
                {
                    liftMin = Mathf.Min(liftMin, w.VisualScale); liftMax = Mathf.Max(liftMax, w.VisualScale);
                    bowY = Mathf.Min(bowY, ship.ToLocal(w.Position).Y);
                }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var lorder = bws.Select(w => w.LaunchedAt).Where(t => t >= 0).OrderBy(t => t).ToList();
            double lgap = 99; for (int g = 1; g < lorder.Count; g++) lgap = Math.Min(lgap, lorder[g] - lorder[g - 1]);
            Check(lorder.Count == 2 && lgap >= 0.83 - 1e-9, $"the bombers take off one at a time, at least 0.83 s apart, as the fighters launch ({lgap:0.000} s)");
            Check(liftMin < 0.66f && liftMax > 0.99f && bowY < -104f,
                  $"a bomber lifts off the deck: from 65% to full size, up the runway to its bow end, 110 u ahead ({liftMin:0.00} to {liftMax:0.00}; {-bowY:0} u ahead)");
        }
        Wing crawl = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 2400 && crawl == null; i++) { crawl = Nodes<Wing>().FirstOrDefault(w => w.Carrier == ship && w.Launching); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(crawl != null && crawl.Velocity.Length() > 5f && crawl.Velocity.Dot(d1.Position - crawl.Position) > 0,
              $"bombers keep closing slowly while they launch ({crawl?.Velocity.Length():0} u/s)");
        float launchDist = crawl?.Position.DistanceTo(d1.Position) ?? -1;
        Check(launchDist > 1850 && launchDist <= 1901, $"the bombers close to 1900 u before they launch ({launchDist:0} u)");
        var fspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(Nodes<Wing>().First(w => w.Carrier == ship && !w.IsBomber)).First(); var bspr = Nodes<Sprite2D>(crawl).First();
        Check(Math.Abs(fspr.Texture.GetHeight() * fspr.Scale.Y - 17f) < 0.1f && Math.Abs(bspr.Texture.GetHeight() * bspr.Scale.Y - 28.125f) < 0.1f, "fighters 17 u, bombers 28.1 u (snout-nosed, 25% smaller)");
        Torpedo first = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200 && first == null; i++) { first = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(first != null, "bombers launch torpedoes");
        // measure over an exact frame count: timers round up to the next frame
        var dir0 = first.Dir; var q0 = first.GlobalPosition;
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        float spd = (first.GlobalPosition - q0).Length() / (30f / 60f);
        Check(first.Dir == dir0 && Math.Abs(spd - 300) < 2, $"torpedo runs straight at a steady 300 u/s (no tracking; {spd:0})");
        Check(Smoke(first).Count > 3, "torpedo leaves a smoke trail");
        // the landings begin inside the next 4 s: the deck is watched from here
        float landFar = 0f, landMin = 9f, landMax = 0f, taxiTurn = 0f; bool sawTaxi = false;
        void SampleDeck()
        {
            foreach (var w in Bombers())
            {
                if (w.BomberPhase == "Landing")
                { landFar = Mathf.Max(landFar, ship.ToLocal(w.Position).Length()); landMin = Mathf.Min(landMin, w.VisualScale); landMax = Mathf.Max(landMax, w.VisualScale); }
                if (w.BomberPhase == "Taxiing") { sawTaxi = true; taxiTurn = Mathf.Max(taxiTurn, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(w.Rotation, ship.Rotation))); }
            }
        }
        for (int fr = 0; fr < 240; fr++) { SampleDeck(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
        Check(d1.Total >= 171.875 && Math.Abs(ship.Stats["torpedo_damage"] - 171.875) < 1e-6,
              $"a torpedo lands for 171.875 (137.5 x1.25 by the chips), on top of the fighters' hits ({d1.Total:0})");
        Check(AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line.StartsWith("REARM") || AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line == "RETURNING",
              $"bomber slot shows the return / rearm ({AbilityBar.StateOf(ship, "bombers", H.Selected).Line})");
        // HOME: each comes in over the carrier's centre and settles onto the runway there, shrinking
        // back to deck size and turning to face the bow, then taxis to its bay
        {
            for (int fr = 0; fr < 60 * 15; fr++) { SampleDeck(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(landMax > 0.95f && landMin < 0.7f && landFar <= 10.01f,
                  $"bombers land on the carrier's centre: set down within 10 u of it, shrinking from full size to deck size ({landFar:0.0} u; {landMax:0.00} to {landMin:0.00})");
            Check(sawTaxi && taxiTurn < 0.01f, "then taxi from the centre to their bays, facing the bow");
        }
        Check(ship.BombersReady == 2 && Bombers().All(w => w.Docked), "bombers return to their bays, rearm, and are ready again");
        // bombers land and re-arm even with the carrier at full speed: they must catch its centre,
        // and the landing and the taxi then run in its frame, on a clock
        Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !Bombers().All(w => w.Ammo <= 0 || !w.Docked); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = true });
        float yaw0 = ship.Rotation; bool allHome = false; float topSeen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !allHome; i++)
        {
            allHome = Bombers().All(w => w.Docked); topSeen = Mathf.Max(topSeen, ship.Velocity.Length());
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.W, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.W, Pressed = false });
        Check(allHome && topSeen > 90f && Bombers().All(w => w.RearmLeft > 0 || w.Armed),
              $"with the carrier at full speed ({topSeen:0} u/s), the bombers still land, park and re-arm");
        ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(15.0);
        // the balance's reference: what the carrier's sheet says (fighters + a strike's torpedoes); re-measured
        // here over 40 s -- lumpier (bomber strikes land in lumps), so within 25% -- to catch it drifting
        {
            var md = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(md.Position)); await Wait(0.1);
            await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.2); md.ResetMeter();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space); await Wait(0.05); Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.05);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40; i++)
            {
                ship.Position = md.Position + new Vector2(0, 520); ship.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                if (i % 30 == 0 && ship.BombersReady == (int)ship.Stats["bomber_count"] && ship.StrikeTarget == null) Key(global::Godot.Key.F);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double carrierDps = md.Total / 40.0;
            // (unequipped the sheet gives fighters 3 x 2.5 / 0.35 s and 8 torpedoes of 137.5 a strike; the
            //  five default chips add 25%, so the expected figure with them is about 117 DPS)
            GD.Print($"  carrier measured: {carrierDps:0.00} DPS over 40 s (reference 117 with the chips)");
            Check(Math.Abs(carrierDps / 117.0 - 1) < 0.35, $"the carrier delivers about the 117 DPS its sheet says with the chips ({carrierDps:0.00})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.R); await Wait(12.0);                       // recall the wing for what follows
        }

        // No tracking, tested on single torpedoes so bombers cannot re-aim: moving
        // the target during a whole strike is not a fair test, because a bomber still
        // lining up correctly aims at where the target now is.
        var home = d1.Position;
        var from = home + new Vector2(0, 500);
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await Wait(3.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(d1.Total - 3) < 0.01, "control: a torpedo at a still target hits for 3");
        await Wait(TargetDummy.IdleReset + 0.5); d1.ResetMeter();
        Combat.LaunchTorpedo(from, Vector2.Up, 240f, 900f, 3.0);
        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        d1.Position = home + new Vector2(160, 0);                  // step aside just after launch
        await Wait(4.5);
        GD.Print($"  moved target took {d1.Total:0} damage");
        Check(d1.Total == 0, "a target that moves after launch is missed (the torpedo does not track)");
        d1.Position = home;

        // ── utility ships: hull, loss, and a rebuild for 10% of the category's investment ──
        {
            var Yd = H.Yard;
            var mn = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            var sv = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
            Check(mn.MaxHull == 120 && sv.MaxHull == 120 && Yd.Hauler.MaxHull == 262.5, "miners and salvagers have 120 hull, the hauler 262.5 (150 +75%)");
            Yd.Credits = 100000; double inv0 = Yd.Invested("MINERS"); int lv0 = Yd.Level("miner_hold");
            double price = Economy.Cost(Economy.ById("miner_hold"), lv0);
            Check(Yd.TryBuy("miner_hold") && Math.Abs(Yd.Invested("MINERS") - inv0 - price) < 1e-6, $"buying an upgrade adds its price to its category's investment (+{price:0})");
            double cost = Yd.RebuildCost("MINERS");
            Check(cost > 0 && Math.Abs(cost - Math.Round(Yd.Invested("MINERS") * 0.10)) < 1e-6, $"a rebuild costs 10% of it ({cost:0} of {Yd.Invested("MINERS"):0})");
            mn.TakeDamage(119);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && Math.Abs(mn.Hull - 1) < 1e-6, "119 damage leaves a miner at 1 hull");
            mn.TakeDamage(5); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && !mn.Visible && mn.Cargo == 0 && Math.Abs(mn.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2 && Yd.ArmOf(mn) < 0 && Yd.QueueIndex(mn) < 0,
                  "destroyed: gone, its cargo lost, its arm and queue place given up, rebuilt in 30 s");
            Yd.Credits = cost - 1; mn.RebuildIn = 0.1; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(mn.State == Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.WaitingForCredits, "short of the rebuild cost, it waits");
            Yd.Credits = cost + 500; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(mn.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed && mn.Hull == 120 && mn.Visible && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 500) < 1e-6, "paid for: rebuilt at the base at full hull, exactly 10% charged");
            // the hull upgrades: +10% a level, 125 cr to start (25% above the other +10% rows), x1.25 a level
            var mh = Economy.ById("miner_hull"); var sh2 = Economy.ById("salvager_hull");
            Check(mh != null && sh2 != null && mh.Tab == "MINERS" && sh2.Tab == "SALVAGERS"
                  && Economy.Cost(mh, 0) == 125 && Economy.Cost(mh, 1) == 156 && Economy.Cost(sh2, 0) == 125,
                  "Miner hull and Salvager hull: 125 cr to start, x1.25 a level (156 next)");
            var fullM = Yd.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State != Gatherer.St.Destroyed);
            fullM.Hull = fullM.MaxHull; Yd.Credits = 100000;
            Yd.TryBuy("miner_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(fullM.MaxHull == 132 && fullM.Hull == 132 && sv.MaxHull == 120, "one level: a miner's hull 132 (+10%, kept full); salvagers still 120");
            Yd.TryBuy("salvager_hull"); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(sv.MaxHull == 132, "and the salvagers' own upgrade: 132");
            // A ship that comes off the pad AFTER an upgrade -- a new hub, the trip home, one more
            // bought -- comes at the upgraded hull. It used to start at the base 120 whatever the
            // level, and fly under a damage bar for good.
            var fresh = new Gatherer { Yard = Yd, Kind = GatherKind.Miner, Index = 9, Name = "FreshMiner" };
            Yd.AddChild(fresh); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(fresh.Hull - 132) < 1e-6, $"a miner built after the upgrade comes off the pad at 132 hull ({fresh.Hull:0})");
            Yd.RemoveChild(fresh); fresh.QueueFree();
            double hcost = Yd.RebuildCost("HAULER");
            Yd.Hauler.ResetToPad(); Yd.Hauler.TakeDamage(262.4); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Math.Abs(Yd.Hauler.Hull - 0.1) < 1e-6, $"262.4 damage leaves the hauler standing ({Yd.Hauler.Hull:0.0} hull)");
            Yd.Hauler.TakeDamage(0.2); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Destroyed && Yd.Hauler.WarpedOut && Math.Abs(Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn - 30) < 0.2, "the hauler, destroyed: gone for 30 s");
            Yd.Hauler.RebuildIn = 0.1; Yd.Credits = hcost + 100; await Wait(0.3);
            Check(Yd.Hauler.State == Hauler.St.Loading && Yd.Hauler.Hull == 262.5 && Math.Abs(Yd.Credits - 100) < 1e-6,
                  $"rebuilt on its pad for 10% of the hauler's investment ({hcost:0} cr)");
        }

        // ── networking: silent offline; what friends elsewhere need; one press a second ──
        {
            var nf0 = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "single player: no socket, no web lookup, no router job running");
            var inet = Net.Describe("203.0.113.7", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var shared = Net.Describe("100.72.1.9", true, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var vpn = Net.Describe("198.51.100.4", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var manual = Net.Describe("", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            var none = Net.Describe("", false, "", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015");
            Check(inet.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && inet.address == "203.0.113.7:27015", "router opened the port and is the internet's edge: friends join public:port");
            Check(shared.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly, "the router opened a port on a carrier-grade address: network only");
            Check(vpn.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && vpn.address == "198.51.100.4:27015" && vpn.message.Contains("VPN"),
                  $"the router's public address is not the one the internet sees: a VPN on this PC, named, with the router's address to try ({vpn.reach})");
            // Nothing opened, and the router's internet side is private: TWO forwards, both named.
            var twoHand = Net.Describe("192.168.4.40", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(twoHand.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && twoHand.message.Contains("to this PC (192.168.1.5)") && twoHand.message.Contains("UDP 27015 to 192.168.4.40"),
                  "two routers, neither opened: both forwards, each to its own address");
            Check(Net.IsPublic("81.2.3.4") && !Net.IsPublic("100.72.1.5") && !Net.IsPublic("0.0.0.0") && !Net.IsPublic("169.254.3.4")
                  && !Net.IsPublic("192.0.0.8") && !Net.IsPublic("192.168.4.40") && !Net.IsPublic("::1"),
                  "only a real internet address counts as public (a router reporting 0.0.0.0 is not an address to give friends)");
            // What a player pastes into JOIN.
            Check(Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4") == ("1.2.3.4", 27015) && Net.ParseAddress(" 1.2.3.4 : 27020 ") == ("1.2.3.4", 27020)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("http://1.2.3.4:27020/") == ("1.2.3.4", 27020) && Net.ParseAddress("[2001:db8::1]:27020") == ("2001:db8::1", 27020)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("2001:db8::1") == ("2001:db8::1", 27015) && Net.ParseAddress("game.example.com") == ("game.example.com", 27015)
                  && Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4:99999") == null && Net.ParseAddress("1.2.3.4:abc") == null && Net.ParseAddress("   ") == null,
                  "JOIN reads an address however it is pasted: a port or none, IPv6 bare or in brackets, a name, spaces, a scheme; and refuses an impossible port");
            Check(manual.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && manual.address == "203.0.113.7:27015" && manual.message.Contains("UDP 27015"),
                  "the router will not open it: the public address, and how to forward the port");

            // The branches that were WRONG answers until now, beside the ones that were right.
            // The first is what a player saw when their router HAD opened the port: Describe was
            // handed "mapped" inferred from whether the router named its outside address, so a
            // router that opened the port and said nothing else was reported as refusing, and the
            // player was sent to forward a port that was already forwarded.
            var opened = Net.Describe("", true, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "");
            Check(opened.reach == Net.Reach.Internet && opened.address == "203.0.113.7:27015",
                  $"port open but the router will not name its address: still hosting for the internet ({opened.reach})");
            var cgnat = Net.Describe("100.72.1.5", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(cgnat.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly && !cgnat.message.Contains("UDP 27015"),
                  $"behind carrier-grade NAT: not sent to forward a port that cannot work ({cgnat.reach})");
            var refused = Net.Describe("", false, "203.0.113.7", 27015, "192.168.1.5:27015", "the router refused (ActionFailed)");
            Check(refused.reach == Net.Reach.Manual && refused.message.Contains("ActionFailed") && refused.message.Contains("firewall"),
                  "a refusal says WHAT the router said, and mentions the firewall");
            Check(none.reach == Net.Reach.LanOnly, "nothing learned: network only");
            Check(new[] { inet, shared, vpn, manual, none, twoHand, cgnat }.All(d => !d.message.Contains("203.0.113.7") && !d.message.Contains("198.51.100.4")),
                  "no status line ever prints the public address");
            var sm = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First();
            Unmeet("multiplayer");
            var toggle = Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "NetToggle");
            bool beforeUnfold = Met("multiplayer");
            toggle.ButtonPressed = true; await Wait(0.05); toggle.ButtonPressed = false;
            Check(!beforeUnfold && Met("multiplayer"), "MULTIPLAYER: met when the panel is unfolded");
            var off = Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Offline");
            off.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(sm.Locked && new[] { "Host", "Join", "Offline", "Reconnect" }.All(n => Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == n).Disabled),
                  "one press a second: after a press, host, join, offline and reconnect wait");
            await Wait(SessionMenu.PressGap);
            Check(!sm.Locked && !Nodes<Button>(sm).First(b => b.Name == "Host").Disabled, "and are back after 1 s");
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle, "and offline stays silent");
            // offline, there is nothing to get back in to
            var rcBtn = Nodes<Button>(sm).FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Reconnect");
            Check(rcBtn != null && !rcBtn.Visible && !Net.I.CanReconnect && !Net.I.Reconnecting, "offline, there is no RECONNECT: nothing to get back in to");
            // A JOIN THAT NEVER CONNECTS, from offline: this pilot's world as it was -- the same ship,
            // not a fresh one at the spawn -- and nothing retried (it was never in)
            var ship0 = Me;
            Net.I.Join("no-such-host.invalid");
            await RealTime(() => !Net.I.Connecting, 15);
            Check(!Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(ship0) && Me == ship0 && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect,
                  "a JOIN that fails from offline leaves the pilot's own ship alone, and is not retried");
            // ...and one to an address that never answers (a literal: nothing to look up) is given up on in 12 s
            ulong jl0 = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            Net.I.Join("127.0.0.1:9");
            await RealTime(() => !Net.I.Connecting, 16);
            double joinGaveUp = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - jl0) / 1000.0;
            Check(!Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && Me == ship0 && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect && joinGaveUp > 11.8 && joinGaveUp < 12.6
                  && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Could not reach"),
                  $"a JOIN to an address that never answers is given up on in 12 s, and not retried ({joinGaveUp:0.0} s; {Net.I.LastStatus})");
            // A LINK LOST BEFORE THE HOST LET THIS PILOT IN (Godot calls that "disconnected", not
            // "failed"): an attempt that did not happen -- not a session lost, so nothing is retried,
            // even with a host to go back to
            typeof(Net).GetProperty("LastHost").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { "127.0.0.1:9" });
            Net.I.Join("127.0.0.1:9"); await Wait(0.2);
            bool midHandshake = Net.I.Connecting;
            Priv(Net.I, "OnHostGone"); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(midHandshake && !Net.I.Connecting && !Net.IsOnline && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Could not get in"),
                  $"a link lost mid-handshake is a failed JOIN, not a dropped session: nothing retried ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            typeof(Net).GetProperty("LastHost").GetSetMethod(true).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { "" });
            // A RETURNING PILOT PUT BACK (what a host does with a held place): its hull, where it was,
            // or its stasis with the time it had left
            var backAt = Me.Position; float backRot = Me.Rotation;
            Me.Restore(100, true, 0); bool hullBack = Math.Abs(Me.Hp - 100) < 1e-6;
            Me.Place(new Vector2(-1500, 1500), 1.0f); bool placed = Me.Position == new Vector2(-1500, 1500) && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Me.Rotation, 1f);
            Me.Restore(0, false, 50); bool heldStasis = !Me.Alive && Math.Abs(Me.StasisLeft - 50) < 0.1;
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", nf0).SetValue(Me, 0.0); Me.UseAbility("reboard", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(hullBack && placed && heldStasis && Me.Alive, $"a returning pilot is put back: its hull, its place, or its stasis with the time it had left ({hullBack}, {placed}, {heldStasis})");
            Me.Place(backAt, backRot); Me.Hp = Me.MaxHp;
            // review: a router answer for a session that is already over must never be kept --
            // its port mapping is closed at once (it used to be stored, leaking the forward)
            var nf = nf0;
            int genNow = (int)typeof(Net).GetField("_hostGen", nf).GetValue(Net.I);
            int closed0 = Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed;
            bool staleClosed = false;
            var stale = new Router.Report();
            stale.Opened.Add(new Router.Hop("UPnP", "test", 27015, () => staleClosed = true));
            typeof(Net).GetMethod("RouterResult", nf).Invoke(Net.I, new object[] { genNow - 1, stale });
            await RealTime(() => staleClosed && Net.I.NetworkIdle, 5);
            Check(((System.Collections.ICollection)typeof(Net).GetField("_hops", nf).GetValue(Net.I)).Count == 0 && Net.I.StaleMappingsClosed == closed0 + 1 && staleClosed,
                  "a router answer for a finished session is not kept, and the port it opened is closed");
            Check(Router.IsCarrierGrade("100.100.1.2") && !Router.IsCarrierGrade("100.63.0.1") && !Router.IsCarrierGrade("192.168.1.2"),
                  "carrier-grade / Tailscale addresses (100.64.0.0/10) are recognised");
            await RouterScenarios();

            // ANOTHER PLAYER'S SHIP, as this peer draws it: owned by peer 2, so it only follows what
            // it is told. Three things went wrong over the internet. A warp's landing flash never
            // faded here (it only ever faded on the owner's own screen). With its packets stopped --
            // a friend's connection dying without a goodbye -- it coasted on in a straight line until
            // the timeout. And a held trigger went on firing all that time. A carrier, so the held
            // trigger fires nothing while it is tested.
            var ghost = new PlayerShip { Name = "Ghost" };
            H.AddChild(ghost);
            ghost.Init(2, Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2600));
            ghost.SetIdentity("Ghost", Colors.Gray, Colors.Gray, ShipClass.Carrier);
            var sf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netPos", sf).SetValue(ghost, ghost.Position + new Vector2(900, 0));   // a warp's jump away
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_netVel", sf).SetValue(ghost, new Vector2(100, 0));
            ghost.Trigger = true;
            await Wait(0.1);
            double Flash() => (double)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_warpFlash", sf).GetValue(ghost);
            double flash0 = Flash(); var at0 = ghost.Position;
            await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!ghost.Mine && flash0 > 0 && Flash() == 0, $"another player's warp: the flash where it lands, and then it fades ({flash0:0.00} -> {Flash():0.00})");
            Check(ghost.Position.DistanceTo(at0) < 60f, $"its packets stopped, another player's ship coasts half a second, not on and on ({ghost.Position.DistanceTo(at0):0} u in 2 s)");
            Check(!ghost.Trigger, "and a trigger it was holding lets go");
            ghost.QueueFree();

            // A host-owned thing on a guest's screen (NetPose) is carried forward along its measured
            // velocity -- a quarter second at most -- instead of stopping dead at the last report,
            // where it was a round trip ago. 10 u in 0.1 s is 100 u/s, smoothed to 50 on a first
            // measurement: a second later it stands 50 x 0.25 = 12.5 u past the report, and no further.
            var pose = new NetPose(); var probe = new Node2D();
            pose.Set(Vector2.Zero, 0f); pose.Follow(probe, 0.1f);
            pose.Set(new Vector2(10, 0), 0f);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) pose.Follow(probe, 1f / 60);
            Check(probe.Position.X > 21f && probe.Position.X < 22.6f, $"a raider on a guest's screen runs a quarter second ahead of its last report, no more (x {probe.Position.X:0.0} of 22.5)");
            probe.Free();
        }

        // ── the stat sheet's arithmetic: gear leans hard, so a stack of downsides must shrink a
        //    number, never zero it, turn it negative or divide by zero ──
        {
            var rate = new Stat { Base = 1, Bonus = -1, Inverse = true };
            var dmg = new Stat { Base = 10, Bonus = -1.5 };
            Check(Math.Abs(rate.Value - 10) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(dmg.Value - 1) < 1e-9,
                  $"the stat multiplier never falls below 0.1: -100% rate on a 1 s interval is 10 s, -150% on 10 damage is 1 ({rate.Value}, {dmg.Value})");
            var sum = ShipStats.Sum(new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["a"] = 1 },
                                    new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["a"] = 2, ["b"] = 3 });
            var one = ShipStats.Sum(null, new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, double> { ["b"] = 1 });
            Check(sum.Count == 2 && sum["a"] == 3 && sum["b"] == 3 && one.Count == 1 && one["b"] == 1,
                  "ShipStats.Sum adds shares key by key ({a:1} + {a:2, b:3} = {a:3, b:3})");
        }

        // ── THE GEAR TABLES: 12 parts per slot type, 4 specialisations x 3 rarities ──
        {
            Check(Enum.GetNames(typeof(Rarity)).SequenceEqual(new[] { "Common", "Rare", "Epic" }), "three rarities: Common, Rare, Epic");
            var drops = Equipment.Drops.ToList();
            var bsC = ShipClass.Battleship; var cvC = ShipClass.Carrier; var ddC = ShipClass.Destroyer;
            (GearSlot s, ShipClass? c)[] kinds = { (GearSlot.Weapon, bsC), (GearSlot.Weapon, cvC), (GearSlot.Weapon, ddC),
                                                   (GearSlot.Utility, bsC), (GearSlot.Utility, cvC), (GearSlot.Utility, ddC),
                                                   (GearSlot.Engines, null), (GearSlot.Shield, null), (GearSlot.Hull, null), (GearSlot.Chip, null) };
            bool twelve = kinds.All(k => drops.Count(i => i.Slot == k.s && i.Class == k.c) == 12
                                    && new[] { Rarity.Common, Rarity.Rare, Rarity.Epic }.All(r => drops.Count(i => i.Slot == k.s && i.Class == k.c && i.Rarity == r) == 4));
            var kit = Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Kit).ToList();
            Check(drops.Count == 120 && twelve && kit.Count == 10 && kit.All(i => i.Rarity == Rarity.Common)
                  && kit.Select(i => i.Id).OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal).SequenceEqual(new[] { "basic_deflector", "bs_broadside", "bs_main_battery",
                     "chip_basic", "cv_bomber_bay", "cv_fighter_hangars", "dd_main_battery", "dd_missile_rack", "reinforced_frame", "std_drive" }),
                  $"the gear: 120 drops, 12 for each slot type with 4 at each rarity, and the 10-part kit, all Common ({drops.Count} drops)");
            Check(Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Class == bsC && i.Slot == GearSlot.Utility).All(i => i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).All(k => k.StartsWith("broadside_")))
                  && Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Class != null && i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).Any(k => k.StartsWith("missile_"))).All(i => i.Class == ddC),
                  "every missile part for one class is the destroyer's; the battleship's utility parts are all broadside parts");
            // a sheet with only the named parts on the kit, chips off
            ShipStats Sheet(ShipClass c, string part)
            {
                var l = Equipment.Default(c); for (int k = Equipment.CoreSlots; k < Equipment.Slots; k++) l[k] = "";
                var it = Equipment.ById(part);
                if (it.Slot == GearSlot.Chip) l[Equipment.CoreSlots] = part; else l[Array.IndexOf(Equipment.Core, it.Slot)] = part;
                return new ShipStats(c, Equipment.Bonuses(c, l), Equipment.Adds(c, l));
            }
            bool Near(double a, double b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 1e-6;
            var e1 = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_1"); var e2 = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_2");
            var h1 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_1"); var h2 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_2"); var h3 = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavy_3");
            Check(Near(e1["fighter_speed"], 704) && Near(e1["fighter_damage"], 5.0) && Near(e2["fighter_speed"], 880) && Near(e2["fighter_damage"], 6.25)
                  && Near(h1["main_damage"], 32.22) && Near(h2["main_damage"], 39.38) && Near(h3["main_damage"], 46.54)
                  && new[] { h1, h2, h3 }.All(h => Near(h["main_interval"], 2 / 0.65)),
                  "a rarer copy leans harder, never costs more: Elite I / II speed 704 / 880; Heavy I / II / III shells 10.62 / 12.98 / 15.34, all reloading in 1.54 s");
            var barrage = Sheet(bsC, "bs_barrage_3"); var snap = Sheet(bsC, "bs_snap_2"); var heavyS = Sheet(bsC, "bs_heavyside_3"); var rapidS = Sheet(bsC, "bs_rapidside_1");
            Check(Near(barrage["broadside_volleys"], 6) && Near(barrage["broadside_windup"], 0.5 / 0.7) && Near(barrage["broadside_cooldown"], 17.5)
                  && Near(snap["broadside_volleys"], 2) && Near(snap["broadside_windup"], 0.2)
                  && Near(heavyS["broadside_mult"], 1.8) && Near(heavyS["broadside_cooldown"], 14 / 0.75)
                  && Near(rapidS["broadside_mult"], 0.75) && Near(rapidS["broadside_cooldown"], 14 / 1.5),
                  "the broadside's gear: Barrage III 6 volleys (0.71 s wind-up, 17.5 s cooldown); Snap II 2 volleys in a 0.2 s wind-up; Heavy III x1.8 shells (18.7 s); Rapid I back in 9.3 s at x0.75");
            var ddHeavy = Sheet(ddC, "dd_heavy_3"); var ddSalvo = Sheet(ddC, "dd_salvo_3");
            Check(Near(ddHeavy["main_damage"], 19.5) && Near(ddHeavy["main_count"], 2) && Near(ddSalvo["missile_mag"], 6) && Near(ddSalvo["missile_reload"], 12.0),
                  "the destroyer's own gear: Heavy Twins III shells 19.5 on its two guns; Salvo Rack III 6 bursts on a 12.0 s reload");
            var elite = Sheet(cvC, "cv_elite_3"); var swarm = Sheet(cvC, "cv_swarm_3"); var line = Sheet(cvC, "cv_line_3");
            Check(Near(elite["fighter_count"], 2) && Near(elite["fighter_speed"], 1056) && Near(elite["fighter_damage"], 7.5) && Near(elite["fighter_turn"], 5.25),
                  $"Elite Hangars III, the owner's example: 2 fighters, +200% speed and damage, +50% turning ({elite["fighter_count"]}, {elite["fighter_speed"]}, {elite["fighter_damage"]}, {elite["fighter_turn"]})");
            Check(Near(swarm["fighter_count"], 9) && Near(swarm["fighter_speed"], 274.56) && Near(swarm["fighter_damage"], 1.95),
                  $"Swarm Hangars III, the owner's example: 9 fighters, 22% slower and weaker ({swarm["fighter_count"]}, {swarm["fighter_speed"]}, {swarm["fighter_damage"]})");
            Check(Near(line["fighter_count"], 4) && Near(line["fighter_damage"], 3.5) && Near(line["fighter_speed"], 422.4),
                  $"Line Hangars III, between them: 4 fighters, +40% damage, +20% speed ({line["fighter_count"]}, {line["fighter_damage"]}, {line["fighter_speed"]})");
            // THE WORST CASE: the most negative share any fitting part gives each stat, in every core
            // slot at once and five times over in the chips, must leave the number above zero
            bool allPositive = true;
            double Worst(ShipClass c, string stat)
            {
                double w = 0;
                foreach (var slot in Equipment.Core)
                    w += Math.Min(0, Equipment.All.Where(i => Equipment.Fits(i, slot, c)).Select(i => i.Pct.GetValueOrDefault(stat)).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Min());
                return w + Equipment.ChipSlots * Math.Min(0, Equipment.All.Where(i => i.Slot == GearSlot.Chip).Select(i => i.Pct.GetValueOrDefault(stat)).DefaultIfEmpty(0).Min());
            }
            foreach (var c in Enum.GetValues<ShipClass>()) foreach (var st in new ShipStats(c).All) if (Worst(c, st.Id) <= -1) allPositive = false;
            Check(allPositive && Near(Worst(cvC, "fighter_damage"), -0.47) && Near(Worst(bsC, "main_damage"), -0.55) && Near(Worst(bsC, "hull"), -0.50) && Near(Worst(cvC, "hull"), -0.50),
                  $"no stack of gear takes a number to zero: worst fighter damage -47% (1.325), shell -55% (8.055), hull -50% (150 / 100) ({Worst(cvC, "fighter_damage"):0.00}, {Worst(bsC, "main_damage"):0.00}, {Worst(bsC, "hull"):0.00})");
            // every stat a part names is real on a class it fits, and none touches the turret counts
            string[] addable = { "fighter_count", "bomber_count", "missile_mag", "bomber_ammo", "broadside_volleys" };
            var unknown = Equipment.All.SelectMany(i => i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).Where(k =>
                              !(i.Class is ShipClass only ? new[] { only } : Enum.GetValues<ShipClass>()).Any(c => new ShipStats(c).All.Any(s => s.Id == k)))
                              .Select(k => i.Id + ":" + k)).ToList();
            bool noCounts = Equipment.All.All(i => !i.Pct.Keys.Concat(i.Add.Keys).Any(k => k is "main_count" or "pd_count" or "strike_range"));
            bool addsOk = Equipment.All.All(i => i.Add.Keys.All(addable.Contains));
            Check(unknown.Count == 0 && noCounts && addsOk,
                  $"every stat a part names exists on a class it fits; no part changes a turret count; whole additions only to wings, magazines and volleys ({string.Join(",", unknown)})");
            // a part for the other class changes nothing, even named in the loadout
            var wrong = Equipment.Default(bsC); wrong[0] = "cv_elite_3";
            var kitB = Equipment.Bonuses(bsC, Equipment.Default(bsC)); var wrongB = Equipment.Bonuses(bsC, wrong);
            Check(wrongB.Count == kitB.Count && wrongB.All(kv => kitB.TryGetValue(kv.Key, out var v) && Math.Abs(v - kv.Value) < 1e-12) && Equipment.Adds(bsC, wrong).Count == 0,
                  "a carrier's hangar named on a battleship changes nothing on it: the sheet is the kit's");
            var words = Equipment.Describe(Equipment.ById("chip_basic"), cvC).ToList();
            Check(words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Fighters")) && words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Hull")) && !words.Any(w => w.StartsWith("Main guns")),
                  $"a part's effects are named for the ship it is on ({string.Join(" | ", words)})");
        }

        // ── LOOT's rules: crates by boss level, rarity unlocking every 5 levels, 70% own class ──
        {
            Check(new[] { 1, 5, 6, 10, 11, 20 }.Select(Loot.CratesFor).SequenceEqual(new[] { 2, 2, 3, 3, 4, 4 }),
                  "crates per pilot: 2, then 3 from boss level 6, 4 from level 11");
            (int c, int r, int e) Bands(int level)
            {
                var rng = new Random(12345); int c = 0, r = 0, e = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
                    foreach (var id in Loot.RollParty(new[] { (1, ShipClass.Battleship) }, level, rng)[1])
                        switch (Equipment.ById(id).Rarity) { case Rarity.Common: c++; break; case Rarity.Rare: r++; break; default: e++; break; }
                return (c, r, e);
            }
            var b5 = Bands(5); var b6 = Bands(6); var b10 = Bands(10); var b11 = Bands(11);
            double Share(int n, (int c, int r, int e) b) => n / (double)(b.c + b.r + b.e);
            Check(b5.r == 0 && b5.e == 0 && Math.Abs(Share(b6.r, b6) - 0.30) < 0.03 && b6.e == 0 && b10.e == 0 && Math.Abs(Share(b10.r, b10) - 0.30) < 0.03
                  && Math.Abs(Share(b11.c, b11) - 0.55) < 0.03 && Math.Abs(Share(b11.r, b11) - 0.30) < 0.03 && Math.Abs(Share(b11.e, b11) - 0.15) < 0.03,
                  $"rarity unlocks every 5 boss levels: L5 all Common; L6 30% Rare; L11 55 / 30 / 15 ({Share(b6.r, b6):0.00}; {Share(b11.c, b11):0.00} / {Share(b11.r, b11):0.00} / {Share(b11.e, b11):0.00})");
            // both pilots measured: each is the other's mirror, so a roll made for the wrong class shows on one of them
            var prng = new Random(777); int kit = 0;
            var share = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<int, (int n, int bs, int cv)> { [1] = (0, 0, 0), [7] = (0, 0, 0) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 2000; i++)
            {
                var roll = Loot.RollParty(new[] { (1, ShipClass.Battleship), (7, ShipClass.Carrier) }, 1, prng);
                foreach (var peer in new[] { 1, 7 })
                    foreach (var id in roll[peer])
                    {
                        var it = Equipment.ById(id); var (n, b, c) = share[peer];
                        share[peer] = (n + 1, b + (it.Class == ShipClass.Battleship ? 1 : 0), c + (it.Class == ShipClass.Carrier ? 1 : 0));
                        if (it.Kit) kit++;
                    }
            }
            double Of(int peer, int k) => k / (double)share[peer].n;
            // own roll (70%): its class's 8 parts of 24; any roll (30%): each class's 8 of 40 -- so its own
            // class 0.7 x 8/24 + 0.3 x 8/40 = 29.3%, another class's 0.3 x 8/40 = 6%
            Check(kit == 0 && Math.Abs(Of(7, share[7].bs) - 0.06) < 0.012 && Math.Abs(Of(7, share[7].cv) - 0.2933) < 0.025
                  && Math.Abs(Of(1, share[1].cv) - 0.06) < 0.012 && Math.Abs(Of(1, share[1].bs) - 0.2933) < 0.025,
                  $"each pilot's own roll: 70% its own class or general, 30% anything -- another class's parts 6%, its own 29% (carrier pilot {Of(7, share[7].bs):0.000} / {Of(7, share[7].cv):0.000}, battleship pilot {Of(1, share[1].cv):0.000} / {Of(1, share[1].bs):0.000})");
        }

        // ── equipment (I): per-class gear; common defaults and five chips (+5% damage, +5% hull each) ──
        {
            Check(Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] == "cv_fighter_hangars" && Equipment.ById("cv_fighter_hangars").Name == "Mk I Fighter Hangars"
                  && Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship).Take(5).All(id => Equipment.ById(id).Rarity == Rarity.Common),
                  "the defaults: common parts in five slots; a carrier's weapon slot holds its Fighter Hangars");
            var bsE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Battleship, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Battleship)));
            var cvE = new ShipStats(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Bonuses(ShipClass.Carrier, Equipment.Default(ShipClass.Carrier)));
            Check(Math.Abs(bsE["hull"] - 375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(bsE["main_damage"] - 22.375) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["hull"] - 250) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(cvE["fighter_damage"] - 3.125) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(cvE["torpedo_damage"] - 171.875) < 1e-6,
                  "five default chips: +25% damage and hull (battleship 375 hull, 22.375 a shell; carrier 250 hull, fighters 3.125, torpedoes 171.875)");
            var cls0 = Me.Class;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(ew != null && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Slot_")) == 5
                  && Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew).Count(r => r.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Chip_")) == 5, "I opens the equipment: five parts and five chips");
            double full = Me.MaxHp;
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Unequip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 1, $"a chip off: 5% less hull ({full:0} -> {Me.MaxHp:0}), the chip in the hold");
            var other = cls0 == ShipClass.Battleship ? ShipClass.Carrier : ShipClass.Battleship;
            Me.SetClass(other); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 5, "the other class keeps its own gear: five chips");
            Me.SetClass(cls0); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Skip(5).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 4 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 1 && Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full * 1.20 / 1.25) < 1e-6,
                  "back on the first class: its gear as it was left (four chips, one in the hold)");
            ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (ew == null) { Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2); ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First(); }
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Equip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Math.Abs(Me.MaxHp - full) < 1e-6 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 0, "the chip back on from the hold: full hull again");
            // THE HOLD IS THE PILOT'S: a chip taken off one class goes on the other. The pilot really
            // changes class (Character.Class, as the refit does), and the window is opened afresh.
            Nodes<Button>(ew).First(b => b.Name == "Unequip").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            await SetClass(other);
            Character.LoadoutFor(other)[Equipment.Slots - 1] = "";
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew2 = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            var holdRow = ew2 == null ? null : Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew2).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_chip_basic");
            holdRow?.GetChildren().OfType<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Equip")?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(Equipment.CoreSlots).Count(x => x == "chip_basic") == 5 && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("chip_basic") == 0,
                  $"the hold is the pilot's: a chip taken off the {cls0} goes on the {other} ({Character.LoadoutFor(other).Skip(Equipment.CoreSlots).Count(x => x == "chip_basic")} chips)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            await SetClass(cls0);
            Character.LoadoutFor(cls0)[Array.IndexOf(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0), "", Equipment.CoreSlots)] = "chip_basic";
            H.PilotChanged(); Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            ew = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First();
            Character.Save(); var saved = (string[])Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).Clone();
            string eqId = Character.Id; Character.Loadout.Clear(); Character.Load(eqId);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(cls0).SequenceEqual(saved), "the gear is saved with the pilot");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().Count() == 0, "Esc closes it");

            // A DROP IN THE HOLD: a core part fits its slot and the part it replaces goes into the
            // hold; a part for the other class is listed but cannot be fitted here
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
            Character.GearHold["cv_line_1"] = 1; Character.GearHold["bs_rapid_1"] = 1;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.2);
            var ew3 = Nodes<EquipmentWindow>().First();
            var otherRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew3).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_bs_rapid_1");
            Check(otherRow != null && !Nodes<Button>(otherRow).Any(), "a battleship part in the carrier's hold is listed, with nothing to fit it by");
            var lineRow = Nodes<HBoxContainer>(ew3).FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == "Hold_cv_line_1");
            lineRow?.GetChildren().OfType<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "Fit")?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            await Wait(0.2);
            Check(Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] == "cv_line_1" && Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("cv_fighter_hangars") == 1
                  && !Character.GearHold.ContainsKey("cv_line_1") && Me.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 4,
                  $"a hangar from the hold fits the weapon slot, the kit's goes into the hold, and the carrier flies 4 fighters ({Me.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter)})");
            // THE K WINDOW shows what gear adds: a Swarm III's six extra fighters, and its downside in red
            Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier)[0] = "cv_swarm_3"; H.PilotChanged();
            Key(global::Godot.Key.I); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.2);
            var kw = Nodes<StatsWindow>().First(); kw.ShowPane(false); await Wait(0.2);
            var grid = (GridContainer)typeof(StatsWindow).GetField("_grid", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(kw);
            var cells = grid.GetChildren().OfType<Label>().ToList();
            int fh = cells.FindIndex(c => c.Text == "FIGHTERS");
            int craftRow = fh < 0 ? -1 : cells.FindIndex(fh, c => c.Text == "  Craft");
            int speed = fh < 0 ? -1 : cells.FindIndex(fh, c => c.Text == "  Top speed");
            var bonusCell = speed < 0 ? null : cells[speed + 3];
            bool redDown = bonusCell != null && bonusCell.Text == "−22%" && bonusCell.GetThemeColor("font_color") == Ui.Bad;
            bool added = craftRow >= 0 && cells[craftRow + 1].Text == "3" && cells[craftRow + 2].Text == "+6" && cells[craftRow + 4].Text == "9";
            bool rule = Nodes<Label>(kw).Any(l => l.Text.Contains("(base + added)"));
            Check(grid.Columns == 5 && added && redDown && rule,
                  $"K shows base, added, bonus and final: Swarm III's craft 3 +6 = 9, its top speed -22% in red (added {added}, red {redDown}, rule {rule})");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
            var cl0 = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Carrier); cl0[0] = "cv_fighter_hangars";
            Character.GearHold.Remove("cv_fighter_hangars"); Character.GearHold.Remove("bs_rapid_1");
            H.PilotChanged(); await SetClass(cls0);
        }

        // ── pilot progression: EXP, levels, points, flat upgrades ──
        {
            Check(Progression.Cost(0) == 1 && Progression.Cost(1) == 2 && Progression.Cost(2) == 3, "upgrade costs 1, 2, 3, ... points");
            Check(Progression.ExpToNext == 1000 && Progression.ExpToNext == 1000, "every level needs 1000 EXP");
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought);
            Unmeet("pilot");
            int up = Progression.AddExp(2600);
            Check(Met("pilot"), "A LEVEL UP: met on the first level gained");
            Check(up == 2 && Character.Level == 3 && Character.Exp == 600 && Character.Points == 2, "2600 EXP from level 1: level 3, 600 over, 2 points");
            // the approved formula, in literal numbers (so the checks can fail)
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(1) - 1) < 1e-12 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(5) - 1.4641) < 1e-9, "boss levels start at 1: S(L) = 1.1^(L-1) (level 5: x1.4641)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.HullMult(1, 3) - 2.2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.DamageMult(1, 3) - 1.4) < 1e-9, "a party of 3: boss hull x2.2, damage x1.4");
            Check(Missions.KillExpFor(5, 10) == 100 && Missions.KillExpFor(2, 1) == 400 && Missions.KillExpFor(1, 1) == 200, "kill EXP 200 x boss level / pilot level (5 vs 10: 100; 2 vs 1: 400)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 1) - 2000) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 2) - 1500) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.BountyEach(1, 3) - 4000.0 / 3) < 1e-9,
                  "the bounty, +50% per extra pilot, split: 2000 solo, 1500 each for two, 1333 each for three");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            int k1 = Progression.AwardBossKill(1), k2 = Progression.AwardBossKill(1);
            Check(k1 == 550 && k2 == 300, $"a level-1 boss: 200 + 250 first clear + 100 = 550; again: 300 ({k1}, {k2})");
            // ONE LADDER: a level counts as cleared whichever known boss held it -- the first-clear bonus is
            // paid once a level, and the next level opens; a clear under an id this build does not know
            // counts for nothing
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            Character.BossCleared["ironhold"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2 };
            bool unknownIgnored = !Missions.Cleared(1) && Missions.HighestBeaten == 0 && Missions.Unlocked == 1;
            Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1 };
            int again = Progression.AwardBossKill(1);
            Check(unknownIgnored && Missions.Cleared(1) && !Missions.Cleared(2) && Missions.Unlocked == 2 && again == 300
                  && Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].SetEquals(new[] { 1 }) && Missions.ForLevel(1).Id == "silver_lancer",
                  $"one ladder: a level cleared under a known boss counts, and pays no second first-clear bonus ({again}); an unknown boss's clears count for nothing");
            // ...WHICHEVER BOSS HELD IT: the bosses take the levels in turn, and a level cleared under the
            // other one counts all the same -- recorded under the Drake, level 1 is no first clear for the Lancer
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1;
            Character.BossCleared["drake_bastion"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1 };
            int crossed = Progression.AwardBossKill(1);
            Check(Missions.ForLevel(1).Id == "silver_lancer" && Missions.ForLevel(2).Id == "drake_bastion" && Missions.ForLevel(3).Id == "silver_lancer"
                  && crossed == 300 && Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"].Contains(1),
                  $"the bosses take the levels in turn (Lancer, Drake, Lancer), and a level cleared under either is cleared: no second first-clear bonus ({crossed})");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 3;
            Check(!Nodes<BossBar>().First().Visible, "outside the arena there is no boss bar");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            var pw = Nodes<PilotWindow>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(pw != null, "L opens the PILOT window");
            Button PBuy(string id) => Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(pw).First(r => r.Name == "Pilot_" + id)).First(b => b.Name == "Buy");
            var me = Me; me.Hp = me.MaxHp; double hull0 = me.MaxHp, hp0 = me.Hp;
            Character.Points = 3;
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            // (points are added BEFORE percentage bonuses: the five default chips' +25% makes +5 into +6.25)
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 1 && Character.Points == 2 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(me.Hp - (hp0 + 6.25)) < 1e-6,
                  "Hull (1 pt): +5 hull (x1.25 by the default chips: +6.25) on the ship at once");
            PBuy("hull").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Character.Bought[1] == 2 && Character.Points == 0 && Math.Abs(me.MaxHp - (hull0 + 12.5)) < 1e-6, "Hull again (2 pts): +10 in all (+12.5 with the chips)");
            Check(PBuy("speed").Disabled, "with no points left, BUY is disabled");
            Character.Points = 6;
            PBuy("damage").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("speed").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); PBuy("turn").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            var baseSheet = new ShipStats(me.Class, Equipment.Bonuses(me.Class, me.Loadout));            // the same gear, no points
            Check(Math.Abs(me.Stats[Progression.DamageStat(me.Class)] - baseSheet[Progression.DamageStat(me.Class)] - 1.25) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["max_speed"] - baseSheet["max_speed"] - 1) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(me.Stats["turn_rate"] - baseSheet["turn_rate"] - Mathf.DegToRad(1f)) < 1e-6,
                  "Weapons +1 damage (x1.25 by the chips) on the main weapon, Engines +1 speed, Rudder +1 degree/s");
            string cid = Character.Id; int[] saved = (int[])Character.Bought.Clone();
            Character.Save(); System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Load(cid);
            Check(Character.Bought.SequenceEqual(saved) && Character.Level == 3, "purchases and level are saved with the character");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.L); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<PilotWindow>() == 0, "L closes it");
            // leave the pilot as found for what follows
            System.Array.Clear(Character.Bought); Character.Points = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.Exp = 0; Character.Save();
            Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity");
        }

        // ── damage rules: one hit per source per 0.35 s; regeneration; 12 s out of combat ──
        {
            var me = Me; double hp0 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a"); me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 5) < 0.2, "the same source lands only once inside 0.35 s");
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:b");
            Check(Math.Abs(hp0 - me.Hp - 10) < 0.3, "a different source lands at once");
            await Wait(0.55); double hp1 = me.Hp;
            me.Hit(5, me.Position + Vector2.Up * 100, "test:a");
            Check(hp1 - me.Hp > 4.5, "after 0.52 s the first source lands again");
            Check(PlayerShip.CombatHold == 12, "out of combat means 12 s without dealing or taking damage");
            me.TakeDamage(me.Hp - me.MaxHp * 0.5); me.NoteCombat(); double h0 = me.Hp;
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp - 0.005) < 0.0015, $"in combat it regenerates 0.5% of max hull a second ({(me.Hp - h0) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_combatT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(me, 0.0);
            double h1 = me.Hp; await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Math.Abs((me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp - 0.03) < 0.004, $"out of combat, 3% a second ({(me.Hp - h1) / me.MaxHp * 100:0.00}%)");
            me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6a): the light fighter -- cruise, boost, post, pin, laser ──
        {
            var me = Me; var keepPos = me.Position;
            me.Position = new Vector2(4000, 2500); me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var r1 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(0, 2300));
            await Wait(0.5); var rp0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float cruise = (r1.Position - rp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r1.Target == me && Math.Abs(cruise - 100f) < 8f && !r1.Boosting, $"a light raider goes for the nearest ship at an unupgraded capital ship's pace ({cruise:0} u/s)");
            Check(Math.Abs(Raider.BoostAt - 1600f) < 1e-3, "it boosts from 1600 u (3 s at 500%, plus its 100 u reach)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && !r1.Boosting; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float atBoost = r1.Position.DistanceTo(me.Position); var b0 = r1.Position; await Wait(0.4);
            float rboost = (r1.Position - b0).Length() / 0.4f;
            Check(Math.Abs(atBoost - 1600) < 30 && Math.Abs(rboost - 500) < 30, $"at 1600 u ({atBoost:0}) it boosts to 500% ({rboost:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !r1.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float rheld = Raider.Gap(r1.Position, me);                 // from the HULL: beside the ship, never on it
            Check(r1.Latched && rheld > 60 && rheld <= Raider.PinRange, $"it lands near its target, not on top: posted {rheld:0} u off the hull");
            var r2 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(300, 0)); var r3 = H.SpawnRaider(me.Position + new Vector2(-300, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && !(r2.Latched && r3.Latched); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var bow = Vector2.Up.Rotated(me.Rotation);
            var angs = new[] { r1, r2, r3 }.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bow.AngleTo(r.Position - me.Position))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
            Check(r2.Latched && r3.Latched && Math.Abs(angs[0] + 90) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[1]) < 20 && Math.Abs(angs[2] - 90) < 20,
                  $"three hold posts ahead, left and right ({string.Join(", ", angs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))} deg off the bow)");
            Check(me.Pinned, "rheld there, they pin it");
            float rot0 = me.Rotation;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = true });
            await Wait(1.0);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventKey { Keycode = global::Godot.Key.A, PhysicalKeycode = global::Godot.Key.A, Pressed = false });
            float rspd = me.Velocity.Length(), rtop = (float)me.Stats["max_speed"];
            Check(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(rot0, me.Rotation)) < 0.01f && rspd <= rtop * 0.2f + 1f && me.SpeedAhead > 5f,
                  $"pinned: A does not turn it, and forced thrust holds it to 20% of top speed ({rspd:0} of {rtop:0} u/s)");
            double Raided() => me.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key.StartsWith("raider:")).Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double dm0 = Raided(); await Wait(5.0);
            double dps = (Raided() - dm0) / 5.0;
            Check(Math.Abs(dps - 3.0) < 0.7, $"three lights, 1 DPS each ({dps:0.00} DPS)");
            foreach (var r in new[] { r1, r2, r3 }) r.TakeDamage(999);
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0 && !Combat.Hostiles.OfType<Raider>().Any() && !me.Pinned, "shot down, they are gone -- and the pin lets go");
            // A miner OUT IN THE BELT, beyond the base's own guns (600 u): one caught docking had its
            // raider shot down by them before it could latch. Where the fleet is at this point depends
            // on how long the router scenarios above took in real time, so it is waited for.
            Gatherer mnr = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 40 && mnr == null; i++)
            {
                mnr = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner && g.State == Gatherer.St.Working && g.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) > 900);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            mnr ??= H.Yard.Gatherers.First(g => g.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            // an EMPTY hold, so it goes on working (still) through the whole check: one picked nearly
            // full left for home at its full 110 u/s as the raider arrived, and a raider cruising at
            // 100 with its boost spent never caught it to latch
            mnr.Cargo = 0;
            var r4 = H.SpawnRaider(mnr.Position + new Vector2(120, 0));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !r4.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float mr0 = mnr.Rotation; var mp0 = mnr.Position; await Wait(0.5);
            float rmspd = (mnr.Position - mp0).Length() / 0.5f;
            Check(r4.Target == mnr && mnr.Pinned && Math.Abs(rmspd - (float)mnr.Speed * 0.2f) < 3f && Math.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation)) < 0.01f,
                  $"a miner is pinned the same way: forward at 20%, no turning ({rmspd:0} u/s of {mnr.Speed:0}; target {(r4.Target as Node)?.Name}, pinned {mnr.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(mr0, mnr.Rotation):0.000})");
            r4.TakeDamage(999); await Wait(0.3);
            me.Position = keepPos; me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me.TakeDamage(-(me.MaxHp - me.Hp));   // hand over at full hull
        }

        // ── raiders (6b): the heavy -- waits at the map's edge; a predicted missile; boosts at 700% on a pinned target ──
        {
            var hm = Me; var keepH = hm.Position;
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2000, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            var hv = H.SpawnRaider(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 500), Enemies.Gunship);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hv.Heavy && Math.Abs(hv.Length - 4 * Raider.LightLength) < 1e-3 && Math.Abs(Raider.HeavyDps - 2 * Raider.RaiderDps) < 1e-9 && Raider.RaiderDps == 1,
                  "a heavy raider: four times a light's length, twice the raider damage (2 DPS)");
            double HvLaser() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"raider:{hv.NetId}").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            double HvMissile() => hm.DamageBySource.Where(kv => kv.Key == $"heavy:{hv.NetId}:missile").Sum(kv => kv.Value);
            var edge = Raider.EdgeSpot(hm.Position);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 22 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) > 8f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.5);
            float hvFacing = Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(hv.Rotation, (hm.Position - hv.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f)));
            Check(hv.Position.DistanceTo(edge) < 10f && Math.Abs(hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - 4200f) < 10f && hvFacing < 15f && HvLaser() == 0,
                  $"its target unpinned, it waits at the map's edge nearest it ({hv.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos):0} u from the base), facing it, laser silent");
            // the missile: within 500 u of the heavy, at where the target will be in 7 s
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 420f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var blastRing = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 1 && blastRing != null && blastRing.A.DistanceTo(hm.Position) < 6f && Math.Abs(blastRing.Duration - 7) < 1e-6,
                  "within 500 u it fires at where the target will be; a red circle marks it for 7 s");
            double mm0 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(HvMissile() - mm0 - Raider.MissileDamage) < 1e-6, $"staying on the circle, the blast lands ({HvMissile() - mm0:0})");
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 && H.BlastsPending == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 300); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;                         // off the circle
            double mm1 = HvMissile();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && H.BlastsPending > 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.BlastsPending == 0 && HvMissile() == mm1, "moving off the circle dodges it");
            // anything out of place -- a wild speed, a broken number -- and it aims right at the target
            var pz = new Vector2(100, 200);
            Check(Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(10, 0)) == pz + new Vector2(70, 0) && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(5000, 0)) == pz
                  && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.NaN, 0)) == pz && Raider.PredictSpot(pz, new Vector2(float.PositiveInfinity, 1)) == pz,
                  "the missile leads a sane speed 7 s; a wild speed, NaN or infinity: it aims at where the target is");
            // live: 2700 u/s in 45 u steps (wild, yet under the 50 u jump filter) -- the circle must sit on the ship
            hm.Position = edge + (Hub.BasePos - edge).Normalized() * 400f; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            typeof(Raider).GetField("_missileCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hv, 0.12);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            int rings0 = Nodes<Telegraph>().Count(t => !t.Line);
            Vector2 atFire = hm.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 20 && Nodes<Telegraph>().Count(t => !t.Line) == rings0; i++)
            {
                atFire = hm.Position; hm.Position += new Vector2(0, 45);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var wildRing = Nodes<Telegraph>().LastOrDefault(t => !t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Radius - Raider.BlastRadius) < 0.1f);
            Check(wildRing != null && wildRing.A.DistanceTo(atFire) < 100f,
                  $"a target moving at a wild 2700 u/s: the circle sits on it, not 7 s ahead ({(wildRing == null ? -1 : wildRing.A.DistanceTo(atFire)):0} u off)");
            // pinned: it boosts at 700% until 300 u away, then closes at cruise and fires 2 DPS
            hm.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(1900, 0); hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.Rotation = 0f;
            var lights = new[] { H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(0, -250)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(250, 0)), H.SpawnRaider(hm.Position + new Vector2(-250, 0)) };
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hm.Pinned; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(0.1); var hp1 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float burn = (hv.Position - hp1).Length() / 0.2f;
            // the spec's numbers as literals: a check that reads the code's own constant cannot catch it being wrong
            Check(hm.Pinned && Math.Abs(burn - 700f) < 60f, $"its target pinned, it boosts at 700% ({burn:0} u/s)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && hv.Position.DistanceTo(hm.Position) > 300f - 20f; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var hp2 = hv.Position; await Wait(0.2);
            float slow = (hv.Position - hp2).Length() / 0.2f;
            // (over the ground: cruise plus the pinned target's own forced thrust, ~20 u/s)
            Check(slow <= 100f + 0.2f * (float)hm.Stats["max_speed"] + 5f, $"inside 300 u it closes at cruise ({slow:0} u/s over the ground)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !hv.Latched; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double hl0 = HvLaser(); await Wait(3.0);
            double hdps = (HvLaser() - hl0) / 3.0;
            Check(hv.Latched && Raider.Gap(hv.Position, hm) <= Raider.HeavyReach && Math.Abs(hdps - 2) < 0.4,
                  $"from 135 u off the hull its laser does 2 DPS ({hdps:0.00})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.ToList()) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            hm.Position = keepH; hm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; hm.TakeDamage(-(hm.MaxHp - hm.Hp));
        }

        // ── raiders (6c): patrols -- 3 lights and 1 heavy; nothing within 2000 u, no target ──
        {
            int pid = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 6500));
            var pat = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == pid).ToList();
            Check(pat.Count == 4 && pat.Count(r => !r.Heavy) == 3 && pat.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 1, "a patrol: three lights and one heavy");
            await Wait(1.0);
            float pd0 = pat[0].Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos);
