            Check(pat.All(r => r.Target == null) && pd0 < 6500 - 60 && pd0 > 6500 - 200,
                  $"nothing within 2000 u: they take no target, and head for the perimeter at a patrol's pace ({6500 - pd0:0} u in 1 s)");
            var pm = Me; var keepP = pm.Position;
            pm.Position = pat[0].Position + new Vector2(0, -1500); pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var heavyP = pat.First(r => r.Heavy);
            Check(pat.Where(r => !r.Heavy).All(r => r.Target == pm) && heavyP.Target == pm,
                  "a ship within 2000 u: the lights go for it, and their heavy takes the same target");
            foreach (var r in pat) if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(r)) r.TakeDamage(9999);
            await Wait(0.3);
            pm.Position = keepP; pm.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; pm.TakeDamage(-(pm.MaxHp - pm.Hp));
        }

        // ── the base's own weapons: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u, a tracking 25-damage missile at 600 u -- raiders only ──
        {
            var bd = Nodes<BaseDefense>().First();
            Check(BaseDefense.LaserRange == 300 && BaseDefense.MissileRange == 600 && BaseDefense.LaserDps == 5 && BaseDefense.MissileDamage == 25
                  && Math.Abs(BaseDefense.MissileSpeed - 1.2f * 100f) < 1e-3, "the base: a 5 DPS laser at 300 u; a 25-damage missile at 600 u, 120 u/s");
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"); var dHome = dd.Position;
            dd.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 250); dd.ResetMeter();
            double l0 = bd.LaserDealt; int m0 = bd.MissilesFired; await Wait(2.0);
            Check(bd.LaserDealt == l0 && bd.MissilesFired == m0 && dd.Total == 0, "it never fires at the practice dummies");
            dd.Position = dHome;
            var spot1 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 280);
            var rb = H.SpawnRaider(spot1); rb.Hp = 1000;
            double lz = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 120; i++) { rb.Position = spot1; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            double laser2 = bd.LaserDealt - lz;
            Check(Math.Abs(laser2 - 10) < 2.6, $"a raider inside 300 u: the laser burns it at 5 DPS ({laser2:0.0} in 2 s)");
            rb.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot2 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -500);        // north of the base: clear of the hauler pad's own gun
            var rc = H.SpawnRaider(spot2); rc.Hp = 1000;
            int mz = bd.MissilesFired; double lz2 = bd.LaserDealt;
            Torpedo bm = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && bm == null; i++)
            {
                rc.Position = spot2;
                bm = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.HostileFire && !t.Heavy && t.TargetId == rc.NetId);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var bp1 = bm?.GlobalPosition ?? Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            float bspd = bm != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(bm) ? (bm.GlobalPosition - bp1).Length() / 0.25f : 0f;
            double hpc = rc.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rc.Hp == hpc; i++) { rc.Position = spot2; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bm != null && bd.MissilesFired > mz && bd.LaserDealt == lz2 && Math.Abs(bspd - 120f) < 6f && Math.Abs(hpc - rc.Hp - 25) < 1e-6,
                  $"a raider at 500 u: no laser, but a tracking missile at {bspd:0} u/s lands for {hpc - rc.Hp:0}");
            rc.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            var spot3 = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -700);
            var rd = H.SpawnRaider(spot3); rd.Hp = 1000;
            int mw = bd.MissilesFired; double lw = bd.LaserDealt;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6; i++) { rd.Position = spot3; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(bd.MissilesFired == mw && bd.LaserDealt == lw, "a raider at 700 u: out of reach, nothing fires");
            rd.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
        }

        // ── enemy fire, shields, stasis, the escape pod, re-boarding ──
        {
            var d3x = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy3");
            Check(d3x.Armed && !H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1").Armed, "only the bottom-right dummy is armed");
            var sh = Me;
            double Dummy() => sh.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("?", out var v) ? v : 0;    // (hull regenerates: count the hit itself)
            double hit0 = Dummy();
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -120); sh.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sh.Rotation = 0f;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && Dummy() == hit0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(Dummy() - hit0 - 50) < 0.01, $"the armed dummy's missile hits a ship within 150 u for 50 ({Dummy() - hit0:0})");
            var shield = Nodes<ShieldFlash>(sh).First();
            Check(shield.Glow(2) > 0 && shield.Glow(0) == 0, "the shield lights the side facing the dummy (astern), not the bow");
            sh.Position = d3x.Position + new Vector2(0, -1200); await Wait(6.0);
            double taken1 = sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum(); await Wait(6.0);        // (hull regenerates: count damage instead)
            Check(sh.DamageBySource.Values.Sum() == taken1, "outside 150 u it holds fire");
            Check(sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).Length * 0.45f), 0f)
                  && !sh.Covers(sh.Position + Vector2.Right.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * (Classes.Art(sh.Class).HalfWidth + 12f), 0f),
                  "the collider is a capsule: the bow is inside, a point just off the beam is not");
            // death
            sh.TakeDamage(sh.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            var pod = Nodes<EscapePod>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(!sh.Alive && sh.StasisLeft > 119 && pod != null && !Combat.Hostiles.Contains(pod as IHittable) && !Combat.Players.Any(p => (object)p == pod),
                  "destroyed: stasis (2:00) and an escape pod that nothing can target");
            Check(pod != null && Math.Abs(pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Texture.GetHeight() * pod.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().First().Scale.Y - 24f) < 0.5f,
                  "the pod is 24 u long (60% of a salvager's 40)");
            var podStart = pod.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W);
            Check(pod.Position.DistanceTo(podStart) > 20f && sh.Velocity == Vector2.Zero, "W flies the pod; the ship stays put in stasis");
            Check(H.GetType().GetField("_cam", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance) != null && sh.ViewPosition == pod.Position, "the camera follows the pod");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!sh.Alive, "F does nothing before stasis ends");
            typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_stasis", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(sh, 0.5);
            await Wait(0.7);
            Check(sh.CanReboard, "after the timer the ship is ready");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(sh.Alive && sh.Hp >= sh.MaxHp * 0.33 - 1e-6 && sh.Hp < sh.MaxHp * 0.34 && Nodes<EscapePod>().Count() == 0, "F re-boards at 33% hull (regenerating from there); the pod is gone");
            sh.TakeDamage(-(sh.MaxHp - sh.Hp));  // (test only) back to full for what follows
        }

        // a double-click on empty space clears the target; a single click there does not
        {
            var dd = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            var empty = Me.Position + new Vector2(-260, -260);
            Click(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a single click on empty space keeps the target");
            DoubleClick(Scr(empty)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == null, "a double-click on empty space clears it");
            Click(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            DoubleClick(Scr(dd.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dd, "a double-click on a target keeps it selected");
        }

        // selection rules still hold
        await Park(d1.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300));
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d1, "Tab selects the enemy nearest the ship");
        Click(Scr(d2.Position)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == d2, "left-click selects another");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(H.Selected == null && H != null, "Esc clears the target before leaving");

        // placement hook
        Vector2? placedAt = null;
        H.BeginPlacement("TEST", p => placedAt = p);
        var spot = d1.Position + new Vector2(-150, -150);
        Click(Scr(spot)); await Wait(0.05);
        Check(placedAt.HasValue && placedAt.Value.DistanceTo(spot) < 3f && !H.Placing, "left-click confirms a placement at the cursor");

        // wings: class swap and rebuild leave no orphans
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == 0, "to battleship: wings gone");
        await SetClass(ShipClass.Carrier);
        Priv(H, "RebuildShips"); await Wait(0.1);
        int craft = (int)(Me.Stats["fighter_count"] + Me.Stats["bomber_count"]);
        Check(Count<Wing>() == craft && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, $"rebuild leaves no orphan wings ({Count<Wing>()} of {craft})");

        // ── camera: wheel zoom, the free camera (Y), its tether ──
        {
            var sh = Me;
            void Wheel(MouseButton b) { Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseButton { ButtonIndex = b, Pressed = true, Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) }); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelDown); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom / Hub.ZoomOutMax), $"wheel out stops at 33% further out ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) Wheel(MouseButton.WheelUp); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, Hub.DefaultZoom * Hub.ZoomInMax), $"wheel in stops at 1.5x ({H.ZoomLevel:0.000})");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom * 0.8f); float kept = H.ZoomLevel;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.FreeCamera, "Y frees the camera");
            var cam0 = H.CameraPosition;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X > 800, $"arrow keys move it ({H.CameraPosition.X - cam0.X:0} u in 1 s)");
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.Right); await Wait(6.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.Right);
            float tether = H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition);
            Check(Mathf.Abs(tether - sh.MyArt.CameraRange) < 1f && sh.MyArt.CameraRange == 5000f, $"it stops 5000 u from the ship ({tether:0})");
            var cam1 = H.CameraPosition;
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(2, 540)) });
            await Wait(0.5);
            Input.ParseInputEvent(new InputEventMouseMotion { Position = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)), GlobalPosition = Win(new Vector2(960, 540)) });
            Check(H.CameraPosition.X < cam1.X - 100f, "the mouse against the screen edge pans it too");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Y); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(!H.FreeCamera && H.CameraPosition.DistanceTo(sh.ViewPosition) < 30f && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(H.ZoomLevel, kept),
                  "Y again returns to the ship, keeping the zoom");
            H.SetZoom(Hub.DefaultZoom);
        }

        // ── THE ENEMY TABLE: six rows, and every figure in them ──────────────────
        // (Behaviour checks elsewhere read Enemies.Of(kind); these are the literals behind them.)
        {
            EnemyDef E(int k) => Enemies.Of(k);
            Check(Enemies.All.Length == 6
                  && E(Enemies.Webifier).Hull == 25 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Dps == 1.0 && E(Enemies.Webifier).Reach == 100f
                  && E(Enemies.Gunship).Hull == 100 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Dps == 2.0 && E(Enemies.Gunship).Reach == 150f,
                  "the two the game is balanced on: a webifier of 25 hull and 1 x damage inside 100 u, a gunship of 100 and 2 x inside 150");
            Check(E(Enemies.Talon).Hull == 18 && E(Enemies.Talon).Dps == 1.4 && E(Enemies.Talon).Cruise == 130f
                  && E(Enemies.Pod).Hull == 60 && E(Enemies.Pod).Dps == 0.7 && E(Enemies.Pod).Cruise == 78f
                  && E(Enemies.Cross).Hull == 130 && E(Enemies.Cross).Dps == 2.6 && !E(Enemies.Cross).Missiles
                  && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Hull == 150 && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Reach == 260f && E(Enemies.Lancerkin).Missiles,
                  "and the owner's four: a talon (18 hull, 1.4 x, 130 u/s), a pod (60, 0.7, 78), a cross (130, 2.6, no missiles), a lancerkin (150, standing 260 u off, with them)");
            Check(Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Pin).Length == 3 && Enemies.OfWay(EnemyWay.Standoff).Length == 3
                  && Enemies.All.All(e => e.Texture != null && e.Texture.StartsWith("res://")),
                  "three that pin and three that stand off, each with its own hull drawn");
            // a wave draws from the table, so the kinds rotate rather than being written in
            var p1 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3400));
            var p2 = H.SpawnPatrol(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, -3600));
            await Wait(0.1);
            var w1 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1).ToList();
            var w2 = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p2).ToList();
            Check(w1.Count > 0 && w2.Count > 0 && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).Distinct().Count() == 2
                  && w1.Select(r => r.Kind).First() != w2.Select(r => r.Kind).First(),
                  $"one patrol of each way, and the next wave draws different kinds ({string.Join(",", w1.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())} then {string.Join(",", w2.Select(r => Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Name).Distinct())})");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.Patrol == p1 || r.Patrol == p2).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);
        }

        // ── THE NINE NEW CLASSES ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // Every class in the table flies, with the hull, mounts and bar its row says; then the
        // one thing each of the nine is FOR.
        {
            foreach (var cd in Classes.All.Where(x => x.Ready).ToList())
            {
                await SetClass(cd.Id);
                var nc = Me; var sheet = nc.Stats;
                int nMain = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => !t.PointDefense), nPd = Nodes<Turret>(nc).Count(t => t.PointDefense);
                Check(nc.Class == cd.Id && Math.Abs(nc.MaxHp - sheet["hull"]) < 1e-6
                      && nMain == (int)sheet["main_count"] && nPd == (int)sheet["pd_count"]
                      && Abilities.For(cd.Id).Length == cd.Abilities.Length + 6
                      && Classes.Art(cd.Id).Texture != null,
                      $"{cd.Name} flies: {sheet["hull"]:0} hull, {nMain} main and {nPd} PD mounts, {cd.Abilities.Length} abilities and six open slots");
            }

            // ── FREIGHTER: three turrets out, one collected, and a bubble that soaks 400 ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightHauler);
            // EMPTY SPACE to work in: the practice fighters at (900, 550) are small craft, so a
            // deployed turret would lock onto one and never kill it, and the belt, the wreck and the
            // portal all have traffic. South-east and far out is clear of every one of them.
            var fr = Me; var yonder = VaryNear(Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(2600, 2400), 500f);
            fr.Position = yonder; fr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                fr.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0;
                fr.UseAbility("deploy", 0);
                fr.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(150f, 240f);
                await Wait(0.1);
            }
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 3 && H.Deployed.Count(t => t.OwnerId == fr.OwnerId) == 3,
                  $"T drops a turret where you are, and a fourth is refused: three out ({fr.TurretsOut})");
            var one = H.Deployed.First();
            Check(Math.Abs(one.Hp - fr.Stats["deploy_hull"]) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Damage - 6) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Interval - 0.5) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(one.Spec(true).Range - 500f) < 1e-3,
                  $"a dropped turret: 120 hull, 6 a shot every 0.5 s inside 500 u ({one.Hp:0} hull)");
            fr.Position = one.Position + new Vector2(90, 0); await Wait(0.15);
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2, $"C over one picks it up again ({fr.TurretsOut} left out)");
            fr.Position = yonder + new Vector2(0, -900); await Wait(0.15);       // clear of all three
            fr.UseAbility("collect", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            Check(fr.TurretsOut == 2 && fr.FailNote("collect") == "NOT OVER ONE", "and from 400 u away it refuses, saying why");
            // a raider walks into one: the turret shoots it, and the raider shoots back
            var tgt = H.Deployed.First();
            var standAt = VaryNear(tgt.Position, 420f);                          // inside its 500 u, any direction
            var rr = H.SpawnRaider(standAt); rr.Hp = 400;
            double rh0 = rr.Hp;
            // held where it appeared: this is a check on the GUN, not on how long a raider with
            // business elsewhere happens to stay in reach of it
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rr.Hp == rh0; i++)
            { rr.Position = standAt; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(rr.Hp < rh0,
                  $"a turret left standing shoots what comes near it ({rh0 - rr.Hp:0.0} damage at {standAt.DistanceTo(tgt.Position):0} u, aiming at {(tgt.Aimed == null ? "nothing" : "it")})");
            rr.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);
            await Wait(0.1);

            fr.UseAbility("bubble", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(fr.BubbleUp && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 400) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleRadius - 260f) < 1e-3,
                  $"F raises a bubble of 400 over 260 u ({fr.BubbleLeft:0})");
            double hull0 = fr.Hp;
            fr.Hit(150, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble1");
            Check(Math.Abs(fr.Hp - hull0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(fr.BubbleLeft - 250) < 1e-6,
                  $"a 150 blow comes out of the bubble, not the hull ({fr.BubbleLeft:0} left, hull {fr.Hp:0})");
            fr.Hit(300, fr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:bubble2");
            Check(fr.BubbleLeft == 0 && Math.Abs(fr.Hp - (hull0 - 50)) < 1e-6,
                  $"and when the pool is spent the rest lands on the hull ({fr.Hp:0} of {hull0:0})");

            // ── TENDER: everything it owns fires twice as fast ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightTender);
            var td = Me; td.Position = yonder; td.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            td.Sl("deploy").Cool = 0; td.UseAbility("deploy", 0); await Wait(0.15);
            var tt = H.Deployed.First();
            double gun0 = td.Spec(false).Interval, drop0 = tt.Spec(true).Interval;
            td.UseAbility("overdrive", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(td.Spec(false).Interval - gun0 / 2) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(tt.Spec(true).Interval - drop0 / 2) < 1e-9
                  && Math.Abs(td.FireRate - 2f) < 1e-6,
                  $"overdrive halves the reload of its gun AND of every turret it has out ({gun0:0.00} -> {td.Spec(false).Interval:0.00} s)");
            foreach (var dpl in H.Deployed.ToList()) H.DeployedTaken(dpl);

            // ── BASTION: everything within 1000 u thrown a thousand units clear ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.FreightBastion);
            var bs = Me; bs.Position = yonder; bs.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var near = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300, 0)); near.Hp = 2000;
            var far = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(1600, 0)); far.Hp = 2000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            float nd0 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd0 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            bs.UseAbility("shockwave", 0); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            float nd1 = near.Position.DistanceTo(yonder), fd1 = far.Position.DistanceTo(yonder);
            Check(nd1 - nd0 > 950 && nd1 - nd0 < 1050 && Math.Abs(fd1 - fd0) < 60,
                  $"the shockwave throws what is within 1000 u a thousand units clear, and nothing beyond it ({nd1 - nd0:0} u / {fd1 - fd0:0} u)");
            near.TakeDamage(99999); far.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── SNIPER: 3 s locked in place, then 150 down a 2500 u line ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavySniper);
            var sn = Me; var dTgt = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            float railAt = Vary(900f, 2200f);                     // anywhere inside its 2500 u reach
            sn.Position = dTgt.Position + new Vector2(0, railAt); sn.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; sn.Rotation = 0f; await Wait(0.3);
            double dmg0 = dTgt.Total;
            sn.UseAbility("railgun", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var wasAt = sn.Position;
            KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyDown(global::Godot.Key.A); await Wait(1.0); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.W); KeyUp(global::Godot.Key.A);
            Check(sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt) < 1f,
                  $"the railgun charges with the hull locked: W and A move it nowhere ({sn.Position.DistanceTo(wasAt):0.00} u)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && dTgt.Total == dmg0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Math.Abs(dTgt.Total - dmg0 - 150) < 0.01 && !sn.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled),
                  $"then 150 lands {railAt:0} u away, on the line, and the hull is free again ({dTgt.Total - dmg0:0.0})");

            // ── WARRIOR: two and a half times its speed, half the damage, then the EMP ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarrior);
            var wr = Me; wr.Position = yonder; wr.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var e1 = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 200f)); e1.Hp = 500;        // inside the EMP's 260 u
            var e2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + Vector2.Right.Rotated(VaryAngle()) * Vary(800f, 1200f)); e2.Hp = 500;   // well outside its 260 u
            var e2Home = e2.Position;
            await Wait(0.1);
            double top0 = wr.Stats["max_speed"];
            wr.UseAbility("rush", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            double wh0 = wr.Hp;
            wr.Hit(100, wr.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:rush");
            Check(wr.Statuses.Has(Status.Hardened) && Math.Abs(wh0 - wr.Hp - 50) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(wr.SpeedMult - 2.5f) < 1e-6,
                  $"the rush: 2.5x top speed ({top0 * wr.SpeedMult:0} u/s) and a 100 blow lands for {wh0 - wr.Hp:0}");
            double e10 = e1.Hp, e20 = e2.Hp;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && e1.Hp == e10; i++) { e2.Position = e2Home; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(Math.Abs(e10 - e1.Hp - 60) < 0.01 && e1.Statuses.Has(Status.Disabled) && e2.Hp == e20,
                  $"and it ends in an EMP: 60 to what is within 260 u ({e1.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u) and it is held; nothing at {e2.Position.DistanceTo(wr.Position):0} u ({e10 - e1.Hp:0})");
            e1.TakeDamage(99999); e2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WARDEN: point defence with no window, and six hunters on six targets ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.HeavyWarden);
            var wd = Me; wd.Position = yonder; wd.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            Check(wd.PdOnline && !wd.PdActive && wd.PdRing == 0f && Math.Abs(wd.Spec(true).Damage - 0.25) < 1e-9,
                  "the warden's point defence is on with nothing pressed, at half a warship's damage");
            var marks = new List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { var m = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(300 + 60 * i, -200 + 90 * i)); m.Hp = 3000; marks.Add(m); }
            await Wait(0.2);
            int torp0 = Nodes<Torpedo>().Count();
            wd.UseAbility("hunters", 0); await Wait(0.2);
            var fired = Nodes<Torpedo>().Where(t => !t.HostileFire && t.TargetId != 0).ToList();
            Check(fired.Count >= 6 - torp0 && fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count() >= 4,
                  $"six hunter-seekers away, each on a target of its own ({fired.Count} out, {fired.Select(t => t.TargetId).Distinct().Count()} targets)");
            foreach (var m in marks) m.TakeDamage(99999);
            await Wait(0.3);

            // ── DART: untouchable through the roll, then faster and firing quicker ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightDart);
            var dt2 = Me; dt2.Position = yonder; dt2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            double dh0 = dt2.Hp, rof0 = dt2.Spec(false).Interval;
            dt2.UseAbility("roll", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            dt2.Hit(80, dt2.Position + new Vector2(0, -200), "test:roll");
            Check(dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.Hp - dh0) < 1e-6,
                  "through the barrel roll nothing touches it at all");
            await Wait(1.4);
            Check(!dt2.Statuses.Has(Status.Evading) && Math.Abs(dt2.SpeedMult - 1.6f) < 1e-6
                  && Math.Abs(dt2.Spec(false).Interval - rof0 / 1.25) < 1e-9,
                  $"and it comes out of it 60% faster, firing a quarter quicker ({rof0:0.000} -> {dt2.Spec(false).Interval:0.000} s)");

            // ── ECHO: what it deals for five seconds, all at once at the end ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightEcho);
            var ec = Me; ec.Position = yonder; ec.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var mark = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(150, 0)); mark.Hp = 4000;
            var near2 = H.SpawnRaider(yonder + new Vector2(260, 0)); near2.Hp = 4000;
            await Wait(0.1);
            ec.UseAbility("echo", 0); await Wait(0.1);
            ec.NoteDealt(90, mark.Position);                       // as a shell of its own would
            Check(Math.Abs(ec.Sl("echo").Own - 90) < 1e-6, $"the echo remembers what it deals ({ec.Sl("echo").Own:0})");
            double m0 = mark.Hp, n0 = near2.Hp;
            var markHome = mark.Position; var nearHome = near2.Position;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && ec.Sl("echo").Left > 0; i++)
            { mark.Position = markHome; near2.Position = nearHome; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Math.Abs(m0 - mark.Hp - 90) < 0.01 && Math.Abs(n0 - near2.Hp - 90) < 0.01,
                  $"and puts all of it down where the last of it landed, on everything within 220 u ({m0 - mark.Hp:0})");
            mark.TakeDamage(99999); near2.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);

            // ── WRAITH: five seconds nothing hostile can pick it ──
            await SetClass(ShipClass.LightWraith);
            var wt = Me; wt.Position = yonder; wt.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.2);
            var hunter = H.SpawnRaider(VaryNear(yonder, 500f)); hunter.Hp = 4000;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && hunter.Target != wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(hunter.Target == wt, "a raider comes for the wraith");
            wt.UseAbility("stealth", 0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && hunter.Target == wt; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable) && hunter.Target != wt && Targeting.Hidden(wt),
                  "and the moment it goes dark the raider lets go of it");
            await Wait(5.2);
            Check(!wt.Statuses.Has(Status.Untargetable), "five seconds later it is visible again");
            hunter.TakeDamage(99999); await Wait(0.2);
            await SetClass(ShipClass.Battleship);
        }

        // ── buildings open menus; READY -> autopilot -> portal -> ARENA -> home ──
        // (Late in the run on purpose: the trip replaces the whole hub scene, so every
        //  node reference taken before it -- dummies, ships -- is stale afterwards.)
        {
            var tioHome = Me.Position;
            Me.Position = Hub.TioPos + new Vector2(260, 0); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.TioPos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.TioOpen && Nodes<TioWindow>().Any(), "left-click on the TIO opens WARP TO TARGET");
            var tw = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(!Nodes<Button>(tw).Any(b => b.Name == "Warp"), "no WARP button: READY drives the mission");
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;
            // start well away from where the portal opens, so the autopilot has a real trip
            Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos + new Vector2(300, 700); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(0.3);
            Nodes<Button>(tw).First(b => b.Name == "Ready").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Opening && Me.AutopilotTo == H.MissionPortalPos,
                  "READY: the party (just me) is ready, the portal starts opening, and my ship flies itself there");
            await Wait(Hub.PortalOpenTime + 0.2);
            var mp = H.GetNodeOrNull<Node2D>("MissionPortal");
            var tio = H.GetNode<Sprite2D>("TIO"); float halfW = tio.GetRect().Size.X * tio.Scale.X / 2f;
            Check(H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen && mp != null && mp.Position.X > Hub.TioPos.X + halfW && mp.Position.Y < Hub.TioPos.Y - Hub.TioHeight / 2f,
                  "after 3 s the portal opens off the TIO's top-right corner");
            float d0 = Me.Position.DistanceTo(H.MissionPortalPos);
            // The base as it LEAVES, marked: the only thing that can put this figure on disk is the
            // save on the way out (the 30 s autosave is held off). That save used to write nothing --
            // the sector had already become the arena -- and a quit out there lost the difference.
            typeof(Yard).GetField("_saveCd", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H.Yard, 1e9);
            // ...and a hauler with a load waiting on its pad: the trip must take it home, not lose it
            H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300;
            double heldAtLeaving = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 45 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H?.Yard != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Yard))
                {
                    H.Yard.Credits = 424242;
                    heldAtLeaving = H.Yard.Ore + H.Yard.Salvage + H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo + H.Yard.Gatherers.Sum(g => g.Cargo);
                }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            var tripRec = typeof(Yard).GetField("_trip", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            double tripStock = tripRec == null ? -1 : (double)tripRec.GetType().GetProperty("Ore").GetValue(tripRec) + (double)tripRec.GetType().GetProperty("Salvage").GetValue(tripRec);
            Check(Math.Abs(tripStock - heldAtLeaving) < 1.0, $"leaving for the arena, the loads come home first: the base set aside holds the hauler's 300 too ({tripStock:0} of {heldAtLeaving:0})");
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"the capital-ship autopilot flew {d0:0} u to the portal and the party went through");
            var leftWith = new ConfigFile(); leftWith.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 424242) < 1e-6,
                  $"leaving for the arena writes the base to disk on the way out ({(double)leftWith.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0} of 424242)");
            var boss = H.Boss as Lancer;
            Check(boss != null && boss.Alive && boss.Type.Id == "silver_lancer" && boss.Type.Name == "SILVER LANCER" && H.Yard == null && !Nodes<TargetDummy>().Any() && Combat.Hostiles.Contains(boss),
                  "the arena at level 1: the Silver Lancer, and no base or dummies");
            Check(Met("boss"), "THE BOSS: met on entering the arena");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            var bb = Nodes<Button>().FirstOrDefault(b => b.Name == "BaseButton");
            Check(bb != null && !bb.IsVisibleInTree(), "in the arena the BASE button is hidden");
            bb?.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);                 // and even pressed, there is nothing to open
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0, "neither B nor the button opens a base menu out there");
            Check(Music.CombatZone, "the arena counts as combat for the music");
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.Rotation = 0;
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Q); await Wait(0.1);                           // point defence up
            Check(Math.Abs(boss.MaxHp - 760) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(Lancer.GunDamage / Lancer.GunEvery - 3) < 1e-9, "the boss: 760 hull at level 1 (solo); its guns 3 DPS");
            // stand in the beam on purpose with a thick hull (test only), and watch the rhythm
            // the boss's health bar: a chunk per hit, bouncing, whitening, fading; bursts merge
            var bar = Nodes<BossBar>().First();
            Check(bar.Visible, "in the arena the boss's health bar shows");
            double hpA = boss.Hp;
            boss.TakeDamage(60); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var ck = bar.Chunks.LastOrDefault();
            Check(ck != null && Math.Abs(ck.To - hpA / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(ck.From - (hpA - 60) / boss.MaxHp) < 1e-6,
                  "a hit leaves a chunk: exactly the slice of the bar it took");
            await Wait(0.06);
            float off = BossBar.Offset(ck.T), wh = BossBar.Whiteness(ck.T);
            Check(Math.Abs(off) > 1f && wh > 0f && wh < 1f, $"the chunk bobs ({off:0.0} px) and is turning white ({wh:0.00})");
            Check(BossBar.Offset(BossBar.BounceFor) == 0 && BossBar.Alpha(BossBar.Life) <= 0.001 && BossBar.Whiteness(BossBar.Life * 0.5) >= 0.999,
                  "the bounce dies away; white by half-life; gone by the end");
            await Wait(BossBar.Life + 0.1);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 0, "and then it is gone from the bar");
            // one small hit a frame for three frames (50 ms): inside the 60 ms window, so one chunk
            // (the bar reads the hull once a frame, so hits within ONE frame could never test this)
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { boss.TakeDamage(1); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(bar.Chunks.Count == 1, $"a burst of small hits, frame after frame, shares one chunk ({bar.Chunks.Count})");
            var me2 = Me; me2.MaxHp = 5000; me2.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me2.Hp));
            double tBeam = -1, tCharge = -1, tVolley = -1, arenaT = 0; int trident = 0; float spread = 0;
            // THE LIVE BEAM, 3 s: it must hold the line it drew, and that line must still be drawn.
            // The boss used to turn after the pilot the whole time, with the telegraph already gone
            // -- an invisible line sweeping the arena. 2.2 s in, the pilot is moved clear to the
            // side (after five 50-hull ticks, which the damage check below counts on): a boss that
            // turns would now swing after it.
            var beamLiveF = typeof(Lancer).GetField("_beamLive", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            double liveT = 0; float liveRot0 = 0, liveTurn = 0; bool liveDrawn = false, sidestep = false;
            async void Watch()
            {
                while (Hub.InArena && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(boss))
                {
                    arenaT += 1.0 / 60;
                    if ((double)beamLiveF.GetValue(boss) >= 0)
                    {
                        if (liveT == 0) liveRot0 = boss.Rotation;
                        liveT += 1.0 / 60;
                        liveTurn = Math.Max(liveTurn, Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(liveRot0, boss.Rotation)));
                        if (liveT > 2.0 && Nodes<Telegraph>(boss).Any(x => x.Line)) liveDrawn = true;
                        if (liveT > 2.2 && !sidestep) { sidestep = true; me2.Position += Vector2.Right.Rotated(boss.Rotation) * 400f; }
                    }
                    // The beam's place in the rhythm is where it COMMITS -- its escorts going out.
                    // Its red line comes later, when their web pins the pilot, so timing the rhythm
                    // off the telegraph would measure that wait, not the rhythm.
                    if (tBeam < 0 && H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)) tBeam = arenaT;
                    foreach (var tl in Nodes<Telegraph>().Where(x => x.Line))
                        if (tCharge < 0 && Math.Abs(tl.Width - Lancer.HullHalfWidth * 2f) < 0.1f) tCharge = arenaT;
                    if (tVolley < 0 && boss.Volleys > 0)
                    {
                        tVolley = arenaT;
                        var big = Nodes<Torpedo>().Where(x => x.HostileFire && x.Size == 2f).ToList();
                        trident = big.Count;
                        if (big.Count == 3) spread = big.Max(x => x.Dir.Angle()) - big.Min(x => x.Dir.Angle());
                    }
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                }
            }
            Watch();
            // The death beam OPENS WITH ITS ESCORTS, well before any red line: two fragile lights
            // 45 degrees to port and starboard of the nose, shivering on the spot while they come
            // round onto the pilot. The beam starts charging once their web has pinned the pilot.
            var escorts = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Raider>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 20 && escorts.Count < 2; i++) { escorts = H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            var bnose = Vector2.Up.Rotated(boss.Rotation);
            var escAng = escorts.Select(r => Mathf.RadToDeg(bnose.AngleTo(r.Position - boss.Position))).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            Check(escorts.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(escAng[0] + 45) < 12 && Math.Abs(escAng[1] - 45) < 12
                  && escorts.All(r => r.Target == me2 && r.Hp > 0 && r.Hp <= Lancer.EscortHull * Missions.S(Missions.Level) + 1e-6),
                  $"two escorts launch at the pilot, 45 degrees to port and starboard ({string.Join(", ", escAng.Select(x => x.ToString("0")))}), fragile");
            Check(escorts.All(r => r.Shivering), "the escorts shiver at the launch point before the run in");
            // ESCORTS OUT, THE BOSS ALREADY HOLDS ITS POSITION -- and turns only to face the pilot. Pushed
            // out past its 650 u standoff (where it would otherwise close at 30 u/s) and twisted 0.6 rad
            // off the pilot first, so both "it held" and "it turned" are real claims.
            {
                var armHome = boss.Position;
                boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
                var armAt = boss.Position;
                boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
                float Off() => Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f));
                float armOff0 = Off();
                await Wait(0.35);
                float armOff1 = Off();
                Check(boss.EscortsOut && !boss.BeamCharging && boss.Locked && boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt) < 0.01f && armOff0 - armOff1 > 0.07f,
                      $"escorts out, the boss holds its position already ({boss.Position.DistanceTo(armAt):0.000} u) and turns only to face the pilot ({armOff0:0.000} -> {armOff1:0.000} rad)");
                boss.Position = armHome;
            }
        // ON THE WIRE. Boosting and Shivering are read by _Draw, and both were host-only: a guest
        // drew neither the escorts' triple-length plume nor any raider's boost plume. They ride
        // the raider packet as two bits now. Bit values as literals, not Raider.FlagShiver -- a
        // check that reads the constant cannot catch the constant changing under it.
        //
        // An escort is boost-ARMED while it shivers (the boost is not spent sitting still), so
        // both bits are set here and only the shiver bit clears when it goes. Asserting the pair
        // BEFORE and AFTER is what makes this a check on the bits rather than on one snapshot:
        // a NetFlags stuck at any constant value fails one half or the other.
        var shiverFlags = escorts.Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(escorts.All(r => (r.NetFlags & 2) != 0),
              $"a shivering escort says so on the wire ({string.Join(",", shiverFlags)})");
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && escorts.Any(r => r.Shivering); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        var runFlags = escorts.Where(r => r.Alive).Select(r => r.NetFlags).ToList();
        Check(runFlags.Count > 0 && runFlags.All(f => (f & 2) == 0) && runFlags.Any(f => (f & 1) != 0),
              $"and once it goes, the shiver bit clears and the boost bit stands ({string.Join(",", runFlags)})");
            Check(Nodes<Telegraph>().All(t => !t.Line), "no beam line yet: the escorts go first, the charge follows their web");
            // THE CHARGE STARTS ON THE PIN -- not on a guess made at launch. Pinned now, well before the
            // web was predicted to land, it charges at once (the old boss waited out its estimate).
            if (boss.EscortsOut)
            {
                double predicted = (double)typeof(Lancer).GetField("_beamPredict", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(boss);
                me2.ApplyStatus(Status.Pinned, 0.3);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.ChargeCause == "pinned" && boss.ArmedFor < predicted - 0.2,
                      $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned, before the web was predicted to land ({boss.ChargeCause} at {boss.ArmedFor:0.00} s, predicted {predicted:0.00} s)");
            }
            else Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.ChargeCause == "pinned",
                       $"the charge starts the moment the pilot is pinned ({boss.ChargeCause} at {boss.ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
            // the bar under the boss's hull resets as a super commits and counts the 15 s to the next
            Check(boss.SuperFill < 0.15 && Math.Abs(boss.NextSuperIn - 15) < 1.5,
                  $"the super-move bar resets as the beam commits, counting the 15 s to the ram ({boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
            Telegraph beam = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && beam == null; i++) { beam = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Line); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(beam != null && boss.TelegraphsPending > 0, "the death beam is telegraphed: a red line before it fires");
            Check(Math.Abs(beam.Duration - Lancer.BeamWindup) < 1e-6 && Lancer.BeamWindup == 6, "the beam charges 6 s");
            // the warning is parented to the boss, so it swings with the hull instead of being
            // pinned to the spot the boss stood on: the line drawn IS the line fired
            Check(beam.GetParent() == boss, "the beam's telegraph rides the hull rather than the world");
            // Push it out past its 650 u standoff BEFORE measuring. Inside that range it would
            // not close anyway, so watching for drift there proves nothing -- a mutant that
            // ignored the lock completely still passed this. Out here it wants to close at
            // 30 u/s, which is 22 u over the window below, so holding still is a real claim.
            var bossWasAt = boss.Position;
            boss.Position += (boss.Position - me2.Position).Normalized() * 700f;
            var lockAt = boss.Position;
            double standoff = boss.Position.DistanceTo(me2.Position);
            await Wait(0.75);
            Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.Locked && standoff > 650 && boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt) < 0.01f,
                  $"the boss is locked in place while the beam charges, {standoff:0} u out where it would otherwise close (drifted {boss.Position.DistanceTo(lockAt):0.000} u)");
            boss.Position = bossWasAt;   // put it back: left out there its tridents take so long to
                                         // arrive that the point-defence check below times out
            // Reach, not trigger: this is how far the beam carries, whatever started its charge (the
            // 30 s rhythm, the nearest pilot, the escorts' web). Compared with
            // the spec's number as a literal, never with BeamLength itself, and checked on the
            // real segment so the drawn indicator and the hit line are both covered.
            var (segA, segB) = boss.BeamSegment();
            var farOut = segA + (segB - segA).Normalized() * 5000f;
            Check(Lancer.BeamLength == 10000f && Math.Abs((beam.B - beam.A).Length() - 10000f) < 1f
                  && Combat.DistToSegment(farOut, segA, segB) < 1f,
                  $"beam and indicator both reach 10000 u -- a point 5000 u out is inside it, where the old 1800 u fell short (indicator {(beam.B - beam.A).Length():0} u)");
            // HARD LOCKED while it charges -- with the pilot FREE: the old boss froze too once the pilot
            // was pinned, and tracked it otherwise, so only a free pilot tells the two apart. Point
            // defence clears the escorts and the web lets go; then, twisted well off the pilot, the boss
            // does not turn back. A boss that tracked would close 0.15 rad of the 0.6 in the 0.5 s.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && (me2.Pinned || H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort)); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.Rotation += 0.6f;
            float heldAt = boss.Rotation;
            await Wait(0.5);
            Check(boss.BeamCharging && !me2.Pinned && Mathf.Abs(Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt)) < 1e-4f,
                  $"hard locked while the beam charges, the pilot free: twisted off it, the boss does not turn back (pinned {me2.Pinned}, turned {Mathf.AngleDifference(boss.Rotation, heldAt):0.0000} rad in 0.5 s)");
            // The twist was a probe, and the aim is locked, so put it back on the pilot by hand: the
            // beam below has to be the real shot, and the damage check at the end of this scenario is
            // the only thing that proves the locked line still lands.
            boss.Rotation = (me2.Position - boss.Position).Angle() + Mathf.Pi / 2f;
            int down0 = Torpedo.Intercepted;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Torpedo.Intercepted == down0; i++)
            {
                if (!Me.PdActive && Me.PdReady) Key(global::Godot.Key.Q);
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            Check(Torpedo.Intercepted > down0, "point defence shoots the boss's missiles down");
            Check(!H.Raiders.Any(r => r.IsEscort), "point defence, switched on, kills the beam's escorts");
            var aMissile = Nodes<Torpedo>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.HostileFire);
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(boss) == 2 && (aMissile == null || Turret.PdPriority(aMissile) == 0), "point defence takes missiles before anything else");
            var tiny = new TinyTarget();
            Check(Turret.PdPriority(tiny) == 1 && Turret.PdPriority(tiny) < Turret.PdPriority(boss)
                  && Targeting.PointDefence.Allows(tiny) && !Targeting.PointDefence.Allows(boss),
                  "then small craft (by their tag, not their size), then everything else -- and a boss is never a PD target");
            Telegraph ring = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 18 && ring == null; i++) { ring = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.Line); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(ring != null && ring.Radius == Lancer.WaveRadius, "the shockwave is telegraphed: a red ring before it lands");
            // without point defence, a pair of escorts reaches the pilot and pins it inside the charge
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Me.PdActive; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            boss.LaunchEscorts(me2);
            bool pinnedInTime = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < (int)(60 * Lancer.BeamWindup) && !pinnedInTime; i++) { pinnedInTime = me2.Pinned; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(pinnedInTime, "left alone (no point defence), the escorts pin the pilot before the charge ends");
            foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && (tCharge < 0 || boss.Charging || tVolley < 0); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            GD.Print($"  boss rhythm: beam {tBeam:0.0} s, trident {tVolley:0.0} s, charge {tCharge:0.0} s; trident of {trident}, spread {Mathf.RadToDeg(spread):0} deg");
            // the watcher starts a moment after the boss's clock, so compare the gaps, not the times
            Check(tBeam > 0 && Math.Abs(tCharge - tBeam - 15) < 0.4 && Math.Abs(tVolley - tBeam - 7.5) < 0.4,
                  "the rhythm: the charge 15 s after the beam, a trident half-way between");
            Check(trident == 3 && Math.Abs(Mathf.RadToDeg(spread) - 50) < 3, "a trident: three double-size missiles, the outer two 25 degrees off the middle");
            double beamDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:beam", out var bd) ? bd : 0, chargeDmg = me2.DamageBySource.TryGetValue("boss:charge", out var cd) ? cd : 0;
            // live 3 s, checked every 0.25 s for 50 -- one source lands at most once per 0.52 s, and
            // ticks fall on frames, so two ticks are sometimes 0.533 s apart: 5 to 7 hits of 50
            Check(beamDmg >= 200 && beamDmg <= 350 && Math.Abs(beamDmg % 50) < 1e-6, $"the beam, live 3 s at 50 a tick, through the 0.52 s guard: {beamDmg / 50:0} hits ({beamDmg:0})");
            Check(Math.Abs(chargeDmg - 40) < 1e-6, $"the charge rams for 40 ({chargeDmg:0})");
            Check(sidestep && liveTurn < 0.01f, $"the live beam holds the line it drew, with the pilot gone from it (turned {Mathf.RadToDeg(liveTurn):0.00} deg)");
            Check(liveDrawn, "and the line is still drawn 2 s into its 3 s burn");
            Check(Music.I == null || Math.Abs(Lancer.TridentSpeed - 135f) < 1e-3, "trident missiles fly at 135 u/s (10% slower)");
            // THE FALLBACKS, each on a beam started by hand. The escorts shot down: the charge still comes,
            // but not before their web would have landed. Escorts that neither pin nor die (the pilot far
            // off): they cannot stall it past 5 s. And the ram waits out a beam rather than snapping round
            // in the middle of one.
            {
                var nf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
                var fBeam = typeof(Lancer).GetField("_beam", nf); var fPredict = typeof(Lancer).GetField("_beamPredict", nf);
                var fCharge = typeof(Lancer).GetField("_charge", nf); var fPending = typeof(Lancer).GetField("_pendingCharge", nf);
                bool RamLine() => Nodes<Telegraph>().Any(t => t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Width - Lancer.HullHalfWidth * 2f) < 0.1f) || fPending.GetValue(boss) != null || boss.Charging;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                fBeam.SetValue(boss, 0.01);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !boss.EscortsOut; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                double predictD = (double)fPredict.GetValue(boss);
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !boss.BeamCharging; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.ChargeCause == "escorts down" && boss.ArmedFor >= predictD - 0.02 && boss.ArmedFor < predictD + 0.1,
                      $"the escorts shot down, the beam still charges -- once their web would have landed, not before ({boss.ChargeCause} at {boss.ArmedFor:0.00} s, web due {predictD:0.00} s)");
                // the ram falls due in the middle of this beam: it waits for the beam to end, then comes
                fCharge.SetValue(boss, 0.01);
                bool ramDuringBeam = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && (boss.BeamCharging || (double)typeof(Lancer).GetField("_beamLive", nf).GetValue(boss) >= 0); i++)
                { ramDuringBeam |= RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                bool ramAfter = false;
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !ramAfter; i++) { ramAfter = RamLine(); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(!ramDuringBeam && ramAfter, $"the ram, due mid-beam, waits for the beam to end and then comes (during: {ramDuringBeam}, after: {ramAfter})");
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && (boss.Locked || RamLine()); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                // the pilot 3000 u off: the escorts need longer than 5 s to reach it
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 3000); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                fBeam.SetValue(boss, 0.01);
                for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && !boss.BeamCharging; i++) { me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                Check(boss.BeamCharging && boss.ChargeCause == "overdue" && Math.Abs(boss.ArmedFor - 5.0) < 0.05,
                      $"escorts that neither pin nor die cannot stall it: it charges 5 s after they launch ({boss.ChargeCause} at {boss.ArmedFor:0.00} s)");
                foreach (var r in H.Raiders.Where(r => r.IsEscort).ToList()) r.TakeDamage(999);
                me2.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 500); me2.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            }
            // the kill: shared EXP for it and the mission, the bounty's credits, then home
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            // (hints off meanwhile: a card starting mid-way would move the batched save these checks time)
            Character.HintsOff = true;
            // clear of where the crates will fall, with the boss's escorts out (they must die with it)
            Me.Position = boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 1500); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            boss.LaunchEscorts(Me); await Wait(0.1);
            int escortsOut = H.Raiders.Count;
            int HoldOnDisk() { var c = new ConfigFile(); if (c.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") != Error.Ok || !c.HasSection("gear_hold")) return 0;
                               return c.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)c.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)); }
            int UnclaimedOnDisk() => ((string)OnDisk("loot", "unclaimed", "")).Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length;
            int hold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum(), disk0 = HoldOnDisk();
            var bossAt = boss.Position;
            // killed mid-wind-up, a beam on its hull and a ring on the ground: its warnings go with it
            Priv(boss, "Tele", true, new Vector2(0, -100), new Vector2(0, -3000), 70f, 10.0, true, 0.0, null, null);
            Priv(boss, "Tele", false, boss.Position + new Vector2(0, 600), Vector2.Zero, 300f, 10.0, false, 0.0, null, null);
            var warnings = Nodes<Telegraph>().Where(t => t.Duration == 10.0).ToList();
            boss.TakeDamage(boss.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.2);
            Check(warnings.Count == 2 && warnings.All(t => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t)),
                  $"a boss killed mid-wind-up takes its warnings with it: no beam charging over a DEFEATED boss ({warnings.Count(t => GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(t))} of {warnings.Count} left)");
            var dropped = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(dropped.Count == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2
                  && dropped.All(c => c.Position.DistanceTo(bossAt) < 300 && Equipment.ById(c.Item) is { Kit: false, Rarity: Rarity.Common }),
                  $"a level-1 boss drops this pilot 2 Common crates where it died, on disk at once ({dropped.Count} crates, {UnclaimedOnDisk()} on disk)");
            Check(escortsOut >= 2 && H.Raiders.Count == 0, $"the boss's escorts die with it ({escortsOut} out, {H.Raiders.Count} left)");
            // NO CLOCK any more: the failure timer is not armed on a win, and a RETURN button appears
            double solEndT = (double)typeof(Hub).GetField("_arenaEndT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
            Check(solEndT < 0 && H.MissionWon && !H.IReturned && H.ReturnTotal == 1 && Nodes<ReturnButton>().Any(),
                  $"after the kill: a RETURN button and no clock sending you home (_arenaEndT {solEndT:0.0}, ready {H.ReturnReady}/{H.ReturnTotal})");
            Priv(H, "ReceiveLoot", new[] { "no_such_part", "chip_basic", "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2", "fr_flak_3" }, bossAt + new Vector2(0, 400), 1);
            Check(H.Crates.Count == 4 && H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item).SequenceEqual(new[] { "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2" }),
                  $"a drop off the wire: no unknown part, no kit, and no more than a boss gives -- 2 at level 1 ({string.Join(",", H.Crates.Skip(2).Select(c => c.Item))})");
            // FLY OVER A CRATE: in the hold at once; on disk 2.5 s after the LAST pickup
            var crA = H.Crates[0]; var crB = H.Crates[1];
            Me.Position = crA.Position - Vector2.Right.Rotated(Me.Rotation) * (Me.MyArt.HalfWidth + 38f); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            await Wait(0.5);
            bool besideLeft = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0;
            Check(besideLeft, "a ship beside a crate, not over it, leaves it where it lies");
            Me.Position = crA.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            bool tookA = !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crA) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 1;
            await Wait(1.5);
            Me.Position = crB.Position; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crB); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.5); int diskMid = HoldOnDisk();
            await Wait(1.3); int diskAfter = HoldOnDisk();
            Check(tookA && diskMid == disk0 && diskAfter == disk0 + 2 && UnclaimedOnDisk() == 2,
                  $"flying over a crate takes it into the hold at once; the file follows 2.5 s after the LAST pickup ({disk0}: {diskMid} 1.5 s after the second, {diskAfter} at 2.8 s)");
            // IN STASIS, THE POD COLLECTS: near is not enough -- it has to fly over the crate
            var crC = H.Crates[0]; var cAt = crC.Position;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1); await Wait(0.3);
            var pod = (EscapePod)typeof(PlayerShip).GetField("_pod", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(Me);
            Me.Position = cAt + new Vector2(0, 900);
            pod.Position = cAt + new Vector2(60, 0); pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.0);
            bool stillThere = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC) && crC.Position == cAt;
            pod.Position = cAt; pod.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
            for (int i = 0; i < 30 && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(!Me.Alive && stillThere && !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crC),
                  $"a pilot in stasis collects with its escape pod: 60 u off leaves the crate where it lies, flying over it takes it");
            Check(Hub.InArena, "five seconds and more after the kill, the party is still in the arena");
            var crD = H.Crates[0];
            Check(H.MissionWon && Character.Level == 1 && Character.Exp == 550, $"the kill of a level-1 boss pays a level-1 pilot 200 + 250 first clear + 100 = 550 (exp {Character.Exp})");
            var onKill = new ConfigFile(); onKill.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            Check(Math.Abs((double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - (424242 + 2000)) < 1e-6,
                  $"the bounty is on disk at the kill, not on the trip home: a quit out there kept the EXP and lost the credits ({(double)onKill.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
            // no clock: RETURN takes you home; solo, the one present pilot is enough
            H.SetMyReturn(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && H.Yard != null, "pressing RETURN takes the pilot home (solo: no one else to wait for)");
            // the crate left lying came home too -- into the hold -- and its node stayed behind
            Check(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(crD) && Count<LootCrate>() == 0 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == hold0 + 4,
                  $"the crate left behind comes home in the hold, its node gone with the arena ({Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() - hold0} of 4 parts)");
            Character.HintsOff = false;
            await Wait(Hub.RaidDelay + 0.5);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "a won mission brings no raid");
            // exactly the credits set aside at departure + the 2000 bounty (a hauler sale before
            // departure is part of what was set aside, so a fixed window was fragile)
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - Yard.TripStartCredits - 2000) < 1e-6, $"the bounty's 2000 credits are paid at home ({Yard.TripStartCredits:0} + 2000 = {H.Yard.Credits:0})");
            // the literal 1/20 asked for -- not Yard.AwayShare, or the check could never fail
            double want = H.Yard.FleetRate(GatherKind.Miner) * Yard.LastAway / 20.0;
            Check(Yard.LastAway > 1 && Yard.LastAwayOre > 0 && Math.Abs(Yard.LastAwayOre - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"while away the base earned 1/20 of its rate ({Yard.LastAwayOre:0.0} ore for {Yard.LastAway:0} s)");
            // levels: the win cleared level 1; docking at the TIO selects level 2
            Check(Character.BossCleared.TryGetValue("silver_lancer", out var cleared) && cleared.Contains(1), "the win records level 1 as cleared");
            Check(Math.Abs(Missions.S(2) - 1.1) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.S(3) - 1.21) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(Missions.ForLevel(3).Hull * Missions.S(3) - 919.6) < 1e-6,
                  "each level is 10% stronger, multiplicatively (the level-3 Lancer: 760 x 1.21 = 919.6 hull)");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            var tw2 = Nodes<TioWindow>().First();
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "docking at the TIO selects the newest unlocked level (2)");
            Check(Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").Disabled, "no level beyond the newest unlocked");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelDown").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 1, "the host can pick an easier, cleared level");
            Nodes<Button>(tw2).First(b => b.Name == "LevelUp").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(Missions.Level == 2, "and back up to the newest");
            // ONE SIDE WINDOW AT A TIME: they share a spot. B over the open TIO used to leave both up,
            // one drawn over the other; now it takes the spot, and B again gives it back empty.
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<TioWindow>() == 0 && Count<BasePanel>() == 1, $"B with the TIO open: the base takes the spot ({Count<TioWindow>()} TIO, {Count<BasePanel>()} base)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Count<BasePanel>() == 0 && Count<TioWindow>() == 0, "and B again closes it");
            H.OpenTio(); await Wait(0.1);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            // a wipe: every ship in stasis at once -> the mission fails, home with nothing
            H.EnterSector(Hub.SectorKind.Arena); await Wait(1.5);
            // THE DRAKE BASTION: level 2 is its. Checked here, in the arena the wipe below fails.
            {
                var dk = H.Boss as Drake;
                var me3 = Me; me3.MaxHp = 5000; me3.TakeDamage(-(5000 - me3.Hp)); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; me3.Rotation = 0f;
                var dspr = dk?.GetChildren().OfType<Sprite2D>().FirstOrDefault();
                Check(dk != null && dk.Type.Id == "drake_bastion" && dk.Type.Name == "DRAKE BASTION" && Math.Abs(dk.MaxHp - 770) < 1e-6
                      && dspr != null && dspr.Texture.ResourcePath.EndsWith("boss_drake.png") && Math.Abs(dspr.Texture.GetHeight() * dspr.Scale.Y - 420f) < 0.5f,
                      $"level 2 is the Drake Bastion: 700 hull x1.1 = 770, 420 u long ({dk?.Type.Name}, {dk?.MaxHp:0})");
                if (dk != null)
                {
                    var nf = BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance;
                    // ITS CLOCKS: a throw every 30 s from 6 s, the shotgun every 15 s from 17 s -- 11 s after a
                    // throw starts, clear of its 9.1 s (7.5 s of warning and 1.6 s of flight), so the two never
                    // run back to back (at the trident's own 13.5 s it fell inside the first throw)
                    double gap = (double)typeof(Drake).GetField("_shotgun", nf).GetValue(dk) - (double)typeof(Drake).GetField("_throw", nf).GetValue(dk);
                    Check(Math.Abs(gap - 11) < 1e-6 && 11 > 7.5 + 1.6 && 30 - 11 - 15 > 0,
                          $"its specials never run back to back: the shotgun 11 s after each throw starts, clear of the throw's 9.1 s ({gap:0.00} s)");
                    // its own clocks out of the way: each special below is started by hand, alone
                    typeof(Drake).GetField("_throw", nf).SetValue(dk, 999.0); typeof(Drake).GetField("_shotgun", nf).SetValue(dk, 999.0);
                    // MAIN GUN: a slow shell every 2.5 s (2.4 DPS at 6 a shell), dodged by moving
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); await Wait(0.1);
                    var shellSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Slug>(Nodes<Slug>());
                    var shellAt = new System.Collections.Generic.List<double>(); Slug shell = null; double clock = 0;
                    double gun0 = me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:gun");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 7 && shellAt.Count < 2; i++)
                    {
                        await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); clock += GetProcessDeltaTime();
                        foreach (var s in Nodes<Slug>().Where(s => s.Look == Slug.Kind.Shell && shellSeen.Add(s))) { shellAt.Add(clock); shell = s; }
                    }
                    float sspd = 0;
                    if (shell != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell)) { var sp0 = shell.Position; for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); sspd = GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(shell) ? (shell.Position - sp0).Length() / (10f / 60f) : 0; }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:gun") == gun0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double gunHit = me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:gun") - gun0;
                    Check(shellAt.Count == 2 && Math.Abs(shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] - 2.5) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(sspd - 220) < 4 && Math.Abs(gunHit - 6.6) < 1e-6,
                          $"its main gun: a slow shell (220 u/s) every 2.5 s, 6 a hit -- 2.4 DPS before the level's scale (x1.1 here: 6.6) ({sspd:0} u/s, {(shellAt.Count == 2 ? shellAt[1] - shellAt[0] : -1):0.00} s apart, {gunHit:0.00})");
                    // THE SCRAP SHOTGUN (started by hand): a warpRing where it will land -- 600 u from the pilot --
                    // then, held still, a fan of seven red lines for 1.875 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 1.5 s), then
                    // seven pieces of scrap down them: the same fixed fan, 10 degrees apart
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 1400); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    typeof(Drake).GetField("_shotgun", nf).SetValue(dk, 0.01);
                    Telegraph warpRing = null;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && warpRing == null; i++) { warpRing = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => !t.Line); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !dk.FanWinding; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var landed = dk.Position; float warpOff = landed.DistanceTo(me3.Position);
                    var fan = Nodes<Telegraph>().Where(t => t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Duration - 1.875) < 1e-6).ToList();
                    var scrapSeen = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<Slug>(Nodes<Slug>());
                    await Wait(1.0);
                    bool heldStill = dk.Position.DistanceTo(landed) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    var scrap = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Slug>();
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && scrap.Count < 7; i++) { scrap.AddRange(Nodes<Slug>().Where(s => s.Look == Slug.Kind.Scrap && scrapSeen.Add(s))); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    float aimTo = (me3.Position - landed).Angle();
                    var offs = scrap.Select(s => Mathf.RadToDeg(Mathf.AngleDifference(aimTo, s.Dir.Angle()))).OrderBy(a => a).ToList();
                    bool fanned = offs.Count == 7 && offs.Zip(new[] { -30f, -20f, -10f, 0f, 10f, 20f, 30f }, (a, b) => Math.Abs(a - b) < 0.5f).All(x => x);
                    Check(warpRing != null && Math.Abs(warpRing.Duration - 1.0) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(warpOff - 600) < 5 && fan.Count == 7 && heldStill && fanned,
                          $"the scrap shotgun: a warp to 600 u off the pilot ({warpOff:0}), a fan of 7 lines for 1.875 s held still, then 7 pieces at -30..+30 in 10s ({string.Join(" ", offs.Select(a => a.ToString("0")))})");
                    // each piece is its own hit (boss:scrap:0..6, the middle one 3): the middle piece, aimed at the
                    // pilot, strikes it -- and a hull as broad as the 302 u battleship's takes its neighbours too
                    double Piece(int k) => me3.DamageBySource.TryGetValue($"boss:scrap:{k}", out var v) ? v : 0;
                    double mid0 = Piece(3), all0 = Enumerable.Range(0, 7).Sum(Piece);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    double midDmg = Piece(3) - mid0, allDmg = Enumerable.Range(0, 7).Sum(Piece) - all0;
                    Check(Math.Abs(midDmg - 20.625) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(allDmg / 20.625 - Math.Round(allDmg / 20.625)) < 1e-6,
                          $"the middle piece strikes the pilot it was aimed at, each piece its own 15 x1.25 = 18.75, x1.1 = 20.625 ({midDmg:0.000}; {allDmg / 20.625:0} of the 7 struck)");
                    // THE ASTEROID THROW (started by hand): a rock beside it in its tractor, a red lane of the
                    // rock's width for 7.5 s (1.25 x the Lancer's 6 s charge), the boss held still -- then the
                    // throw: slow, then very fast (the first half of the flight covers an eighth of the lane),
                    // 250 x1.1 = 275 to a ship in the lane, and the rock breaks apart at the lane's end
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && dk.Locked; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    me3.Position = dk.Position + new Vector2(0, 700); me3.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(0.2);
                    typeof(Drake).GetField("_throw", nf).SetValue(dk, 0.01);
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 && dk.Rock == null; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var rock = dk.Rock;
                    var lane = Nodes<Telegraph>().FirstOrDefault(t => t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Width - 180f) < 0.5f);
                    var drakeAt = dk.Position; var rockAt = rock?.Position ?? Vector2.Zero;
                    await Wait(3.0);
                    bool heldRock = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && rock.Position.DistanceTo(rockAt) < 0.01f && !rock.Thrown
                                    && dk.Position.DistanceTo(drakeAt) < 0.01f && dk.Locked;
                    Check(rock != null && lane != null && Math.Abs(lane.Duration - 7.5) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(lane.Hold - 1.6) < 1e-6
                          && Math.Abs(rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt) - 220) < 1 && heldRock,
                          $"the throw: a rock beside it in its tractor (220 u off), a lane of its width for 7.5 s, the boss and the rock held still ({rockAt.DistanceTo(drakeAt):0} u, lane {lane?.Duration:0.0} s)");
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) && !rock.Thrown; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                    var lane0 = rock?.From ?? Vector2.Zero; float laneLen = rock == null ? 1 : rock.From.DistanceTo(rock.To);
                    await Wait(0.8);
                    double half = rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock) ? rock.Position.DistanceTo(lane0) / laneLen : -1;
                    bool broke = false;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && rock != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(rock); i++) { broke |= rock.Done; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
                    double rockDmg = me3.DamageBySource.GetValueOrDefault("boss:rock");
                    Check(Math.Abs(half - 0.125) < 0.04 && broke && Math.Abs(laneLen - 1800) < 1 && Math.Abs(rockDmg - 275) < 1e-6,
                          $"thrown: half the flight covers an eighth of the 1800 u lane ({half:0.000}), it strikes the pilot for 275 ({rockDmg:0}), and breaks apart at the end ({broke})");
                }
            }
            // EVERY BOSS SPECIAL HAS ITS OWN SOUND, heard as it happens: the Lancer's beam (its charge, and
            // the growl as it fires), ram, shockwave and trident, fought above; the Drake's gun, warp, scrap
            // blast, tractor, throw and the rock breaking, here
            {
                var bossSounds = new[] { "boss_beam_charge", "boss_beam", "boss_ram", "boss_shockwave", "boss_trident",
                                         "drake_gun", "drake_warp", "drake_scrap", "drake_tractor", "drake_throw", "drake_rock" };
                var unheard = bossSounds.Where(n => GD.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") == null || Sfx.Played.GetValueOrDefault(n) == 0).ToList();
                Check(unheard.Count == 0, $"every boss special has its own sound, and each was heard in its fight (unheard: {string.Join(", ", unheard)})");
            }
            int exp1 = Character.Exp; int failLevel = Missions.Level;
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 8 && Hub.InArena; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Character.Exp == exp1 && Me.Alive, "the whole party in stasis: the mission fails, home with no reward");
            // ...and the boss sends its raiders after you: 2 patrols (solo), from the map's edge, at the failed level's strength
            Check(H.Raiders.Count == 0, "the raid is not there at once (every guest's world loads first)");
            // measured the frame they appear: a raider that spots a miner boosts inward at once
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && H.Raiders.Count == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            double sL = Math.Pow(1.1, failLevel - 1);
            var raid = H.Raiders.ToList();
            GD.Print($"  raid: {raid.Count} raiders, level {failLevel} (x{sL:0.00}), {raid.Min(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0}-{raid.Max(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos)):0} u out");
            Check(raid.Count == 8 && raid.Count(r => r.Heavy) == 2 && raid.Select(r => r.Patrol).Distinct().Count() == 2,
                  "a failed mission: the boss sends 2 patrols (3 lights and a heavy each)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) - Hub.RaidEdge) < 110f), "in from the edge of the base's map (3200 u out)");
            Check(raid.All(r => Math.Abs(r.Strength - sL) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(r.MaxHull - Enemies.Of(r.Kind).Hull * sL) < 1e-6),
                  $"at the failed boss's level: hull and damage x{sL:0.00} (1.1^(L-1), L = {failLevel})");
            foreach (var r in raid) r.TakeDamage(1e9);
            await Wait(0.3);
            // the fighter-class autopilot, flown on its own (no player fighter class yet)
            Vector2 fp = Vector2.Zero, fv = Vector2.Zero; float fh = 0f; var goal = new Vector2(800, -600);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12; i++)
            {
                var (turn, thrust) = Autopilot.Fighter(fp, fh, fv, goal, 300f, 30f);
                fh += turn * 4f / 60f; fv += Vector2.Right.Rotated(fh) * thrust * 600f / 60f; fv *= 0.985f;
                if (fv.Length() > 300f) fv = fv.Normalized() * 300f;
                fp += fv / 60f;
            }
