            Check(fp.DistanceTo(goal) < 80f, $"the fighter-class autopilot reaches its point ({fp.DistanceTo(goal):0} u off)");
            Check(Settings.MusicOn, "music is on by default");
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear(); Character.Points = 0; Character.Save();
            Me.Position = Hub.BasePos + new Vector2(0, 330); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; await Wait(1.5);
            Click(Scr(Hub.BasePos)); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Nodes<BasePanel>().Any(), "left-click on the base station opens the BASE menu");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.B); await Wait(0.1);
        }

        // ── radar and the Esc menu ──
        {
            var radar = Nodes<Radar>().First();
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[Settings.RadarSize]), "the radar sits top right at the chosen size");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(H.EscMenuOpen && Hub.ControlsLocked, "Esc (nothing else open) opens the menu; the helm is locked");
            var menu = Nodes<EscMenu>().First();
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "LARGE").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Settings.RadarSize == 2 && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[2]), "LARGE radar: saved and applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "SMALL").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(radar.Size.X, Radar.Sizes[0]), "SMALL radar applied");
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "MEDIUM").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
            Nodes<Button>(menu).First(b => b.Text == "50%").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Mathf.IsEqualApprox(Settings.MusicVolume, 0.5f), "music volume set from the menu");
            // ...and the row LIGHTS the live step. The default used to be 0.6, which is not one of
            // the five the menu offers, so the match that picks the lit button found nothing and
            // the row opened with none of them pressed. Both rows are checked: a settings row where
            // nothing is selected looks broken whichever row it is.
            foreach (var g in new[] { "Radar", "Music" })
            {
                var opts = Nodes<Button>(Nodes<HBoxContainer>(menu).First(r => r.Name == g)).ToList();
                Check(opts.Count > 1 && opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed) == 1,
                      $"the {g} row shows exactly one live choice ({opts.Count(b => b.ButtonPressed)} of {opts.Count} lit)");
            }
            // ...and the harness cap SURVIVES the menu re-applying the volume. Checked here, after
            // a menu has just written the audio settings, rather than at startup where nothing has
            // had a chance to overwrite it. 5% as a literal, never Settings.TestVolume: a check
            // that reads the value it is testing cannot catch the value being wrong.
            int mbus = AudioServer.GetBusIndex("Master");
            float lin = mbus < 0 ? -1f : (float)Mathf.DbToLinear(AudioServer.GetBusVolumeDb(mbus));
            Check(mbus >= 0 && !AudioServer.IsBusMute(mbus) && Mathf.Abs(lin - 0.05f) < 0.001f && Settings.Volume > 0.05f,
                  $"the harness runs at 5% master volume whatever the settings say ({lin * 100:0.0}%, setting {Settings.Volume * 100:0}%)");
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
            Check(!H.EscMenuOpen, "Esc closes the menu");
        }

        // ── music by mood: ambient, 20% combat on a selected enemy, 100% in combat ──
        {
            var sh = Me; var mus = Music.I;
            Check(mus != null, "the music player is running");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            await Wait(9.0);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.AmbientLevel, 1f) && mus.CombatLevel == 0f, "nothing selected: ambient only");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1"));
            await Wait(2.5);
            Check(mus.Target == Music.Mood.Alert && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 0.2f), $"enemy selected: combat music at 20% ({mus.CombatLevel:0.00})");
            Check(Math.Abs(Music.I.AmbientLevel - 0.35f) < 0.02, $"and the ambient at half its alert level under it (0.35: {Music.I.AmbientLevel:0.00})");
            sh.Hit(1, sh.Position + Vector2.Up.Rotated(sh.Rotation) * 80f); await Wait(2.0);
            Check(sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Combat && Mathf.IsEqualApprox(mus.CombatLevel, 1f) && mus.AmbientLevel == 0f,
                  "taking damage: combat music at 100%");
            H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
            await Wait(PlayerShip.CombatHold + 3.0);
            Check(!sh.InCombat && mus.Target == Music.Mood.Ambient, "8 s after the last damage, back to ambient");
            sh.TakeDamage(-1);
        }

        Key(global::Godot.Key.K); await Wait(0.1);
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Check(Count<StatsWindow>() == 0 && H != null, "Esc closes the K window first");
        H.GetType().GetField("_selected", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(H, null);
        // a part dropped and not flown over when the pilot quits (as in a victory window)
        Character.Unclaimed.Add("drv_surge_2"); Character.Save(); int surge0 = Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2");
        Key(global::Godot.Key.Escape); await Wait(0.1);
        Nodes<Button>(Nodes<EscMenu>().First()).First(b => b.Name == "Quit").EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed);
        await Wait(0.3);
        Check(GetTree().CurrentScene is MainMenu, "QUIT TO MAIN MENU returns to the main menu");
        GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(0.4);
        Check(H != null && Count<PlayerShip>() == 1, "hub re-enters cleanly");
        Check(Character.GearHold.GetValueOrDefault("drv_surge_2") == surge0 + 1 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
              "a part not flown over before a quit is in the hold the next time the pilot enters a world");

        // ── sound: the music 35% quieter; effects by the camera's distance and zoom ──
        Check(Math.Abs(Music.Master - 0.65f) < 1e-6, "the music plays 35% quieter than it started");
        Check(Math.Abs(Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1)) < 1e-4 && Sfx.VolumeAt(1000, 1) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3
              && Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 0.5f) < Sfx.VolumeAt(0, 1) - 3 && float.IsNegativeInfinity(Sfx.VolumeAt(6000, 1)),
              "effects: 0 dB at the view's centre, quieter with distance and when zoomed out, silent far away");
        Check(new[] { "laser_light", "laser_fighter", "laser_boss", "laser_hit", "missile_whoosh", "impact_thunk", "cannon" }
                  .All(n => GD.Load<AudioStream>($"res://sfx/{n}.wav") != null),
              "all seven effects load");
        int P(string n) => Sfx.Played.TryGetValue(n, out var v) ? v : 0;
        GD.Print($"  sounds played: light laser {P("laser_light")}, fighter {P("laser_fighter")}, boss laser {P("laser_boss")}, hit {P("laser_hit")}, whoosh {P("missile_whoosh")}, thunk {P("impact_thunk")}, cannon {P("cannon")}");
        Check(P("laser_light") > 0 && P("laser_hit") > 0, "lasers buzz where they fire and crackle where they hit");
        Check(P("laser_boss") > 0, "the boss's lasers have their own, deeper buzz");
        Check(P("missile_whoosh") > 0 && P("impact_thunk") > 0, "missiles and torpedoes whoosh at launch and thunk when they hit");
        Check(P("laser_fighter") > 0, "a carrier's fighters have their own report, not the capital ship's");
        Check(P("cannon") > 0, "the battleship's guns have their own file, not the impact played quietly");

        // ── THE LEVEL IS IN THE FILE, and these read the files to prove it ──────────────
        // Every trim in Sfx is 0 now: a sound's loudness is a property of the sound, not of
        // whoever plays it. That only means anything if the files actually carry those levels,
        // so this measures them. Ratios as literals -- 0.65 is "35% quieter" as a person means
        // it -- never as Sfx constants, which no longer exist to read.
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav") - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a carrier fighter's report is 35% quieter than a capital ship's, IN THE FILE ({Peak("res://sfx/laser_fighter.wav") / Peak("res://sfx/laser_light.wav"):0.000})");
        // The bomber's launch whoosh, 35% quieter than it used to be heard. The old file is in
        // retired/, and what is compared is the LEVEL IT PLAYS AT: the old file times its old
        // -8 dB trim, which is what the ear was actually getting.
        double oldWhoosh = Peak("res://retired/sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -8 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh - 0.65) < 0.02,
              $"a missile launch is 35% quieter than it was heard before ({Peak("res://sfx/missile_whoosh.wav") / oldWhoosh:0.000})");
        // ...and the impact is NOT, because it was already right. Same level as before, to the
        // sample: the old file at its old -2 dB trim.
        double oldThunk = Peak("res://retired/sfx/impact_thunk.wav") * Math.Pow(10, -2 / 20.0);
        Check(Math.Abs(Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk - 1.0) < 0.02,
              $"the impact is exactly as loud as it was, because it was already right ({Peak("res://sfx/impact_thunk.wav") / oldThunk:0.000})");

        // leaks: leaving and re-entering the hub must not grow the orphan-node or object
        // counts (the first cycle warms caches; compare the next two against it)
        async Task Cycle()
        {
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://MainMenu.tscn"); await Wait(0.5);
            GetTree().ChangeSceneToFile("res://Hub.tscn"); await Wait(1.0);
        }
        // Collect .NET garbage first, so the count shows real leaks, not objects merely
        // waiting for the collector (it runs whenever it likes).
        async Task<double> Settled()
        {
            for (int g = 0; g < 2; g++) { System.GC.Collect(); System.GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers(); }
            for (int f = 0; f < 3; f++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            return Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectCount);
        }
        await Cycle();
        double objA = await Settled(), orphA = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        await Cycle(); await Cycle();
        double objB = await Settled(), orphB = Performance.GetMonitor(Performance.Monitor.ObjectOrphanNodeCount);
        GD.Print($"  leak check: orphan nodes {orphA:0} -> {orphB:0}; objects {objA:0} -> {objB:0} (after collection)");
        Check(orphB <= orphA, "no orphan nodes accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        Check(objB <= objA + 20, "no objects accumulate across leaving and re-entering the hub");
        // THE LEVEL COMES WITH THE SECTOR: a peer sent into a world is told the level in the same call,
        // set before the world -- and an arena's boss -- is built from it, rather than left to the
        // mission report that follows. (Sent home again here, where the hub has just been rebuilt.)
        {
            int lv0 = Missions.Level;
            Priv(H, "NetSector", (int)Hub.SectorKind.Home, 7);
            int lvSet = Missions.Level;
            await Wait(1.0);                                   // the home world, rebuilt
            Missions.Level = lv0;
            Check(lvSet == 7 && H != null && !Hub.InArena, $"a peer sent into a world is told the level with it, before the world is built ({lvSet})");
        }

        // ── LAST, because it ends a session and that resets the fleet ────────────────────
        // A session can end without anything else moving. Quitting to the menu changes scene and
        // Yard._ExitTree writes on the way out; pressing PLAY OFFLINE, or a host closing the lobby
        // while everyone stands at home, changes nothing but the socket -- and nothing wrote the
        // character on that route, with the autosave up to thirty seconds away.
        //
        // Hosted first so there is a session to leave, and the figure is changed WHILE in it, so a
        // stale file cannot pass: the value on disk has to be the one from inside the session.
        // Placed at the very end on purpose -- going offline re-parks the base and resets the
        // ships, which quietly broke four later checks when this sat in the middle of the run.
        var yard = H.Yard;
        Net.I.Host(27124);
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 && !Net.IsOnline; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        bool hosted = Net.IsOnline;
        yard.Credits = 4242;
        // a place held for a pilot that dropped, as the session ends
        var soloHeld = (System.Collections.IDictionary)typeof(Hub).GetField("_held", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
        var heldT = typeof(Hub).GetNestedType("Held", BindingFlags.NonPublic);
        var ghostS = Activator.CreateInstance(heldT);
        heldT.GetField("OldPeer").SetValue(ghostS, 515151); heldT.GetField("Name").SetValue(ghostS, "Ghost");
        heldT.GetField("Until").SetValue(ghostS, Time.GetTicksMsec() + 60000);
        soloHeld["ghost"] = ghostS; Priv(H, "RefreshAway");
        bool ghostInParty = H.PartyIds.Contains(515151);
        Net.I.GoOffline();
        await Wait(0.15);
        Check(ghostInParty && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Idle && H.PartyIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { Net.LocalId }) && soloHeld.Count == 0,
              $"a session ended with a place held: the solo world's party is this pilot alone -- its portal is not kept shut ({string.Join(",", H.PartyIds)})");
        var afterLeaving = new ConfigFile();
        afterLeaving.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
        Check(hosted && Math.Abs((double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - 4242) < 1e-6,
              $"leaving a session writes this peer's character, with no scene change to do it for them ({(double)afterLeaving.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0})");
    }

    // ── multiplayer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // ── a dedicated two-player arena run (its own port, after the three-player run) ──
    // Mission 1: both READY, both at the portal, into the arena together; the boss dies;
    // both home. Mission 2: into the arena again; the host drops -- the guest must land
    // home, offline, with its OWN base (a marker credit total set before it joined).
    async Task ArenaMp(string role)
    {
        const int Port = 27125;
        async Task ToArena(string who, int mission)
        {
            // the home world must be BUILT first: the scene swap is deferred, and for a frame
            // or two the old one is still current
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 5 && (H == null || Hub.InArena || H.GetNodeOrNull("TIO") == null || !H.IsNodeReady()); i++)
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            H.SetMyReady(true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 12 && H.Mission != Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 14 && !Hub.InArena; i++)
            {
                if (H != null && Me != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen) { Me.Position = H.MissionPortalPos; Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.IsNodeReady() && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && Me != null, 5);
            Check(Hub.InArena, $"{who}: into the arena with the other pilot (mission {mission})");
        }
        if (role == "ahost")
        {
            Character.Name = "Arena Host";
            Net.I.Host(Port);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && Net.I.Players.Count < 2; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.I.Players.Count == 2, "arena host: the guest joined");
            await Wait(1.0);
            await ToArena("arena host", 1);
            // Built after a scene change, so from what is REMEMBERED of each pilot, not a fresh
            // announcement. It used to be only names and colours: every boss a solo boss (it counted
            // the ships before there were any), and every guest at default gear.
            Check(Math.Abs(H.Boss.HullMult - 1.6) < 1e-9 && Math.Abs(H.Boss.DamageMult - 1.2) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: a party of two faces a boss scaled for two (hull x1.6, damage x1.2; x{H.Boss.HullMult:0.00}, x{H.Boss.DamageMult:0.00})");
            var gship = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(gship != null && gship.Class == ShipClass.Destroyer && Math.Abs(gship.MaxHp - 287.5) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(gship.Stats["missile_mag"] - 6) < 1e-9,
                  $"arena host: the guest's destroyer and its own gear came into the arena with it (287.5 hull, a Salvo Rack III's 6 bursts; {gship?.Class}, {gship?.MaxHp:0.0}, {gship?.Stats["missile_mag"]:0})");
            // Long enough for the guest to watch the super-move bar move, and no longer: this
            // scenario has a second arena visit after it, and a stay stretched to fit a whole beam
            // cycle pushed that off the end of every wait that follows.
            await Wait(4.0);
            // the host in stasis at the moment of the kill: a pilot shot down in the fight still
            // helped win it, and gets its own crates -- only its own
            int ahHold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            Me.TakeDamage(Me.Hp + 1);
            H.Boss.TakeDamage(H.Boss.Hp + 1);
            await Wait(0.3);
            var ahCrates = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(!Me.Alive && ahCrates.Count == 2 && Count<LootCrate>() == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 2,
                  $"arena host: in stasis at the kill, still its own 2 crates, and none of the guest's ({ahCrates.Count} here, {Count<LootCrate>()} nodes)");
            // NO CLOCK: the party goes home only once EVERY present pilot presses RETURN. The guest
            // presses first (it collects, then readies); one pilot ready is not enough on its own.
            // (generously: the guest presses only after its own checks of the fight and its crates,
            // which can run past the kill)
            await RealTime(() => H.ReturnReady == 1, Wan ? 60 : 45);
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena && H.ReturnReady == 1 && H.ReturnTotal == 2,
                  $"arena host: one pilot's RETURN is not enough -- the party waits ({H.ReturnReady} of {H.ReturnTotal})");
            H.SetMyReturn(true);                                  // now both (a stasis pilot readies too); home together
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);   // the home world is built a frame after the sector changes
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline, "arena host: the boss down, both pilots home -- still hosting");
            Check(Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == ahHold0 + 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0 && ahCrates.All(c => !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(c)),
                  "arena host: the crates it never reached came home in its hold; their nodes did not");
            await Wait(2.0);
            H.SelectLevel(2);                                    // mission 2 is the Drake Bastion's level
            await ToArena("arena host", 2);
            Check(H.Boss is Drake { Type.Id: "drake_bastion" }, $"arena host: mission 2, level 2, is the Drake Bastion ({H.Boss?.Type.Name})");
            H.Boss.DamageMult = 0;                               // nobody is lost while the rest is checked
            H.Boss.HullMult = 2.2;                               // a party-of-3 scale: the guest must show the host's
            // in its gun's reach, and a rock thrown as soon as the guest is in the arena to be told of it
            // (a boss's events go to the peers already there), for the guest to see both
            if (H.Boss is Drake hd)
            {
                Me.Position = hd.Position + new Vector2(0, 600); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
                await RealTime(() => H.Ships.Any(s => !s.Mine && Hub.PeerSector(s.OwnerId) == Hub.SectorKind.Arena), 6);
                typeof(Drake).GetField("_throw", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(hd, 0.01);
            }
            await Wait(3.5);
            // A GUEST DROPPED MID-ARENA (no goodbye): its place is held, and it comes back into the
            // arena, where it was
            // (only once this host sees the guest where it parked: the place held must be that one)
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } p && p.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, 8);
            var ag = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine); int agOld = ag?.OwnerId ?? 0;
            Check(ag != null && ag.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, $"arena host: the guest parked at (900, 300) before its drop ({ag?.Position})");
            Drop(agOld);
            await RealTime(() => !H.Ships.Any(s => s.OwnerId == agOld), 3);
            Check(H.Ships.Count() == 1 && H.PartyIds.Count() == 2, $"arena host: a guest dropped mid-arena keeps its place in the party ({H.Ships.Count()} ship, {H.PartyIds.Count()} in the party)");
            // THE BOSS DIES WHILE IT IS AWAY: it helped win it, so the kill is owed to it
            var arenaHeld = (System.Collections.IDictionary)typeof(Hub).GetField("_held", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            H.Boss.TakeDamage(H.Boss.Hp + 1);
            var owedA = arenaHeld.Values.Cast<object>().Select(x => (System.Collections.IList)x.GetType().GetField("Owed").GetValue(x)).FirstOrDefault();
            Check(H.MissionWon && owedA?.Count == 1, $"arena host: the boss killed while the guest is away -- the kill is owed to it ({owedA?.Count})");
            // (no need to prop the arena open any more: with no victory clock it stays until a RETURN)
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine) is { } back && Hub.PeerSector(back.OwnerId) == Hub.SectorKind.Arena
                                 && back.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, Wan ? 25 : 15);
            var ag2 = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.Mine);
            Check(ag2 != null && ag2.OwnerId != agOld && ag2.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50,
                  $"arena host: it came back into the arena, where it was ({ag2?.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)):0} u off; its world {(ag2 == null ? "-" : Hub.PeerSector(ag2.OwnerId)?.ToString())}; at {ag2?.Position})");
            await Wait(3.0);                                     // (the guest checks what it was paid)
            Net.SkipGoodbye = true;                              // then the HOST vanishes -- no goodbye, as in a crash
            Net.I.GoOffline();
            await Wait(2.0);
        }
        else
        {
            Character.Name = "Arena Guest";
            // Gear the host can only know from this pilot's announcement: two chips off a destroyer
            // (three left: 250 x 1.15 = 287.5 hull, not the default five's 312.5). The host checks it
            // survives the move into the arena.
            Character.Class = ShipClass.Destroyer;
            var agl = Character.LoadoutFor(ShipClass.Destroyer); agl[8] = ""; agl[9] = "";
            agl[4] = "dd_salvo_3";                      // and a dropped rack: a magazine of 6 bursts
            H.Yard.Credits = 12345;                               // its own base, before it visits
            // Level 2 against the level-1 boss: its OWN share is 100 for the kill (the host's is 200)
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 2; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            await Wait(0.8);
            Net.I.Join(HostAt(Port));
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 10 && !(Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1, "arena guest: joined");
            await ToArena("arena guest", 1);
            await GuestSeesTheFight();
            if (Wan) Check(Net.Arriving(1.5) < 1.45 && Net.Arriving(1.5) >= 0.6,
                           $"over the internet, a 1.5 s warning is shown a round trip short, so it ends when this pilot is judged ({Net.Arriving(1.5):0.00} s)");
            int agHold0 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            await RealTime(() => H.MissionWon, 15);
            Check(H.MissionWon && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0,
                  $"arena guest: the boss the host killed reads zero here too ({(GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) ? H.Boss.Hp : -1):0})");
            Check(H.MissionWon && !H.IReturned && H.ReturnTotal == 2 && Nodes<ReturnButton>().Any(),
                  $"arena guest: after the win a RETURN button, no countdown ({H.ReturnReady} of {H.ReturnTotal} ready)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Crates.Count == 2, 5);
            var agCrates = H.Crates.ToList();
            Check(agCrates.Count == 2 && Count<LootCrate>() == 2 && ((string)OnDisk("loot", "unclaimed", "")).Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length == 2,
                  $"arena guest: its own 2 crates, and on its own file at once ({agCrates.Count} crates)");
            Check(Met("loot") && !Met("equipment"), "arena guest: LOOT is met with its first crates");
            FlyOver(agCrates[0].Position);
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(!GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(agCrates[0]) && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == agHold0 + 1 && H.Crates.Count == 1,
                  "arena guest: flying over its own crate takes it -- no host involved");
            Check(Met("equipment"), "arena guest: EQUIPMENT is met with the first part taken");
            FlyOver(agCrates[1].Position + new Vector2(0, 1500));      // and well clear of the other: that one is left behind
            await Wait(6.0);
            Check(Hub.InArena, "arena guest: still in the arena 6 s after the kill -- no clock");
            H.SetMyReturn(true);                                  // done collecting; the host readies once it sees this
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 30);
            // the sector changes at once, the world a frame later: wait for the home world to be built
            await RealTime(() => H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady(), 5);
            Check(!Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline, "arena guest: home with the host after the win");
            Check(Character.Exp == 450, $"arena guest: its OWN EXP for the kill -- 100 at level 2, +250 first clear, +100 (450; exp {Character.Exp})");
            var agFile = new ConfigFile(); agFile.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg");
            int agDisk = agFile.HasSection("gear_hold") ? agFile.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)agFile.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)) : 0;
            Check(agDisk == agHold0 + 2 && (string)agFile.GetValue("id", "name", "") == "Arena Guest",
                  $"arena guest: both parts reached its OWN file's hold -- the one it took, and the one it left ({agDisk - agHold0} of 2; in memory {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() - agHold0}, unclaimed {Character.Unclaimed.Count})");
            // what this guest is shown of the Drake Bastion, watched from before the arena is built
            bool sawDrake = false, sawShell = false, sawRock = false;
            var drakeWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H == null || !Hub.InArena || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss)) return false;
                sawDrake |= H.Boss is Drake { Type.Id: "drake_bastion" };
                sawShell |= Nodes<Slug>().Any(s => s.Cosmetic && s.Look == Slug.Kind.Shell);
                sawRock |= Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && r.Hold <= 7.5 && r.From.DistanceTo(H.Boss.Position) < 260f);
                return sawDrake && sawShell && sawRock;
            } };
            AddChild(drakeWatch);
            await ToArena("arena guest", 2);
            int agOldId = Net.LocalId;
            int agHold1 = Character.GearHold.Values.Sum();
            Me.Position = new Vector2(900, 300); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            // its boss is the host's boss, scaled as the host scaled it (the host sets a party-of-3 x2.2)
            double seenMult = 0;
            await RealTime(() => { if (H?.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss)) seenMult = H.Boss.HullMult; return Math.Abs(seenMult - 2.2) < 1e-9; }, 5);
            Check(Math.Abs(seenMult - 2.2) < 1e-9, $"arena guest: its boss is the host's boss, scaled as the host scaled it (x2.2; x{seenMult:0.00})");
            // A PEER ARRIVING MID-THROW is sent the rock and its lane as they stand (Boss.CatchUp, from
            // the host's catch-up for a peer that reports its world, as a rejoining or slow-loading guest
            // does) -- asked for here by reporting again, a second into the throw's 7.5 s warning by the
            // rock's own clock, well before the host drops this peer
            await RealTime(() => Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && r.Elapsed >= 1.0), 4);
            typeof(Hub).GetMethod("ReportSector", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).Invoke(H, null);
            await RealTime(() => Nodes<ThrownRock>().Any(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && !r.IsQueuedForDeletion() && r.Hold < 6.9), 3);
            var lateRocks = Nodes<ThrownRock>().Where(r => r.Cosmetic && !r.Done && !r.IsQueuedForDeletion()).ToList();
            Check(lateRocks.Count == 1 && lateRocks[0].Hold < 6.9 && Nodes<Telegraph>().Any(t => t.Line && Math.Abs(t.Width - 180f) < 0.1f && t.Duration < 6.9),
                  $"arena guest: a peer arriving mid-throw is sent the rock and its lane as they stand -- one rock, with what was left of its warning ({lateRocks.FirstOrDefault()?.Hold:0.00} s)");
            await RealTime(() => drakeWatch.Seen, 4);
            Check(sawDrake && sawShell && sawRock,
                  $"arena guest: mission 2 is the Drake Bastion here too, and its slow shells and its thrown rock reach this guest (Drake {sawDrake}, shell {sawShell}, rock {sawRock})");
            drakeWatch.QueueFree();
            // DROPPED MID-ARENA: to its own world at once, then back in -- into the arena
            await RealTime(() => !Net.IsOnline, Wan ? 20 : 10);
            await RealTime(() => !Hub.InArena, 2);
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && !Hub.InArena && Net.I.Reconnecting, "arena guest: dropped mid-arena, home to its own world, trying to get back in");
            await RealTime(() => Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1, 12);
            Check(Hub.InArena && Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != agOldId, "arena guest: back in under a new id -- into the arena, where the party is");
            await RealTime(() => Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50, 5);
            Check(Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)) < 50,
                  $"arena guest: put back where it was when it dropped ({Me?.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(900, 300)):0} u off; at {Me?.Position})");
            // the boss died while it was away: it helped win it, so it is paid on its return -- its EXP
            // (the level-2 Drake at pilot level 2: 200, +250 its first clear of level 2, +100 = 550 on its
            // 450: 1000, a level -- level 3 with 0 over) and both its parts (straight to the hold if they
            // reach it at home, else as crates)
            await RealTime(() => Character.Level == 3 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count == agHold1 + 2, 6);
            Check(Character.Level == 3 && Character.Exp == 0 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count == agHold1 + 2,
                  $"arena guest: the kill made while it was away is paid on its return: its EXP and its 2 parts (level {Character.Level}, exp {Character.Exp}; {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() + Character.Unclaimed.Count - agHold1} parts)");
            await RealTime(() => H.MissionWon && H.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0, 5);
            Check(H.MissionWon && H.Boss != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(H.Boss) && H.Boss.Hp == 0, "arena guest: back in an arena already won -- the boss reads zero");
            // THE HOST VANISHES (no goodbye): 3 tries over about 20 s, then RECONNECT
            await RealTime(() => !Net.IsOnline, 20);
            ulong vanished = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            var tries = new List<double>(); bool wasTrying = false; PlayerShip homeShip = null;
            while (!Net.I.CanReconnect && Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished < 25000)
            {
                double since = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished) / 1000.0;
                if (Net.I.Connecting && !wasTrying) tries.Add(since);
                wasTrying = Net.I.Connecting;
                if (homeShip == null && since > 1.0 && H != null && H.Yard != null && H.IsNodeReady()) homeShip = Me;
                await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            }
            double toButton = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - vanished) / 1000.0;
            Check(Net.I.CanReconnect && Net.I.Attempt == 3 && tries.Count == 3 && Math.Abs(tries[0] - 2) < 0.3 && Math.Abs(tries[1] - 8) < 0.3 && Math.Abs(tries[2] - 14) < 0.3
                  && Math.Abs(toButton - 19) < 0.7 && !Net.IsOnline,
                  $"arena guest: a vanished host is tried at 2, 8 and 14 s, 5 s each, then RECONNECT is offered at 19 s ({string.Join(", ", tries.Select(t => t.ToString("0.0")))}; {toButton:0.0} s)");
            Check(homeShip != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(homeShip) && Me == homeShip, "arena guest: the failed tries left its own world and ship alone");
            await Wait(1.0);
            // its own base, plus its shares of two kills with two pilots -- level 1, 2000 x 1.5 / 2 = 1500;
            // level 2, x1.1 = 1650 -- the second made while it was away, but split two ways all the same
            double want = 12345 + 1500 + 1650;
            Check(!Hub.InArena && !Net.IsOnline && H.Yard != null && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: the host gone mid-arena, home offline with its own base and its bounty share ({H.Yard?.Credits:0} of {want:0})");
            // ...AND IT IS ON DISK, in THIS peer's own file. It used to read the host's: every
            // process shares one user:// and only solo made a character, so host and guest wrote
            // the same file and the loser's numbers were simply gone. Each role makes its own now,
            // which is what makes this assertable at all.
            var guestFile = new ConfigFile();
            Check(guestFile.Load($"{Character.Dir}/{Character.Id}.cfg") == Error.Ok
                  && (string)guestFile.GetValue("id", "name", "") == "Arena Guest"
                  && Math.Abs((double)guestFile.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0) - want) < 1e-6,
                  $"arena guest: its own base reached its OWN file ({(string)guestFile.GetValue("id", "name", "?")}, {(double)guestFile.GetValue("base", "credits", -1.0):0} of {want:0})");
            int agDisk2 = guestFile.HasSection("gear_hold") ? guestFile.GetSectionKeys("gear_hold").Sum(k => (int)guestFile.GetValue("gear_hold", k, 0)) : 0;
            Check(agDisk2 == agHold1 + 2 && (int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "level", 0) == 3 && (int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "exp", -1) == 0,
                  $"arena guest: the kill paid on its return is on its own file: its EXP, both parts in the hold ({agDisk2 - agHold1} of 2, exp {(int)guestFile.GetValue("progress", "exp", 0)})");
            // PRESSING RECONNECT: a try (5 s: the host was here a moment ago, or it is gone), and when
            // that fails too, the button again
            var rcSm = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First(); var rcB = Nodes<Button>(rcSm).First(b => b.Name == "Reconnect");
            bool offered = rcB.IsVisibleInTree() && !rcB.Disabled;
            rcB.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.2);
            bool trying = Net.I.Connecting && !rcB.Visible;
            ulong pressedAt = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            await RealTime(() => Net.I.CanReconnect, 9);
            double tryFor = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - pressedAt) / 1000.0 + 0.2;
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(offered && trying && Net.I.CanReconnect && rcB.IsVisibleInTree() && !Net.IsOnline && Math.Abs(tryFor - 5) < 0.6 && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("RECONNECT to try again"),
                  $"arena guest: RECONNECT pressed tries once more, for 5 s, and fails back to the button ({offered}, {trying}, {tryFor:0.0} s; {Net.I.LastStatus})");
        }
    }

    // WHAT A GUEST IS SHOWN. Both of these were host-only state read by drawing code, so on a
    // guest they were simply wrong -- and nothing said so, because the solo run is the host.
    async Task GuestSeesTheFight()
    {
        Boss boss = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 2 && boss == null; i++)
        {   // InArena goes true before the boss node exists on this peer
            boss = H?.Boss;
            await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        }
        Check(boss != null && !Net.Sim, "arena guest: the boss is here, and this peer is not the host");
        if (boss == null) return;

        // THE SUPER-MOVE BAR. _beam and _charge only tick under Net.Sim, so a guest's bar sat at
        // zero for the whole fight. Watched over time rather than sampled once: the failure was a
        // value that never moved, and a single reading cannot tell a stopped clock from a slow one.
        // Sampled only once a figure has actually ARRIVED. Before the first packet a guest's
        // countdown is -1, which reads as 0 s left, and a first sample of 0 makes "it went down"
        // impossible to tell from "it has not started".
        for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && boss.NextSuperIn <= 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
        double firstFill = boss.SuperFill, firstLeft = boss.NextSuperIn;
        Check(firstLeft > 0, $"arena guest: the host's super-move countdown arrives at all ({firstLeft:0.0} s)");
        await Wait(1.5);
        Check(boss.NextSuperIn > 0 && boss.NextSuperIn < firstLeft - 0.5 && boss.SuperFill > firstFill,
              $"arena guest: the boss's super-move bar runs ({firstLeft:0.0} -> {boss.NextSuperIn:0.0} s, fill {firstFill:0.00} -> {boss.SuperFill:0.00})");
    }

    async Task Mp(string role)
    {
        await Wait(0.1);
        if (role == "host")
        {
            Character.Name = "HostPilot"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Battleship; Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity"); Priv(H, "SendIdentity", 0);
            H.GetNode<PlayerShip>("Ship_1").Position = new Vector2(-900, 900);   // out of the way
            int refusals = 0;
            Net.I.Status += s => { if (s.StartsWith("Refused")) refusals++; };
            Net.I.Host(27115);
            // A raider that exists BEFORE either guest joins: they can only learn of it from the
            // catch-up a joining guest is sent (Hub.NetMySector). Far out, and gone at 9 s.
            var early = H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-6000, -6000));
            H.Yard.Credits = 1000;                     // for the guest's purchase request
            Character.Exp = 0; Character.Level = 1; Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.Checking && Net.I.LanAddress.EndsWith(":27115"), "hosting starts at once; the router is asked in the background");
            await Wait(9.0);
            // this harness's network has no router and no internet (NoRouterNoInternet): it must
            // say so, and how to get friends in anyway
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.LanOnly && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("forward UDP 27115") && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Tailscale"),
                  $"no UPnP router: LAN hosting stands, and the status explains the fix");
            Check(Router.IsShared("100.72.1.5") && Router.IsShared("192.168.1.20") && !Router.IsShared("81.2.3.4"),
                  "carrier-grade and private addresses are recognised as unreachable from outside");
            // THE BUILD HANDSHAKE. The save format is versioned (2, as a literal: a check that reads
            // the number it tests cannot catch the number being wrong); a SESSION is matched on the
            // build's fingerprint. The refusal is proved on a real connection by the third player,
            // who first knocks as another build: exactly one refusal here, and no ship for it.
            Check(Game.Version == 2 && Net.Accepts(Net.Protocol) && !Net.Accepts(Net.Protocol ^ 1),
                  $"a session admits this build and refuses any other (save format {Game.Version}, build {Net.Protocol:x8})");
            Check(refusals == 1, $"the host refused the one peer that knocked as another build ({refusals})");
            var ships = Nodes<PlayerShip>().ToList();
            Check(ships.Count == 3, $"host sees 3 ships: itself and two guests ({ships.Count})");
            Check(ships.Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third"), "the second guest's identity reached the host");
            // Each guest is known by its CHARACTER: a returning pilot gets a new peer id, not a new self
            var gInfo = Net.I.Players.Values.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Guesty"); var tInfo = Net.I.Players.Values.FirstOrDefault(p => p.Name == "Third");
            Check(gInfo != null && gInfo.CharacterId.EndsWith("-guest") && tInfo != null && tInfo.CharacterId.EndsWith("-guest2"),
                  $"the host knows each guest's character, not just its peer id ({gInfo?.CharacterId}, {tInfo?.CharacterId})");
            var third = ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third");
            third?.Hit(250, third.Position, "test:reconnect");
            if (GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(early)) H.RaiderDown(early);
            // pinned by the HOST, the way a raider's web does it: the guest must feel it (it checks)
            ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty")?.ApplyStatus(Status.Pinned, 3.0);
            // and a salvager lost, the way a raid takes one: the guest must see its rebuild run
            H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager)?.TakeDamage(1e6);
            // hosting here (no router, no internet): both answers come back empty -> network only
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 9 && Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.Checking; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            var smh = Nodes<SessionMenu>().First(); var rev = Nodes<Button>(smh).First(b => b.Name == "RevealAddress");
            Check(Net.I.Reachability == Net.Reach.LanOnly && !rev.Visible, $"no router or internet here: network only, nothing to reveal ({Net.I.Reachability})");
            Net.I.Reachable(Net.Reach.Manual, "203.0.113.7:27015", "Hosting (test).");
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(rev.Visible && !rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7") && !Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("203.0.113.7"), "the address friends need is hidden until clicked");
            rev.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7:27015"), "click: it shows the public address and port");
            rev.EmitSignal(BaseButton.SignalName.Pressed); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(!rev.Text.Contains("203.0.113.7"), "click again: hidden");
            Character.BossCleared.Clear();
            // every pilot: its own EXP by its own level, and its own drops -- each guest ITS OWN, and only its own
            H.AnnounceBossKill(1, Vector2.Zero);
            // what the host rolled for whom, by character, for each guest to hold its own parts against
            var kills = (System.Collections.IList)typeof(Hub).GetField("_recentKills", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            var rolled = (System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<int, string[]>)kills[kills.Count - 1].GetType().GetField("Drops").GetValue(kills[kills.Count - 1]);
            var dropsFile = new ConfigFile();
            foreach (var kv in rolled) dropsFile.SetValue("drops", kv.Key == Net.LocalId ? Character.Id : Net.I.Players[kv.Key].CharacterId, string.Join(",", kv.Value.OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
            dropsFile.Save("user://smoke_drops.cfg");
            Check(rolled.Count == 3 && rolled.Values.All(d => d.Length == 2), $"the host rolls a kill's drops for each of the 3 pilots, 2 each ({rolled.Count})");
            // ...and the base owner's record is the level-3 boss: AUTO-SELL opens on THIS base for everyone
            Character.BossCleared["silver_lancer"] = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<int> { 1, 2, 3 };
            H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-3500, 3500));   // far off: the guests must see it
            H.SpawnRaider(new Vector2(-3700, 3700), Enemies.Gunship);
            Check(!H.AllReady, "the host cannot warp until every pilot is ready");
            H.SetMyReady(true); Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            for (int i = 0; i < 600 && !H.AllReady; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.AllReady && H.PartyIds.Count() == 3, "both guests' READY reached the host: the party of three is ready");
            Check(Multiplayer.GetPeers().All(id => Hub.PeerSector(id) == Hub.SectorKind.Home),
                  "every guest has told the host it is home (the yard talks only to guests at home)");
            H.StartMission();
            Check(Character.Exp == 550, $"the host gets its own EXP for the kill (level 1: 550; exp {Character.Exp})");
            Check(H.Yard.Level("salvage_rate") == 1 && H.Yard.Credits == 900, "a guest's BUY request was honoured by the host");
            var g = ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty");
            Check(g != null && g.Name == PlayerShip.NodeName(g.OwnerId), "guest ship named by its id");
            Check(g != null && g.Pilot == "Guesty" && g.Class == ShipClass.Carrier && g.Main == new Color(1, 0, 0), $"guest identity reached host ({g?.Pilot}/{g?.Class}/{g?.Main})");
            Check(g != null && g.Loadout[0] == "cv_swarm_3" && g.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 9 && g.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber) == 2,
                  $"the guest's dropped hangar reached the host: its carrier flies 9 fighters and 2 bombers here ({g?.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter)} / {g?.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber)})");
            var dummy = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            Check(g != null && g.WingTarget == dummy, "guest's Space order reached the host");
            Check(dummy.Total > 5, $"guest's wing hit the dummy on the host ({dummy.Total:0.0})");
            Check(g != null && g.PdActive, "guest's PD activation reached the host");
            Check(Nodes<Torpedo>().Any(x => !x.Cosmetic) || dummy.Total > 12, "guest's bomber strike launched real torpedoes on the host");
            // stay up while the guest watches its bombers come home and dock (it checks ~22 s in), and
            // send the hauler on an escort half way through for it to see
            ulong w0 = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            H.Yard.Credits = 100000;                  // enough for AUTO-SELL: only its owner rule can refuse a guest
            GetTree().CreateTimer(3.0).Timeout += () => { if (H?.Yard != null) { H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300; } };
            GetTree().CreateTimer(6.0).Timeout += () =>
            {
                if (H?.Yard == null) return;
                H.Yard.Hauler.ResetToPad(); H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo = 300; H.Yard.RequestDispatch(escorted: true);
            };
            // two seconds into the escort, on to its fourth leg and just short of the south-west outpost:
            // the guest must see it hold there and offload
            GetTree().CreateTimer(8.0).Timeout += () =>
            {
                if (H?.Yard?.Hauler is not { Escorted: true } eh) return;
                typeof(Hauler).GetField("_leg", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(eh, 3);
                eh.Position = Hub.EscortRoute[3] + new Vector2(15, -15);
            };
            await Wait(2.0);
            // A DROP: Third's connection cut by the engine, no goodbye -- what a Wi-Fi drop looks like
            var heldD = (System.Collections.IDictionary)typeof(Hub).GetField("_held", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static).GetValue(null);
            int thirdOld = third?.OwnerId ?? 0;
            Drop(thirdOld);
            await RealTime(() => !Multiplayer.GetPeers().Contains(thirdOld) && !H.Ships.Any(s => s.OwnerId == thirdOld), 3);
            var hEntry = heldD.Values.Cast<object>().FirstOrDefault();
            int owedOnDrop = hEntry == null ? -1 : ((System.Collections.IList)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Owed").GetValue(hEntry)).Count;
            double hullOnDrop = hEntry == null ? -1 : (double)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Hp").GetValue(hEntry);
            Check(owedOnDrop == 1, $"the kill 2 s before the drop is owed to the dropped pilot too: the host may not have reached it ({owedOnDrop})");
            double holdLeft = hEntry == null ? 0 : ((long)(ulong)hEntry.GetType().GetField("Until").GetValue(hEntry) - (long)Time.GetTicksMsec()) / 1000.0;
            Check(H.PartyIds.Count() == 3 && H.PilotName(thirdOld).Contains("Third") && H.PilotName(thirdOld).Contains("reconnecting") && H.IsReady(thirdOld)
                  && holdLeft > 85 && holdLeft <= 90.5,
                  $"a dropped pilot's place is held for 90 s: still in the party of 3, still READY ({H.PilotName(thirdOld)}, {holdLeft:0.0} s left)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Ships.Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld), Wan ? 25 : 12);
            // the hull as it comes back: its place is restored with its identity, and it regenerates from then on
            double hullBack = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld)?.Hp ?? -1;
            await Wait(0.5);
            var third2 = H.Ships.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Third" && s.OwnerId != thirdOld);
            Check(third2 != null && H.IsReady(third2.OwnerId) && !H.PartyIds.Contains(thirdOld) && H.PartyIds.Count() == 3 && hullOnDrop < 300 && Math.Abs(hullBack - hullOnDrop) < 1 && heldD.Count == 0,
                  $"back under a new id: READY and the damaged hull move to it, the place is released ({third2?.OwnerId}, {hullBack:0.0} hull back of {hullOnDrop:0.0} held)");
            // A HOLD THAT LAPSES: a held pilot that was not READY keeps the portal shut until its time is up
            var heldType = typeof(Hub).GetNestedType("Held", BindingFlags.NonPublic);
            object Ghost(int peer, ulong ms)
            {
                var gh = Activator.CreateInstance(heldType);
                heldType.GetField("OldPeer").SetValue(gh, peer); heldType.GetField("Name").SetValue(gh, "Ghost");
                heldType.GetField("Until").SetValue(gh, Time.GetTicksMsec() + ms);
                return gh;
            }
            heldD["ghost"] = Ghost(424242, 300); Priv(H, "RefreshAway");
            bool shut = !H.AllReady;
            await Wait(0.6);
            Check(shut && heldD.Count == 0 && H.AllReady, "a held pilot that was not READY keeps the portal shut -- until its place lapses");
            while (Time.GetTicksMsec() - w0 < 15000) await Wait(0.25);
            // the third player has LEFT, saying goodbye: that is not a drop, and no place is held for it
            Check(heldD.Count == 0 && H.PartyIds.Count() == 2 && !Net.I.Players.Values.Any(p => p.Name == "Third"),
                  $"a pilot that leaves on purpose is gone from the party: no place held ({heldD.Count} held, a party of {H.PartyIds.Count()})");
            heldD["ghost2"] = Ghost(424243, 60000);          // a place still held when the host leaves
            Net.I.GoOffline();   // drop the guest
            await Wait(2.0);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 4 && !Net.I.NetworkIdle; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Net.I.NetworkIdle && heldD.Count == 0, "back offline: no socket, no lookup, no router job left running, and no place still held");
        }
        else if (role == "guest2")
        {   // a third player: joins, sees everyone, leaves with the session
            await Wait(1.6);
            // FIRST, AS ANOTHER BUILD. It must be refused -- and learn why, on its own screen, which
            // the old handshake never managed: its refusal went out in the same breath as the
            // disconnect, and the disconnect threw it away.
            Net.PretendProtocol = Net.Protocol ^ 1;
            Net.I.Join(HostAt(27115));
            await RealTime(() => !Net.I.Connecting, 8);
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("different build"),
                  $"a player on another build is refused, and told why ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            Net.PretendProtocol = null;
            await Wait(0.3);
            Net.I.Join(HostAt(27115));
            await Wait(1.2);
            Character.Name = "Third"; Character.Class = ShipClass.Battleship;
            Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity"); Priv(H, "SendIdentity", 0);
            await Wait(3.0);
            // A GUEST'S BROADSIDE, inside the 6 s this guest waits after joining (so everything the host
            // times against it -- the drop above all -- happens when it always did). F is a request: this
            // pilot never starts its own -- the host does, and
            // the wind-up, the volleys and the cooldown come back in the host's report, with cosmetic
            // copies of the shells. Checked one frame after the press: a guest that started its own
            // would already be winding up; the host's word cannot have come back yet.
            {
                var me3 = Me; double bsClock0 = me3.Clock;
                AimWorld(me3.Position + new Vector2(300, -300)); await Wait(0.1);
                Key(global::Godot.Key.F); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                bool ownStart = me3.BroadsideTracking || me3.BroadsideCooldownLeft > 0;
                bool sawWindup = false, sawShells = false; double cdBack = 0;
                for (ulong t0 = Time.GetTicksMsec(); Time.GetTicksMsec() - t0 < 4000 && cdBack <= 0; )
                {
                    sawWindup |= me3.BroadsideWindupLeft > 0 || me3.BroadsideVolleysLeft > 0;
                    sawShells |= Nodes<Shell>().Any(s => s.Cosmetic);
                    cdBack = me3.BroadsideCooldownLeft;
                    await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
                }
                Check(!ownStart && sawWindup && sawShells && cdBack > 13 && cdBack <= 14,
                      $"third player: its broadside is the host's -- not started here ({ownStart}), then the wind-up or volleys, the shells and the 14 s cooldown reach it ({sawWindup}, {sawShells}, {cdBack:0.0} s)");
                await Wait(Math.Max(0.1, 3.0 - (me3.Clock - bsClock0)));   // the rest of the 6 s, in game seconds
            }

            H.SetMyReady(true);                           // READY for the bounty
            Nodes<PlayerShip>().First(x => x.Mine).AutopilotTo = null;   // stay put: the scene must not change mid-test
            var all = Nodes<PlayerShip>().ToList();
            Check(all.Count == 3 && all.Any(s => s.Pilot == "HostPilot") && all.Any(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty"),
                  $"third player sees the host and the other guest ({string.Join(", ", all.Select(s => s.Pilot))})");
            Check(all.Count(s => s.Mine) == 1 && all.First(s => s.Mine).OwnerId == Net.LocalId, "third player flies its own ship");
            await RealTime(() => Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() >= 2, 3); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == 2 && HoldIds() == RolledFor(Character.Id),
                  $"third player: its own 2 parts from the kill, the very ones the host rolled for it ({HoldIds()} / {RolledFor(Character.Id)})");
            int exp3 = Character.Exp;
            var guesty = all.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Pilot == "Guesty");
            Check(guesty != null && guesty.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter) == 9 && guesty.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber) == 2,
                  $"...and the other guest's carrier with ITS gear: 9 fighters, 2 bombers ({guesty?.WingCount(WingKind.Fighter)} / {guesty?.WingCount(WingKind.Bomber)})");
            // DROPPED by the host (no goodbye): off to its own world, then back in, and back where it was
            Me.Position = new Vector2(2400, -1200); Me.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; Me.AutopilotTo = null;
            await Wait(0.5);
            int oldId = Net.LocalId; double hp3 = Me.Hp;
            // its hull up to the moment of the drop (it regenerates: the host holds the last figure)
            for (ulong t0 = Time.GetTicksMsec(); Net.IsOnline && Time.GetTicksMsec() - t0 < 20000; )
            { if (Me != null && Me.OwnerId == oldId) hp3 = Me.Hp; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            ulong tDrop = Time.GetTicksMsec();
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && Net.I.Reconnecting && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Lost the connection"),
                  $"third player: a drop with no goodbye is not the end -- offline, and trying to get back in ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            await RealTime(() => Net.I.Connecting, 5);
            double firstTry = (Time.GetTicksMsec() - tDrop) / 1000.0;
            await RealTime(() => Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1, 10);
            Check(Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId != 1 && Net.LocalId != oldId && firstTry >= 1.8 && firstTry <= 2.5,
                  $"third player: back in under a new id, the first try 2 s after the drop ({firstTry:0.0} s)");
            await RealTime(() => Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(2400, -1200)) < 50, 5);
            // its hull as the host restored it: the lowest it reads once back (a fresh ship reads full
            // until the host's figure arrives, and from then on it regenerates)
            double hpBack = Me?.Hp ?? -1;
            for (ulong t0 = Time.GetTicksMsec(); Time.GetTicksMsec() - t0 < 500; ) { if (Me != null) hpBack = Math.Min(hpBack, Me.Hp); await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(Me != null && Me.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(2400, -1200)) < 50 && hp3 < 300 && Math.Abs(hpBack - hp3) < 2 && H.IsReady(Net.LocalId) && Nodes<PlayerShip>().Count() == 3,
                  $"third player: back where it was, with its damaged hull and its READY ({Me?.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(2400, -1200)):0} u off, {hpBack:0.0} of {hp3:0.0} hull)");
            // the kill it was owed came again with the rest -- and is not paid twice
            Check(Character.Exp == exp3 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == 2 && Character.Unclaimed.Count == 0,
                  $"third player: a kill it already had is not paid again on its return (exp {exp3} -> {Character.Exp}, {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum()} parts)");
            // ...nor after a restart: the serials paid are on its file (a restart is a load from it)
            long lastPaid = Character.PaidKills.LastOrDefault();
            Character.Load(Character.Id);
            Priv(H, "NetKill", 1, 3, lastPaid, new[] { "fr_flak_1", "fr_flak_2" }, Vector2.Zero);
            Check(lastPaid != 0 && Character.PaidKills.Contains(lastPaid) && Character.Exp == exp3 && Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == 2,
                  $"third player: nor after a restart -- the kills it was paid for are on its file (exp {exp3} -> {Character.Exp}, {Character.GearHold.Values.Sum()} parts)");
            // IT LEAVES, on purpose: a goodbye, so nothing to retry -- and the host keeps no place for it
            Net.I.GoOffline();
            await Wait(1.0);
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && !Net.I.Reconnecting && !Net.I.CanReconnect && !Net.I.Connecting,
                  $"third player: leaving on purpose is not a drop -- nothing is retried ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
        }
        else
        {
            H.Yard.Ore = 5000;       // "own" hub total, should come back after leaving
            // a NON-default loadout (two chips off): a host that ignored the guest's gear would fall
            // back to the default (five chips) and replicate 250 hull instead of 230
            // The CLASS FIRST: gear is per class, and this used to edit whichever class the
            // character happened to already be -- inherited from whoever wrote the shared file
            // last. With a fresh character per role it is a Battleship here, so the two chips
            // came off a hull this pilot never flies and the carrier still had five.
            Character.Class = ShipClass.Carrier;
            var gl = Character.LoadoutFor(Character.Class); gl[8] = ""; gl[9] = "";
            gl[0] = "cv_swarm_3";                       // a boss drop: nine fighters, which only this pilot knows it has
            await Wait(0.8);
            // Everything this pilot holds as it leaves for the host's world -- the ore above, AND the
            // 7777 credits every role's base now starts with. This check used to assume the ore was
            // all of it, and failed from the day each role got a base of its own.
            double Held() => H.Yard.Ore + H.Yard.Salvage + H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo + H.Yard.Gatherers.Sum(x => x.Cargo) + H.Yard.Credits;
            double held0 = Held();
            Net.I.Join(HostAt(27115));
            await Wait(1.2);
            Character.Name = "Guesty"; Character.Main = new Color(1, 0, 0); Character.Class = ShipClass.Carrier;
            Priv(H, "ApplyLocalIdentity"); Priv(H, "SendIdentity", 0);
            var mine = Nodes<PlayerShip>().FirstOrDefault(s => s.Mine);
            Check(mine != null && mine.OwnerId == Net.LocalId && Net.LocalId != 1, $"guest owns its own ship (id {Net.LocalId})");
            Check(Nodes<PlayerShip>().Count() >= 2, $"guest sees the host ({Nodes<PlayerShip>().Count()} ships)");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !H.Raiders.Any(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(-6000, -6000)) < 2500); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.Raiders.Any(r => r.Position.DistanceTo(new Vector2(-6000, -6000)) < 2500),
                  "a raider that existed before this guest joined reached it (the catch-up a joining guest is sent)");
            // Watched at the END of every frame: the ship overwrote the host's pinned flag in its own
            // frame, so a check made at any earlier point would have seen the flag and passed.
            var pinWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () => mine != null && GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(mine) && mine.Pinned };
            var dispatchWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () => H?.Yard?.Hauler is { CanDispatch: true } };
            // the host's escort holding at an outpost, watched every frame from here on: when it comes
            // is the host's timetable, not this guest's
            int stopLeg = -1; double stopMax = 0, shownMin = 9, shownMax = -1;
            var stopWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (H?.Yard?.Hauler is not { Escorted: true, State: Hauler.St.Escorting } sh || sh.StopLeft <= 0 || sh.Cargo <= 0) return false;
                stopLeg = sh.Leg; stopMax = Math.Max(stopMax, sh.StopLeft);
                shownMin = Math.Min(shownMin, sh.ShownCargo / sh.Cargo); shownMax = Math.Max(shownMax, sh.ShownCargo / sh.Cargo);
                return true;
            } };
            AddChild(stopWatch);
            AddChild(dispatchWatch);
            var heldWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () => H != null && H.PartyIds.Any(i => H.PilotName(i).Contains("Third") && H.PilotName(i).Contains("reconnecting")) };
            AddChild(heldWatch);
            AddChild(pinWatch);
            H.Yard.BuyUpgrade("salvage_rate");            // a request: the host decides
            H.SetMyReady(true);                           // READY for the bounty
            Nodes<PlayerShip>().First(x => x.Mine).AutopilotTo = null;   // stay put: the scene must not change mid-test
            var dummy = H.GetNode<TargetDummy>("TargetDummy1");
            if (mine != null) { mine.Position = dummy.Position + new Vector2(-300, 300); mine.Velocity = Vector2.Zero; }
            await Wait(1.5);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Tab); await Wait(0.05);
            Check(H.Selected == dummy, "guest: Tab selects the nearest dummy");
            // its own bombers on its deck, watched every frame: each lift-off and landing placed HERE, in
            // its carrier's frame on its own deck clock -- exactly where that clock puts it (1.6 s, the
            // first quarter rolling out to the runway, then up it to 115.5 u ahead; landing in 1 s onto
            // the centre) -- not trailing the host's reports; on the runway and on the centre at deck size
            float gLift = 9f, gLand = 9f, gOff = 0f; bool gBow = false;
            var fDeckT = typeof(Wing).GetField("_deckT", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            var fTouch = typeof(Wing).GetField("_touch", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
            var deckWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () =>
            {
                if (mine == null || !GodotObject.IsInstanceValid(mine)) return false;
                foreach (var w in Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == mine && w.IsBomber))
                {
                    var lp = mine.ToLocal(w.Position); var sp = Nodes<Sprite2D>(w).First();
                    float drawn = sp.Scale.Y * sp.Texture.GetHeight() / 28.125f;
                    double dt = (double)fDeckT.GetValue(w); var bay = mine.Bay(w);
                    if (w.BomberPhase == "Lifting")
                    {
                        float k = (float)(dt / 1.6), u = Mathf.Clamp((k - 0.25f) / 0.75f, 0f, 1f);
                        var want = k < 0.25f ? bay.Lerp(new Vector2(0f, bay.Y), Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, k / 0.25f))
                                             : new Vector2(0f, bay.Y).Lerp(new Vector2(0f, -110.06f), u * u);
                        gOff = Mathf.Max(gOff, lp.DistanceTo(want));
                        if (Mathf.Abs(lp.X) < 0.01f) gLift = Mathf.Min(gLift, drawn);
                        if (Mathf.Abs(lp.X) < 0.01f && lp.Y < -40f) gBow = true;
                    }
                    if (w.BomberPhase == "Landing")
                    {
                        var want = ((Vector2)fTouch.GetValue(w)).Lerp(Vector2.Zero, Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, (float)(dt / 1.0)));
                        gOff = Mathf.Max(gOff, lp.DistanceTo(want));
                        if (lp.Length() < 0.5f) gLand = Mathf.Min(gLand, drawn);
                    }
                }
                return true;
            } };
            AddChild(deckWatch);
            // the hits the host resolves show here as damage numbers too, from the figures it sends
            var numberWatch = new LateSampler { Probe = () => DamageNumbers.Live?.Figures.Any(f => !f.Taken && f.Who == dummy.GetInstanceId()) == true };
            AddChild(numberWatch);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Space);          // fighters attack
            Key(global::Godot.Key.F);              // bomber strike
            await Wait(1.0);
            Key(global::Godot.Key.Q);              // point defence
            // torpedoes live a couple of seconds; watch for one across the window
            bool sawTorp = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < 240; i++) { if (Nodes<Torpedo>().Any(x => x.Cosmetic)) sawTorp = true; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(mine != null && mine.PdActive, "guest sees its PD window running (replicated)");
            Check(sawTorp, "guest sees cosmetic copies of its torpedoes");
            var host = Nodes<PlayerShip>().FirstOrDefault(s => s.OwnerId == 1);
            await RealTime(() => Nodes<PlayerShip>().Count() == 3 && Nodes<PlayerShip>().Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third"), 8);
            Check(Nodes<PlayerShip>().Count() == 3 && Nodes<PlayerShip>().Any(s => s.Pilot == "Third"), "guest sees the other guest too (3 ships)");
            Check(H.Yard.Level("salvage_rate") == 1, "the upgrade the host granted reached the guest");
            for (int i = 0; i < 480 && Character.Exp != 550; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(Character.Exp == 550, $"the host's kill reached this guest, which took its own 550 (exp {Character.Exp})");
            await RealTime(() => Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() >= 2, 3); await Wait(0.5);
            Check(Character.GearHold.Values.Sum() == 2 && HoldIds() == RolledFor(Character.Id),
                  $"a kill's drops with two guests in the session: this guest gets its own 2 parts, the very ones the host rolled for it ({HoldIds()} / {RolledFor(Character.Id)})");
            Check(Met("equipment") && !Met("loot"), "a kill's parts reaching a guest at home: EQUIPMENT is met (they are in the hold), not LOOT (there are no crates)");
            await RealTime(() => H.Yard.OwnerBoss == 3, 3);
            Check(H.Yard.OwnerBoss == 3 && Missions.HighestBeaten == 1,
                  $"AUTO-SELL here is judged by the base owner's boss record (3), not this guest's (1) ({H.Yard.OwnerBoss}, {Missions.HighestBeaten})");
            var ghud = Nodes<HaulerHud>().FirstOrDefault();
            Check(ghud != null && Nodes<Button>(ghud).Any(b => b.Name == "Escort") && Nodes<Button>(ghud).Any(b => b.Name == "Dispatch") && Nodes<Button>(ghud).All(b => !b.Visible),
                  "a guest sees the host's hauler load, but DISPATCH and ESCORT are the base owner's alone");
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && H.Raiders.Count == 0; i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            for (int i = 0; i < 60 * 3 && !H.Raiders.Any(r => r.Heavy); i++) await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame);
            Check(H.Raiders.Count >= 2 && Combat.Hostiles.OfType<Raider>().Any() && H.Raiders.Any(r => r.Heavy),
                  "the host's raiders reached this guest, a heavy as a heavy (hostiles it can target)");
            await Wait(0.1);
            Check(Met("raid"), "RAIDERS: a guest meets a raid from the raiders it is sent (no host involved)");
            var gm = H.Yard.Gatherers.First(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Miner);
            Check(H.Yard.Gatherers.Any(x => x.Position.DistanceTo(Hub.BasePos) > 400) && H.Yard.Hauler.Position.Y == Hub.LaneY,
                  "guest sees the host's miner out working and the hauler on its lane");
            Check(host != null && host.Pilot == "HostPilot", $"host identity reached guest ({host?.Pilot})");
            Check(H.Yard.Ore < 1000, $"guest shows host's totals ({H.Yard.Ore:0})");
            Check(dummy.Total > 0 && dummy.LastSecond > 0, $"dummy readout reached the guest ({dummy.LastSecond:0.00} / {dummy.Total:0.0})");
            int near = Nodes<Wing>().Count(w => w.Carrier == mine && w.Kind == WingKind.Fighter && w.Position.DistanceTo(dummy.Position) < 400);
            Check(near >= 3, $"guest sees its fighters at the target, as the host flies them ({near})");
            // a flash lives 0.1 s, so watch for one over two seconds rather than sampling once
            var flashes = (System.Collections.IList)H.GetType().GetField("_flashes", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(H);
            int seen = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) { if (flashes.Count > 0) seen++; await ToSignal(GetTree(), SceneTree.SignalName.ProcessFrame); }
            Check(seen > 0, $"guest sees the host's hit flashes ({seen} of 60 frames)");
            Check(mine != null && Math.Abs(mine.MaxHp - 230) < 1e-6 && Math.Abs(mine.Hp - 230) < 1e-6,
                  $"hull replicated from the host, which applied this pilot's own gear (three chips: 200 x 1.15 = 230; {mine?.Hp:0})");
            await Wait(12.0);                      // strike done, bombers home and docked
            Check(pinWatch.Seen, "a guest's own ship is pinned when the host pins it (a raider's web works on guests too)");
            var lostSv = H.Yard.Gatherers.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Kind == GatherKind.Salvager);
            Check(lostSv != null && lostSv.Lost && lostSv.RebuildIn > 5 && lostSv.RebuildIn < 29,
                  $"a salvager the host lost is lost here too, its 30 s rebuild counting down -- not stuck at 0 s ({lostSv?.RebuildIn:0.0} s)");
            var gb = Nodes<Wing>().Where(w => w.Carrier == mine && w.IsBomber).ToArray();
            Check(gb.Length == 2 && gb.All(w => w.Docked && w.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(mine.ToGlobal(mine.Bay(w))) < 0.5f),
                  "guest sees its bombers parked in their bays on the carrier's deck (the state from the host, placed in its carrier's frame)");
            Check(gLift < 0.67f && gBow && gLand < 0.67f && gOff < 0.05f,
                  $"guest places its own bombers' deck moves on its own clock, in its carrier's frame: on the runway at deck size, up to the bow, down on the centre at deck size ({gLift:0.00}, {gLand:0.00}; {gOff:0.000} u from the deck clock's pose)");
            deckWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(numberWatch.Seen, "guest sees damage numbers where the hits the host resolves land (from the dummy's replicated figures)");
            numberWatch.QueueFree();
            Check(H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") != null && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen,
                  "the host's mission portal opened here too (the party was ready)");
            // AUTO-SELL is the base owner's to buy: this guest's request changes nothing
            double hostCredits = H.Yard.Credits;
            H.Yard.BuyUpgrade("hauler_autosell"); await Wait(1.5);
            Check(H.Yard.Level("hauler_autosell") == 0 && hostCredits >= 4462 && Math.Abs(H.Yard.Credits - hostCredits) < 1e-6,
                  $"a guest cannot buy AUTO-SELL on the host's base: it is the owner's (level {H.Yard.Level("hauler_autosell")}, {hostCredits:0} -> {H.Yard.Credits:0} cr)");
            // the host put its hauler at the south-west outpost (the last quarter aboard): this guest saw
            // it hold there and the pods drain -- never more than that quarter shown
            await RealTime(() => stopWatch.Seen && shownMin < 0.15, 12);
            Check(stopWatch.Seen && stopLeg == 3 && stopMax > 3 && stopMax <= 4 && shownMin < 0.15 && shownMax <= 0.26
                  && Nodes<Sprite2D>().Count(s => s.Name.ToString().StartsWith("Outpost")) == 4,
                  $"a guest sees the host's hauler hold at the south-west outpost and offload -- its outposts built here too (leg {stopLeg}, {stopMax:0.0} s, shown {shownMax:0.00} -> {shownMin:0.00} of the load)");
            Check(dispatchWatch.Seen && !Met("hauler"), "the hauler hint is the base owner's: a guest watching the host's loaded hauler is not shown buttons it cannot press");
            bool portalWasOpen = H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.PortalOpen;
            await Wait(6.0);                       // the host closes the session ~24.5 s in
            Check(!Net.IsOnline && Net.LocalId == 1 && Net.I.LastStatus.Contains("Host closed") && !Net.I.Reconnecting,
                  $"guest fell back offline when host left -- told it was closed, so nothing is retried ({Net.I.LastStatus})");
            Check(heldWatch.Seen, "while the third player was away, this guest's party list showed it as reconnecting");
            Check(portalWasOpen && H.Mission == Hub.MissionState.Idle && H.GetNodeOrNull("MissionPortal") == null && H.PartyIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { 1 }),
                  $"the host gone, its open portal and its party went with it: this pilot's own world has neither ({H.Mission}; party {string.Join(",", H.PartyIds)})");
            // Back to its OWN world, with nothing lost: everything held -- stock, the
            // ships' cargo, and credits from a sale -- is at least the 5000 it had, and
            // nowhere near the host's few hundred.
            double own = Held();
            double share = Missions.BountyEach(1, 3);                // the host's announced kill, a party of three
            Check(own - share >= held0 - 1e-6 && own - share < held0 + 400,
                  $"guest's own totals restored, nothing lost, plus its bounty share ({own:0.00} held, {share:0.00} of it the share, {held0:0.00} on leaving)");
            Check(H.Yard.Level("salvage_rate") == 0, "guest's own upgrade levels restored (the host's level 1 stays in the host's world)");
            Check(H.Yard.Hauler.Cargo <= H.Yard.Capacity && H.Yard.Gatherers.All(x => x.Cargo == 0 || x.State == Gatherer.St.Working),
                  "economy ships restart docked and empty in your own world");
            var ships = Nodes<PlayerShip>().ToList();
            Check(ships.Count == 1 && ships[0].Mine && ships[0].Name == "Ship_1", "guest rebuilt to its own single ship");
        }
    }
}

// Samples at the END of a frame, after every other node's _Process -- the one place a value that
// something overwrites every frame can be seen as it really stands.
public partial class LateSampler : Node
{
    public System.Func<bool> Probe;
    public bool Seen;
    public override void _Ready() => ProcessPriority = int.MaxValue;
    public override void _Process(double delta) { if (Probe?.Invoke() == true) Seen = true; }
}

// A fighter-sized stand-in, only for the PD priority check.
// A small craft, for the point-defence priority: it says what it IS (Tag.Light), which is what
// the rule reads now -- it used to be inferred from a hit radius under 20 u.
public class TinyTarget : IHittable, ITagged
{
    public int NetId => 0;
    public Vector2 Position => Vector2.Zero;
    public float HitRadius => 8f;
    public bool Alive => true;
    public Tag Tags => Tag.Light;
    public void TakeDamage(double d) { }
}
